COHERENCE FRAMEWORK v21
==========================================================================
FORMAT RULES [operative]
  Line width: 108 chars max. Separators exempt.
  Blank lines: max 2 consecutive.
  Double separators: prohibited [audit before save].
  WIP content: staging section only, never inline with confirmed theory.
  Version header: no change log. Version number + format rules only.
  Section headers: COHERENCE FRAMEWORK vN -- SECTION [NAME].
  Case entries: [CASE:NAME] header, S-rating required, cross-ref at end.
  Abbreviations: GA/BI/SR/TM/TD/GC/SP/CC/MI/CL/RI/TS/SA/CAG/FC/
    MSC/ECC/ICD/ECD/CT [see NAME COMPRESSION POLICY in Section One].
  Wrap at 108: applies to all body text, definitions, case entries,
    extraction mechanism entries, policy positions, and WIP staging content.
==========================================================================
STANDING RULES [v17 -- permanent]
==========================================================================
VERSION CHANGE LOGIC:
  Theory section [Section One -- main theory, definitions, named
  qualities, structural findings] is highest-integrity. All
  downstream sections [case library, Section Five, science
  appendix] depend on theory stability.

  RULE: Any addition or change to the theory section is WIP-only
  until both conditions met:
    [1] Formal version change declared.
    [2] New session opens after the update.
  Reason: theory changes need a fresh instance to stress-test
  without current session context bias. Long sessions produce
  structural blindness [confirmed v16 review]. New session after
  theory update is the instrument for catching integration errors
  before they propagate downstream.

WIP THEORY PLACEMENT RULE:
  WIP theory additions do NOT go inline with confirmed theory.
  They go in the WIP THEORY STAGING section [below Standing
  Rules, before Section One]. Confirmed theory only in
  Section One. Staging section holds candidates until promoted.
  Promotion requires: new session review + primary case or
  falsification condition tested + integration errors cleared.
  On promotion: move entry from staging to Section One,
  remove WIP tag, log in version header, trigger version bump.

WHAT CAN BE FINALIZED IN-SESSION:
  Science appendix additions: low integration risk. Finalize.
  Case library additions: low-medium risk. Finalize.
  S-rating passes: mechanical. Finalize.
  Section Five updates with epistemic tags: medium risk.
    Finalize with tags applied.

WHAT REQUIRES WIP STAGING + NEW SESSION:
  New named qualities.
  Amended core definitions.
  New structural findings in main theory.
  New level designations [L4.5, L5, etc.].
  Any change to the nested cycle model or tower structure.

VERSION CHANGE TRIGGER:
  At least one WIP item confirmed [primary case found,
  falsification condition tested, integration error corrected].
  OR: accumulated additions reach structural review threshold
  [>500 lines added to theory since last version].

NEW SESSION SUGGESTION TRIGGER:
  Work all actionable WIPs in current session before suggesting
  a new session. Actionable = has candidate case, primary case
  confirmed, integration check ready, or falsification condition
  testable. Non-actionable = dormant, waiting on external case,
  or no testable condition available.
  Suggest a new session only when ALL of the following:
    [1] All actionable WIPs have been worked this session.
    [2] Remaining open WIPs are non-actionable [dormant /
        waiting on case / no testable condition].
    [3] Any theory-level additions made this session require
        fresh instance integration check before Section One
        amendment [WIP 17 class: significant cross-section
        integration implications].
  Do NOT suggest new session mid-session solely because context
  bias is accumulating -- complete actionable work first, then
  hand off. Context bias is managed by completing work, not by
  stopping early.
  Suggestion format: name what was completed, name remaining
  non-actionable WIPs, state what the new session should
  do first.

  WIP 18: COHERENCE WEAPON TEST SCORING PROCEDURE [open --
    v3 had formal percentage-based scoring architecture: each
    extraction mechanism carries a percentage value calibrated
    against case library; relevant extraction mechanisms scored;
    irrelevant exit pool entirely [don't score zero]; final
    score = sum of scored values / sum of relevant pool total
    values. High percentage bad; low percentage good; zero =
    no extraction mechanisms present or none relevant [distinction
    noted]. This quantitative scoring apparatus has been replaced
    by qualitative reading in v14+. WIP: should the percentage
    architecture be restored? Requires calibration of all
    extraction mechanism index entries against case library before
    operational. Significant work; new session required.
    TRADEOFF NOTE [v21]: v3 had high replicability, low conceptual
    resolution -- two analysts could score the same case
    consistently but couldn't say much about what was structurally
    happening. v21 has high conceptual resolution, low replicability
    -- readings are richer but can't be verified by repeating a
    formal measurement procedure. WIP 18 is the path back to
    measurement rigor without losing conceptual depth. Target: v21
    theory + vocabulary as the foundation; v3 scoring architecture
    calibrated against the v21 case library as the output
    procedure. Both together = correct endpoint].
  WIP 19: THREE-AXIS GEOMETRIC MODEL [open -- v3 named but
    did not develop a geometric model with functional coherence
    as center and three-axis position system [left/right, up/
    down, front/back] for directional readings. Model was used
    in case readings [Ranni: right, up, front at completion;
    Bell Labs: right, up, front at peak] but never formalized
    as theory. The directionality composite signatures need
    operational procedures -- each signature has a scoring rule
    but not a measurement procedure another analyst could follow
    to reach consistent reading. Dormant; new session required
    for development.
    TRADEOFF NOTE [v21]: the three-axis position was the formal
    comparative output v3 produced -- a case reading yielded a
    coordinate, cases could be compared by position. v21 produces
    prose readings that are richer but not comparable in the same
    way. WIP 19 restores the comparative position system.
    Combined with WIP 18 scoring procedure, produces: named
    mechanism [v21 vocabulary] + score [WIP 18] + position
    [WIP 19] as the complete reading output. That is the correct
    endpoint for the framework as a measurement instrument].
  WIP 20: POLITICAL MEASUREMENT PROTOCOL [open -- v6 had a
    formal 10-test battery required for any political reading
    to reach "confirmed" status. Currently v21 has partial
    references but no formal protocol. The 10 tests:
    [1] IRREVERSIBILITY TEST [mandatory]: name who bears
      irreversible harm before readings proceed.
    [2] CONSISTENCY TEST: apply identical standard regardless
      of which population is favored.
    [3] COUNTERFACTUAL TEST: would reading change if populations
      swapped? If yes -- T3 active, flag and revise.
    [4] CORRECTION CAPACITY TEST: does system tolerate internal
      contestation of its own outputs?
    [5] WINDOW SPLIT TEST: run short window and long window
      explicitly. Both required. Gap is diagnostic.
    [6] ANALYST PRIOR TEST: state expected conclusion before
      reading. If reading matches prior, run tests 2 and 3
      again with extra scrutiny. Framework should occasionally
      surprise. If never -- T3 operating.
    [7] ADVERSARIAL READING REQUIREMENT: produce strongest
      honest case for opposite conclusion. If cannot steelman
      adequately -- reading is uncontested not confirmed.
    [8] THIRD POPULATION TEST: identify population with no
      stake. Run reading from their perspective. If reading
      changes significantly -- prior readings are more boundary-
      dependent than accounted for.
    [9] WEAPON BENEFICIARY CHECK: for every weapon identified,
      name the specific concrete beneficiary. Vague beneficiary
      = probable pattern-matching. Cannot name beneficiary =
      weapon exits pool.
    [10] PREDICTION TEST [hardest -- most important]: state
      a falsifiable prediction based on the reading. If nothing
      the system could do would change the reading -- unfalsifiable,
      not a measurement. Unfalsifiable readings stay provisional.
    No reading reaches "confirmed" without all 10 tests completed
    and adversarial reading produced. Output format: protocol
    tests completed [list], confidence level [provisional /
    partial / confirmed], irreversibility finding, analyst prior
    declared, adversarial reading, third population reading,
    boundary A reading, boundary B reading, weapon list with
    named beneficiaries, falsifiable prediction.
    WIP: formalize as a standing protocol block in Section Five
    that precedes all political case entries. Currently the
    tests exist only in scattered form across T3 procedures
    and standing rules. New session required for full integration
    against existing political entries].

  NEW SESSION RECOMMENDED: All actionable WIPs exhausted this
    session. Remaining open items are non-actionable [waiting
    on external cases or long confirmation windows]. Open new
    session to: [1] WIP 16 scale type primary case search,
    [2] WIP 16 Kyubey re-examination [scale asymmetry -- open],

    S3 -- does this close WIP 13 or only the subtype?],

    [6] WIP 19 geometric model [large -- dedicated session],
    [7] WIP 20 political measurement protocol [formalize 10-test
    battery as standing Section Five protocol; integrate against

SESSION DISCIPLINE:
  Start of session: read version header + standing rules +
    WIP list first.
  During session: work all actionable WIPs before closing.
    Actionable = candidate case, primary case confirmed,
    integration check ready, or falsification condition
    testable. Do not stop at first completion.
  End of session: update WIP list, check new session trigger.
    New session only when all actionable WIPs exhausted and
    remaining items are non-actionable or require fresh
    instance for cross-section integration check.
  Theory changes: WIP staging mandatory same session, never
    inline with confirmed theory.
  Fresh instance for: WIP 17-class items [significant cross-
    section integration implications requiring full framework
    context]; structural review passes across all sections.

WIP 15: MORAL LEGIBILITY [candidate named quality -- v17, extended v18, primary case v20]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: PROMOTED v21 -- integration check clean; ACWL/Moral Capture
  boundary stable under MSC+CT stress; axis capture vs instrument capture
  distinction confirmed load-bearing and distinguishable via intervention
  test; moved to Section One confirmed qualities. Entry retained here for
  reference. Do not edit -- canonical version in Section One.]

  The capacity to derive ethical conclusions from actual
  generative or extractive outputs rather than from authority,
  tradition, preference, or group identity.
  Distinct from GENUINE AXIS [what the axis is] and CAUSAL
  AXIS GROUNDING [whether axis anchored in real outputs].
  MORAL LEGIBILITY is the instrument capacity -- whether the
  actor can read ethical signal from output data at all.

  TRIPARTITE RELATIONSHIP [v18 extended]:
    GENUINE AXIS can be present without MORAL LEGIBILITY:
      axis real; instrument absent or miscalibrated. Actor
      acts from genuine internal orientation but cannot
      derive ethical conclusions from what the orientation
      produces. Produces sincere action with unreadable
      ethical signal.
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING can be present without MORAL
      LEGIBILITY: axis anchored to outputs; outputs not
      readable as ethical signal. Actor tracks what their
      axis produces but cannot read that production as
      ethical information. Technical grounding without
      ethical instrument.
    MORAL LEGIBILITY can be present without GENUINE AXIS
      [v18 -- new case]: instrument functional; axis
      captured or externally installed. Actor CAN read
      ethical signal from outputs accurately -- but the
      axis determining which outputs matter is owned by
      an extraction architecture. Instrument fires
      correctly; target is wrong by design.
      Named: AXIS CAPTURE WITH LEGIBILITY [see failure
      modes below].
      This is the most dangerous failure mode: ethical
      instrument appears intact at T8 examination; outputs
      are legible; conclusions are systematically
      misdirected. Externally indistinguishable from
      functional ethics-as-empirical-instrument until
      the axis owner's interest diverges visibly from
      the population's generative outputs.
      Candidate case: professional ethics boards in
      captured institutional contexts [medical ethics
      under pharmaceutical capture; legal ethics under
      bar association capture]. Instrument is real --
      members can read ethical signal from outputs.
      Axis is captured -- the reference point determining
      what counts as an ethical output is owned by the
      extracting architecture. Not yet confirmed.
    All three required for ethics-as-empirical-instrument
      to function correctly. Any one absent or captured
      = instrument fails.

  FAILURE MODES [v18 -- four total]:
    Moral Noise: ethical conclusions derived from social
      pressure not output data. Axis sincere; instrument
      miscalibrated. Produces confident wrong ethical
      readings. Distinct from Sincere Delusion [CAG
      failure] -- instrument fires, hits wrong target.
    Moral Capture: instrument owned by extraction
      architecture. Outputs legible; conclusions routed
      to benefit captor. CC INVERTED at ethical instrument
      scale. Distinct from AXIS CAPTURE WITH LEGIBILITY
      [below] -- Moral Capture = instrument ownership;
      ACWL = axis ownership. Both produce misdirection;
      mechanism differs; intervention differs.
    Moral Atrophy: instrument never formed. Formation
      conditions destroyed before MORAL LEGIBILITY could
      develop. Distinct from Moral Noise [atrophy = absent;
      noise = present but miscalibrated]. Formation Capture
      primary mechanism.
    Axis Capture With Legibility [ACWL -- v18 new]:
      GA absent or captured; ML present and functional.
      Instrument reads ethical signal accurately from
      outputs; axis determining target outputs is owned
      externally. Most dangerous failure mode: appears
      as functional ethics from outside. Tell: ethical
      conclusions consistently benefit axis owner at
      population substrate cost, despite accurate
      instrument. Counter: axis examination, not
      instrument examination -- the instrument is not
      the problem.

  INTERVENTION DIVERGENCE [v18]:
    Moral Noise -> recalibrate instrument [axis is fine].
    Moral Capture -> liberate instrument from owner.
    Moral Atrophy -> rebuild instrument from outside.
    ACWL -> examine and revise axis, not instrument.
    Misidentifying ACWL as Moral Capture = wrong
      intervention: liberating a correctly-functioning
      instrument from a non-owner does nothing if the
      axis is still captured. Error: treating the
      instrument as the problem when the axis is.

  Science connection: ethics-as-empirical-instrument
    requires MORAL LEGIBILITY as precondition. Science
    as primary culture [L5 candidate] requires ethics
    derivable from outputs not authority. ACWL is the
    specific failure mode that allows science culture to
    appear ethical while serving extraction -- instrument
    intact; axis captured at funding/priority level.
  Test [provisional]: can actor derive ethical conclusions
    from actual generative/extractive output data
    independently of authority, tradition, or group
    pressure? If yes: check whether axis is genuine or
    externally installed [ACWL test].
  Primary case needed: none confirmed. ACWL candidate
    pool: professional ethics in captured institutions.
    Moral Atrophy candidate pool: populations under
    sustained ICD + FC stack.
  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS; CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING; EVIL
    [CC INVERTED]; GOOD; WIP 14 [L4.5]; FC+ECC stack
    [extraction mechanism index]; FORMATION CAPTURE.

WIP 14: L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[See WIP Active List in main theory for full entry.]
[Staged here as reminder: theory-level content, not yet confirmed.]
[v18: near-cases stress-tested; consistent termination mechanism
  holds; S3 pattern evidence formalized in WIP Active List entry.]
[v20: CARRIER ESCAPE named as specific threshold condition; positive
  case requirement sharpened; see CARRIER ESCAPE block below.]

  CARRIER ESCAPE [named threshold condition -- v20]:
    L4.5 requires methodology to escape carrier dependency and
    become self-reproducing in substrate independently. Near-cases
    confirm: methodology quality is insufficient. Athens, Islamic
    Golden Age, Scottish Enlightenment all had the methodology.
    None achieved carrier escape before termination. The threshold
    is not methodology presence -- it is methodology survival after
    carrier destruction.
    Positive case requirement [three conditions, all required]:
    [1] Developed distributed correction capacity.
    [2] Survived destruction of carrier institutions.
    [3] Became self-reproducing in substrate without specialized
        formation requirements at civilizational scale.
    Mathematical proof culture: meets [1] and [2]; fails [3]
      [formation requirement gates substrate access at scale].
    Internet-era candidates [Wikipedia governance, distributed
      open methodology]: structurally capable of meeting all
      three; too young for generational confirmation. Earliest
      viable confirmation window 2030-2040.
    No confirmed positive case meeting all three conditions.
    Falsification: documented methodology meeting all three
      conditions with generational confirmation record.

WIP 16: ASYMMETRY TYPES [predatory / developmental / scale]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: PARTIAL -- developmental type PROMOTED v21: stress-test clean;
  GA requirement in operator confirmed load-bearing and explicit in
  promoted entry; extraction definition stable; moved to Section One.
  Scale type WIP STAGING -- primary case still needed. Kyubey re-
  examination open under scale type [developmental promotion does not
  resolve]. Predatory type confirmed [existing framework].
  Origin: Madoka Magica framework read + Kyubey scale-asymmetry
  challenge to MSC definition. v19 first entry.
  v20: developmental primary case confirmed; four-condition instrument
  placed; integration check clean. v21: developmental promoted.]

  PROBLEM STATEMENT:
    The framework currently collapses all information asymmetry
    into MSC [Measurement Surface Capture] or the extraction
    operational definition [trickery as foundation]. This is
    insufficient. Three structurally distinct asymmetry types
    produce the same surface reading [information withheld;
    receiver does not understand the full situation] but have
    different mechanisms, different intervention paths, and
    different ethical status.

  THREE CANDIDATE TYPES:

  PREDATORY ASYMMETRY [confirmed -- existing framework]:
    Information withheld serves operator at receiver's expense.
    Operator benefits from the ignorance persisting.
    Tell: closing the asymmetry would damage operator's position.
    Intervention: MSC counter [independent measurement].
    Example: Kyubey withholding grief seed endpoint if purpose
      is to maintain willing contractors. Standard extraction.

  DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [candidate -- not yet confirmed]:
    Information withheld serves receiver's eventual capacity.
    Asymmetry designed to close over time as instrument develops.
    Operator benefits from the asymmetry closing -- a developed
    receiver is the intended output, not a permanently dependent
    one. Tell: operator actively works toward asymmetry closing;
    full information is the goal, not the threat.
    Examples [candidate]: parent withholding mortality detail
      from toddler; apprenticeship; initiation architecture
      where sequence and readiness determine transmission timing.
    Failure mode: developmental asymmetry used as cover for
      predatory asymmetry. Tell: asymmetry never closes; operator
      benefits from continued dependency.
    Intervention: examine whether asymmetry is closing. If not
      closing on any timeline: revert to predatory reading.
    Primary case needed. Not yet confirmed as distinct type.

  SCALE ASYMMETRY [candidate -- not yet confirmed]:
    Information structurally untransmittable across the gap
    between operational level and individual instrument. Not
    hidden by choice; inaccessible by structure. The scale
    gap produces asymmetry that cannot close because the
    individual instrument cannot hold the operational frame
    regardless of willingness. Tell: operator attempts honest
    transmission; receiver cannot process the information
    usefully; honest transmission produces same response as
    deception because the instrument lacks the capacity to
    distinguish them.
    Kyubey case [FLAG FOR RE-EXAMINATION]:
      Explains the entropy problem honestly; Madoka cannot
      process "your suffering is load-bearing for universal
      entropy balance" as anything other than violation.
      Honest transmission produces horror. Asymmetry may
      not be maintained by Kyubey's choice -- may be
      produced by the scale gap between thermodynamic
      necessity and individual formation-window instrument.
      If scale asymmetry confirmed: KYUBEY case may not be
      clean extraction architecture. Extraction operational
      definition may not apply cleanly. Kyubey possibly
      closer to necessary cost at civilizational scale.
      The individual instruments [Madoka, Homura] acting
      at personal scale against a thermodynamic problem
      may be cosmically adolescent in the specific sense
      of applying individual-scale solutions to scale-
      asymmetry problems. Neither reading confirmed.
      Evidence for predatory: targets formation-window
        girls specifically; could target older instruments.
        Ignorance may be instrumentally useful, not just
        structurally unavoidable.
      Evidence for scale: attempts honest explanation;
        explanation produces horror not understanding;
        response functionally identical whether Kyubey
        explains or withholds. Both readings may be
        simultaneously true.
    Other candidate cases: some religious transmission
      architecture; some L4.5 systems where civilizational-
      scale operation produces scale asymmetry as structural
      property not extraction mechanism deployment.
    Primary case needed. Not yet confirmed as distinct type.

  FRAMEWORK IMPLICATIONS IF CONFIRMED:
    Extraction operational definition requires amendment:
      current definition does not distinguish predatory
      from scale asymmetry. A system operating under
      genuine scale asymmetry may meet surface criteria
      for extraction without meeting structural criteria.
    MSC extraction mechanism definition requires sub-typing: developmental
      and scale asymmetry may not be extraction mechanisms -- may be
      structural properties of certain coherence configs.
    Historical initiation architecture requires re-
      examination: most high-level knowledge transmission
      operated on asymmetry assumptions. Current framework
      classifies all gatekeeping as extraction-adjacent.
      If developmental asymmetry confirmed, some recoverable
      as legitimate transmission architecture.

  FALSIFICATION CONDITIONS [provisional]:
    Predatory: falsified by documented case where operator
      benefited from asymmetry closing and worked toward it.
    Developmental: falsified by no documented case where
      asymmetry closed serving receiver without operator
      benefit from closing.
    Scale: falsified by documented case where honest
      transmission across confirmed scale gap produced
      functional understanding in receiver.

  MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT [developmental type -- v20]:
    Four-condition test [all required for developmental
    classification]:
    [1] Asymmetry maintained on receiver's developmental
        schedule, not operator's convenience.
    [2] Asymmetry closes when receiver reaches capacity.
    [3] Closure serves receiver's autonomous function.
    [4] Operator has no structural interest in asymmetry
        persisting beyond receiver's readiness.
    All four required. Any one absent: revert to predatory
    reading regardless of stated intent.
    Behavioral test, not intent test -- stated intent to
    develop the receiver is compatible with predatory
    mechanism. Test the conditions, not the claim.

  PRIMARY CASE CONFIRMED [developmental type -- v20]:
    DEVELOPMENTAL_ASYMMETRY_FAMILY [S:3] written v20.
    Four-condition test applied and confirmed against
    both good developmental asymmetry [all four met]
    and contaminated version [conditions 2 and 4 fail].
    Integration check clean: extraction definition
    sharpens without amendment [operator benefit from
    ignorance persisting is the load-bearing distinction];
    MSC clarifying note added [developmental withholding
    is not MSC deployment -- mechanism inverted].
    Developmental type ready for promotion to candidate
    pending new session stress-test.
    Scale type still needs primary case.
    Predatory type already confirmed [existing framework].

  Cross-ref: EXTRACTION [operational definition --
    sharpened, no amendment required; developmental
    asymmetry does not meet extraction criteria];
    MSC [extraction mechanism index -- developmental withholding not
    MSC; note added]; [REF:KYUBEY] [scale asymmetry
    separate problem -- developmental confirmation
    does not resolve]; [REF:TOWER_OF_BABEL] [predatory
    clean case]; L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION
    THRESHOLD [WIP 14 -- scale asymmetry may be
    structural property of L4.5 systems]; GENUINE AXIS
    [developmental asymmetry requires genuine axis in
    operator]; FAMILY COHERENCE TYPE [primary case pool];
    [CASE:DEVELOPMENTAL_ASYMMETRY_FAMILY] [primary
    confirmation case].

WIP 17: PURPOSE RESTATEMENT [effective distribution / value identification /
  boundary control / resource management -- v20]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: PROMOTED v21 -- integration check clean; three-goal structure
  holds under all stress; no goal subsumes others; distribution framing
  additive; no existing theory amended; moved to Section One. Entry
  retained here for reference. Canonical version in Section One.]

  CURRENT IMPLICIT PURPOSE:
    Map coherence and extraction patterns across individuals,
    institutions, and civilizations.

  PROPOSED EXPLICIT PURPOSE:
    Identify where value is generated, where it is captured, where
    it leaks, and who absorbs the cost -- in order to support
    effective distribution at any scale.

  THREE OPERATIVE GOALS:

  VALUE IDENTIFICATION -- locate what is actually generative vs what
    extracts from generative capacity without return. Distinct from
    moral evaluation. A thing can be legal, normal, and socially
    approved while still being net-extractive on this instrument.

  BOUNDARY CONTROL -- identify where distribution boundaries are,
    who set them, who they serve, and whether they are legible to
    the people inside them. Most people operate inside boundaries
    they did not choose and cannot see clearly. The framework makes
    them visible.

  RESOURCE MANAGEMENT -- given accurate value and boundary maps,
    what allocation decisions follow. Applies at individual scale
    [warehouse worker, cognitive load, energy budget] and
    civilizational scale [generational extraction, institutional
    capture].

  RELATIONSHIP TO EXISTING THEORY:
    Extraction always wrong -- preserved; reframed as extraction
      produces ineffective distribution, which is the structural
      reason it degrades generative cycles rather than a moral claim.
    Evil/good binary -- dissolved; distribution lens replaces it.
      The hamster manager, NC labor architecture, Lenovo conditions
      -- none require moral condemnation to analyze accurately.
      Who captures, who absorbs cost, what the mechanism is.
      That is sufficient.
    Centralization not always wrong -- centralized structures are
      distribution mechanisms. Most produce capture at every layer
      due to distance from value generation. Some do not. The
      instrument now has a clear test.

  ASYMMETRY NOTE:
    Corps and consulting firms already have sophisticated versions
    of this. The framework's value is not revealing something
    unknown to powerful actors -- it is making the model legible
    to people currently absorbing cost without an accurate map of
    why. That asymmetry is the political dimension of the purpose
    restatement.

  INTEGRATION CHECK REQUIRED -- NEW SESSION:
    Extraction mechanism index: does every entry orient correctly under
      distribution framing.
    Case library: do existing S-ratings hold under value
      identification lens or do some need revision.
    Political section: generational extraction, regulatory capture,
      neurodivergent population entries -- check against new
      purpose statement.
    WIP 15 and 16: do promotion criteria change under new purpose
      framing.

  [PRIMARY_Q: Does naming effective distribution as the explicit
    purpose clarify or constrain the framework's analytical range?
    Does the three-goal structure hold under stress or does one
    goal subsume the others?]

END OF WIP THEORY STAGING [v21]

SECTION ONE -- METHOD
[SEC:BASIC_QUALITIES_OF_COHERENCE]
==========================================================================
FRAMEWORK PURPOSE [promoted v21]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Explicit purpose: identify where value is generated, where it is
  captured, where it leaks, and who absorbs the cost -- in order to
  support effective distribution at any scale.

  THREE OPERATIVE GOALS:

  VALUE IDENTIFICATION -- locate what is actually generative vs what
    extracts from generative capacity without return. Distinct from
    moral evaluation. A thing can be legal, normal, and socially
    approved while still being net-extractive on this instrument.

  BOUNDARY CONTROL -- identify where distribution boundaries are, who
    set them, who they serve, and whether they are legible to the
    people inside them. Most people operate inside boundaries they did
    not choose and cannot see clearly. The framework makes them visible.

  RESOURCE MANAGEMENT -- given accurate value and boundary maps, what
    allocation decisions follow. Applies at individual scale [warehouse
    worker, cognitive load, energy budget] and civilizational scale
    [generational extraction, institutional capture].

  RELATIONSHIP TO EXISTING THEORY:
    Extraction always wrong -- preserved; reframed as extraction
      produces ineffective distribution. The structural reason it
      degrades generative cycles is now explicit, not implied.
    Evil/good definitions -- stable; distribution lens adds the
      analytical frame underneath. Evil = surplus routed to minority
      at majority cost with CC absent/inverted = ineffective
      distribution by structural definition. No amendment.
    Centralization -- distribution mechanism. Most produce capture
      at every layer due to distance from value generation. Some do
      not. The instrument now has a clear test.
    Extraction mechanism index -- each extraction mechanism distorts one or more of the three
      goals. ICD: resource management [SR without SP]. FC: value
      identification [installs false axis]. MSC: boundary control
      [captures the legibility surface]. Orientation confirmed clean.

  GENERATION CLARIFICATION [v21]:
    Generation is a structural requirement for anything to function --
    not the problem. Extraction mechanisms require generation as much
    as distribution strategies do. Both depend on generative capacity.
    The problem is never generation itself. The problem is routing:
    where does surplus go, who controls the direction, who set the
    boundary that determines the destination.
    Distribution strategy and extraction mechanism are therefore not
    opposites on a generation axis -- they are opposites on a routing
    axis. Distribution strategy: surplus routed outward to recipients
    in a way that enables their capacity. Extraction mechanism: surplus
    routed inward to operator at recipient substrate cost.
    Implication: attacking generation to counter extraction destroys
    the substrate both depend on. Correct intervention targets routing
    and boundary control, not generation itself.

  ASYMMETRY NOTE:
    Powerful actors already operate sophisticated versions of this
    model. The framework's value is making it legible to people
    currently absorbing cost without an accurate map of why.

  AXIS CAPTURE WARNING:
    The three operative goals are structurally neutral as analytical
    tools. The same map -- value identification, boundary control,
    resource management -- can be run with extraction as the governing
    purpose instead of distribution. A sophisticated extractor arrives
    at identical outputs; they route them differently.
    This is the ACWL failure mode [WIP 15 / MORAL LEGIBILITY]: instrument
    functional; axis captured. The distribution framing does not protect
    against axis capture. What orients the framework toward distribution
    rather than extraction is the GENUINE AXIS of the operator -- not
    the tool itself.
    Tell: who absorbs the cost of the map being made? If the people
    providing the data absorb the cost of the analysis, the framework
    is running in extraction orientation regardless of stated purpose.
  Cross-ref: MORAL LEGIBILITY [ACWL failure mode]; GENUINE AXIS;
    AXIS CAPTURE WITH LEGIBILITY.

  Integration check: v21 [clean -- all stress points passed;
    three-goal structure holds; no goal subsumes others].
  Cross-ref: EXTRACTION [operational definition]; EVIL; GOOD;
    GENERATIVE CYCLE; SURPLUS PRODUCTION; extraction mechanism index [all entries].

BASIC QUALITIES OF COHERENCE
Organized by Nested Cycle Model [primary -- see THEORY section].
Tower vocabulary retained as nesting-depth descriptor.
C1 [fundamental] / C2 [epistemic] / C3 [temporal].
Q-numbers preserved for case library reference.

NESTED CYCLE STRUCTURE OVERVIEW:
  FOUNDATION -- preconditions enabling all cycles
  C1 [fundamental] -- generative domain, core: GENERATIVE CYCLE
  C2 [epistemic] -- readable domain, core: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
  C3 [temporal] -- transmissible domain, core: TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY
  L3.5 -- inter-system interface, core: RECOGNITION [unchanged]
  L4 -- meta domain, core: REFLEXIVITY [confirmed v16]

  Level vocabulary: L1=C1, L2=C2, L3=C3. All existing tests and
  case citations valid. Tower order = nesting depth, not dependency
  sequence. See NESTED CYCLE MODEL in THEORY section.

TOWER CODEPENDENCE MODEL [legacy reference -- superseded by Nested
  Cycle]
  Qualities: tension unit cores and constants only.
  Former support qualities deprecated as qualities; content converted
  to level tests. Case library citation cleanup complete.
  Each integer level has one tension unit [3 similar but different
  qualities; middle difficult but possible = floor of next level].
  Half levels are static analysis checkpoints between integer levels.

  TENSION UNIT QUALITIES [12 confirmed + 3 confirmed at L4]:
    L1: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION / CORRECTION CAPACITY
    L2: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY / REFERENCE
      INTEGRITY
    L3: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY / CAUSAL AXIS
      GROUNDING
    L3.5: RECOGNITION / COMPATIBILITY / RECIPROCITY [confirmed]
    L4: REFLEXIVITY / INTEGRATION / PROPAGATION [confirmed v16 --
      three primary cases]

  CONSTANT QUALITIES [required from L1 upward]:
    CORRECTION CAPACITY -- fires at every level [also L1 tension
      unit]
    BOUNDARY INTEGRITY -- scopes every quality's operation
    TENSION MAINTENANCE -- holds all levels in shape
    Post-L0.5 only. L0.5 pre-constant zone, foundation only.

  FOUNDATION [preconditions -- not tension units]:
    GENUINE AXIS, CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING, SUBSTRATE REGENERATION,
    TENSION DISSOLUTION [TENSION MAINTENANCE opposite]

  LEVEL STRUCTURE RULE:
    Integer levels = dynamic [change, correction firing, cycle].
    Half levels = static [analysis, state reading, no change].

  LEVEL 0.5 [static -- pre-generative]:
    Description: substrate condition before GENERATIVE CYCLE fires.
    Quality: VIABILITY -- meets threshold for GENERATIVE CYCLE.
    Test: substrate above GENERATIVE CYCLE firing threshold?
    High = L1 accessible. Low = immediate collapse.

  LEVEL 1 [dynamic -- generative]:
    GENERATIVE CYCLE [loop] / SURPLUS PRODUCTION [exit] / CORRECTION
      CAPACITY [correction]
    Similar: all generative. Different: sustain / distribute /
      correct.
    Middle: cycle generates, exits, corrects drift -- difficult
      because any one can dominate.
    Tests:
      T1.1 -- Proportional Response [tests CORRECTION CAPACITY]
        Does correction scale match deviation magnitude, or does
        it scale with the power threat the correction poses?
        High = contained, proportional. Low = overcorrection
        [suppression] or under-response [Correction Theatre].
        Key distinction: correction firing against powerful
        deviators is the hard case -- proportionality requires
        same response regardless of corrected system's power to
        retaliate.
        Case: Salamanders [high]; Erebus [low -- correction at
        max toward anyone who questions].

      T1.2 -- Outward Landing [tests SURPLUS PRODUCTION completion]
        Does surplus exit the boundary AND produce usable capacity
        in recipients? Both required -- exit without landing is
        distribution waste; landing without exit is hoarding.
        High = distribution strategy. Low = Accumulation Lock or
        Distribution Waste.
        Case: Bell Labs [high -- exits and lands]; Imperium of
        Man [low -- tithe exits, recipients depleted not enabled].

      T1.3 -- Input Filtering [tests GENERATIVE CYCLE substrate]
        Does screening happen upstream of the cycle, before
        corrupt substrate enters and the cycle runs on it?
        Post-cycle verification is not filtering -- by then the
        cycle has already processed corrupted input correctly.
        High = clean cycle. Low = Garbage Cycle [cycle functions,
        output corrupt because input was not screened].
        Case: nanotube DNA transfer [INPUT FILTERING fails at
        cell boundary -- cycle runs correctly on corrupted
        substrate]; Rosenbaum [hallucinations enter research
        cycle upstream of fact-checking surface].

      T1.4 -- Relational Substrate [tests GENERATIVE CYCLE at
        social scale]
        Stable reciprocal relationships present to sustain cycle?
        AND: does substrate source independence exist, or is all
        substrate supplied by single actor making exit = total
        collapse?
        High = cycle stable, substrate distributed. Low =
        dependency [one source] or isolation [no source].
        Case: Malcolm X/NOI [low -- NOI supplied housing, income,
        identity, community simultaneously; exit = life
        reconstruction].
        [Scale recurrence: T1.4 is the L1 substrate expression
        of the same reciprocal-exchange structure that appears
        as a full tension unit at L3.5. Not a merger -- different
        scope and function at each level. See SCALE RECURRENCE
        in theory.]

      T1.5 -- Cycle Rhythm [tests GENERATIVE CYCLE timing]
        Timing internally generated or externally controlled?
        High = self-regulating. Low = Intermittent Reinforcement.
        [Gate vs gradient: CYCLE STABILITY operates as binary
        gate not continuous throttle at substrate level. Rhythm
        absence = window closed entirely, not reduced. Confirmed
        at two substrates: glymphatic [irregular rhythm prevents
        active SUBSTRATE REGENERATION window from opening];
        thalamus [20-45 Hz oscillation absence = consciousness
        absent not reduced]. Gate distinction is load-bearing --
        partial rhythm is not partial function.]

  LEVEL 2 [dynamic -- epistemic]:
    MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [surface] / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [chain] /
      REFERENCE INTEGRITY [calibration]
    Similar: all epistemic. Different: read / trace / anchor.
    Middle: honest surface, traceable chain, real calibration
      standard -- difficult because any one can be captured
      independently.
    Tests:
      T2.1 -- Causal Memory [tests CAUSAL LEGIBILITY across time]
        Past corrections retained AND applicable to current
        deviation? Both required -- memory that exists but cannot
        be applied does not function as correction history.
        High = calibration stable and usable. Low = Amnesia
        Manufacture [active destruction] or Signal Saturation
        [past indistinguishable from noise].
        Case: Doctor Manhattan [low -- temporal simultaneity
        makes all corrections arrive as noise, past not
        distinguishable]; 1984 Memory Hole [low -- active
        destruction].

      T2.2 -- Correction Direction [tests CORRECTION CAPACITY
        at L2]
        Two distinct questions, both required:
        [A] Is CORRECTION CAPACITY distributed across independent
            points or monopolized by single actor?
        [B] Is correction aimed at actual cause or proxy/symptom?
        Failure A: Correction Monopoly -- fires only toward
        monopolist's benefit regardless of aim.
        Failure B: Correction Displacement -- distributed but
        aimed wrong; visible activity without resolving deviation.
        Both can fail independently. Both must hold for high
        score.
        Case: Gwyn [A fails -- correction monopolized]; McNamara
        [B fails -- distributed, aimed at body count metric not
        war].

      T2.3 -- Signal Reading [tests MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY input
        boundary]
        Two distinct questions, both required:
        [A] Signal content: what is it? [substance correct?]
        [B] Signal source: who is it from? [origin trustworthy?]
        Both can fail independently. ESG cases: content appears
        distribution strategy-shaped [A passes] but source is extraction actor
        [B fails]. Charitable donation capture deployment: source
        appears legitimate [B passes] but content is extraction
        architecture [A fails].
        High = both correct. Low = Reception Blindness [either].
        Case: ESG deployment [A passes, B fails]; Obligation
        Manufacture [B passes, A fails].

  LEVEL 2.5 [static -- between epistemic and temporal]:
    Description: corrected state duration relative to transmission
      window.
    Quality: PERSISTENCE
    Test: corrected state survive until transmission window opens?
    High = L3 receives corrected state. Low = Correction
      Evaporation.

  LEVEL 3 [dynamic -- temporal]:
    TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [sent] / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [usable]
      / CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [grounded]
    Similar: all temporal. Different: content / revision /
      anchoring.
    Middle: method transmitted, successor revises, axis grounded
      in outputs -- difficult because fidelity and autonomy pull
      apart.
    Tests:
      T3.1 -- Package Sufficiency [tests TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
        content]
        Does transmitted package contain method or conclusion?
        Method = procedure successor can run and revise.
        Conclusion = destination without route; successor receives
        where to arrive, not how to travel or how to check if
        arrival was correct.
        High = correctable inheritance [method]. Low = Hollow
        Transmission [conclusion only].
        Key operative variable: method vs conclusion, not volume
        of transmission. High-volume conclusion transmission
        [Augustine] scores lower than low-volume method
        transmission [Ignatius Spiritual Exercises].
        Case: Ignatius [high -- Exercises are procedure, reader
        generates own outputs]; Augustine [low -- doctrine
        transmitted, method not transmissible].

      T3.2 -- Axis Reality [tests CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]
        Is the axis traceable to actual outputs, or is it sincere
        but ungrounded? Distinct from whether axis is genuine
        [GENUINE AXIS] -- axis can be internally generated,
        examined, and real to the system while having no causal
        connection to the world it claims to act on.
        High = axis grounded in actual measurable outputs.
        Low = Sincere Delusion [genuine axis, zero output
        connection].
        Case: McNamara [low -- axis genuine, not grounded in
        actual war outcomes]; Maiad [low -- genuine axis, outputs
        unachievable]; cargo cults [low -- sincere axis, no
        causal connection to desired outcomes].

      T3.3 -- Correction Landing [tests CORRECTION CAPACITY
        at L3]
        Does correction change the thing it targets, or does
        visible correction activity occur without the target
        changing? Distinct from T2.2 [direction] -- correction
        can be distributed, aimed at actual cause, and still
        not land.
        High = target changes after correction fires. Low =
        Correction Theatre [visible activity, target unchanged].
        Case: McNamara [low -- strategy adjustments fired
        continuously at metrics while war outcomes unchanged];
        institutional after-action reviews [low -- correct
        diagnosis produced, structure unchanged].

  LEVEL 3.5 [static -- between temporal and meta]:
    Description: whether coherent systems can read and connect.
    Tension unit: RECOGNITION / COMPATIBILITY / RECIPROCITY
    Similar: all inter-system. Different: reading / connecting /
      sustaining.
    Middle: COMPATIBILITY -- requires SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY in both
      systems; neither can engage without losing own axis.
      Recognition without it = accurate isolation. Reciprocity
      without it = extraction.
    Test: Read? Connect without depletion? Sustain?
    [Confirmed: Malcolm X post-Mecca [recognition + partial
    compatibility, reciprocity incomplete at death]; Merton/Suzuki
    [recognition + compatibility confirmed, reciprocity not yet
    institutionally stable]. Both cases hit same asymmetric
    failure pattern -- confirms triad is doing real work.
    Secondary: Enemy Mine [1985 film -- Davidge/Jeriba] confirms
    COMPATIBILITY emerging from structural constraint rather than
    willingness. Forced survival contact produces genuine
    COMPATIBILITY without prior RECOGNITION choice. Suggests
    COMPATIBILITY is structural not volitional -- the architecture
    of contact determines whether exchange depletes or sustains.]

    RECOGNITION
      Accurate reading of another system's coherence state across
      system boundaries. Not detection of presence -- reading of
      quality stack. Requires own MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY to be
      functional and oriented outward. A system that cannot read
      its own surface cannot read another's.
      Failure: Coherence Blindness -- high-coherence system
      present and detectable; coherence state unreadable.
      Distinct from MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [reads own surface] --
      Recognition reads another system's surface.
      Test: Other system's coherence state readable?

    COMPATIBILITY
      Structural capacity for exchange between two systems without
      one system's generative cycle running on the other's
      substrate depletion. Not willingness -- architecture. Both
      systems must have sufficient TENSION MAINTENANCE to hold
      their own structure under contact, and sufficient SUCCESSOR
      AUTONOMY to engage without axis capture. Low compatibility
      means contact itself is the extraction mechanism: the more coherent system
      pulls the less coherent one into an extraction relationship
      regardless of intent.
      Failure: Extraction Interface -- connection forms, appears
      reciprocal at surface, one system's GENERATIVE CYCLE running
      on the other's substrate. Structurally identical to
      parasitism. Most dangerous failure mode because it mimics
      the success state [connection formed] while producing the
      opposite result.
      Distinct from RECIPROCITY [dynamic maintenance of exchange]
      -- Compatibility is structural precondition at formation.
      Test: Exchange possible without asymmetric depletion?

    RECIPROCITY
      Exchange between compatible systems sustains both systems'
      generative capacity over time. Not symmetric output -- each
      system receives what its GENERATIVE CYCLE depends on, which
      need not be identical. The mycorrhizal exchange is
      reciprocal: tree provides carbon, fungus provides
      phosphorus, neither the same thing, both GENERATIVE CYCLE
      sustained.
      Requires TENSION MAINTENANCE in the exchange itself --
      the relationship must hold productive tension rather than
      drifting toward one system's dominance.
      Failure: Drift to Extraction -- begins genuinely reciprocal,
      TENSION MAINTENANCE fails over time, exchange degrades to
      one-directional flow.
      Test: Exchange sustains both over time?

  LEVEL 4 [dynamic -- meta-correction]:
    Tension unit: REFLEXIVITY / INTEGRATION / PROPAGATION
    [CONFIRMED v16 -- THREE primary cases:
      COHERENCE_FRAMEWORK_SELF [T8 mandatory; recursive].
      BENEDICT_BENEDICTINE [no T8; Cistercian reform cycle;
        700-year external record; non-recursive confirmation].
      JESUIT [Examen as daily meta-correction procedure;
        PROPAGATION strongest in library; 480-year record].
    Combined confidence substantially higher than single case.
    L4 Mimicry WIP: see WIP Item 13 -- confirmation criteria
    require a named failure mode to be complete.]
    Similar: all meta-level. Different: revise / absorb / transmit.
    Middle: INTEGRATION -- reflexivity is cognitively accessible
    [systems can notice their own correction failures] but
    integration requires changing the mechanism while it runs.
    Reflexivity without it = oscillation. Propagation without it
    = each generation relearns the same correction failure.
    Test: Pattern corrected? Held? Transmitted?
    Structural limit: L4 attainment may require L3 functional
    across sufficient fraction of system to enable meta-correction
    transmission. Current conditions [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
    suppression at population scale] make L4 structurally
    impossible regardless of individual cases. L4 cases, if
    they exist, would be found only in high-coherence subgroups
    with functional L3 distribution.

    REFLEXIVITY
      A feedback pathway exists from correction outputs back to
      the correction architecture itself. The structure routes
      correction results to where corrections are generated,
      making the pattern visible to the mechanism. Without this
      pathway the correction architecture is opaque to itself --
      CORRECTION CAPACITY fires, results accumulate, pattern
      errors cannot be read regardless of how well CORRECTION
      CAPACITY operates at object level.
      Failure: Correction Lock -- no feedback pathway to
      correction architecture. McNamara case: CORRECTION CAPACITY
      functioning at object level; feedback pathway to systems-
      analysis architecture absent.
      Distinct from CORRECTION CAPACITY [fires corrections] --
      Reflexivity is whether the architecture receives its own
      output as input.
      Test: Feedback pathway from correction outputs to
      correction architecture present?

    INTEGRATION
      Corrections produced by Reflexivity absorbed into the
      system's operating structure, not just acknowledged or
      noted. The difference between a system that can identify
      its correction pattern failure and a system that changes
      how it corrects.
      Requires TENSION MAINTENANCE at meta level -- the system
      must hold tension between existing correction architecture
      and the proposed revision without collapsing [continuing
      unchanged = Reflexive Oscillation; abandoning correction =
      CORRECTION CAPACITY failure].
      Failure: Reflexive Oscillation -- identifies pattern
      failure, cannot incorporate revision, cycles back to same
      failure.
      Distinct from REFLEXIVITY [identifies] -- Integration is
      whether identification changes the operating structure.
      Test: Correction pattern revision held in structure?

    PROPAGATION
      Revised correction pattern crosses transmission boundary to
      successors. Distinct from TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
      [transmits content axis] -- Propagation transmits
      correction methodology itself.
      A system can transmit conclusions, transmit correctable
      method [T3.1 high], and still fail to transmit how to
      detect and revise systematic correction errors. Requires
      Integration complete [cannot transmit unabsorbed revision].
      Failure: Generational Reset -- each successor discovers
      same correction pattern failure independently.
      Test: Revised pattern crosses transmission boundary?

  LEVEL 4.5 [static -- conditional]:
    Description: True coherence structurally enables adjacent
      systems.
    Quality: PROPAGATION [distinct from L4 -- structural
      enabling, not meta-correction transmission]
    Test: high-coherence systems raise floor of nearby systems?
    High = coherence ecology. Low = coherence islands.
    [Overlap with SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION resolved: OUTWARD LANDING
    measures surplus flow across boundary [SURPLUS PRODUCTION
    completion]. L4.5 measures structural ecological effect --
    adjacent system floor raised by presence of high-coherence
    system, not by surplus transfer. Bell Labs confirms both
    as distinct simultaneous effects. Closed.]

  ASCENDING PATH:
    L0.5 viability check before L1.
    Constants [CORRECTION CAPACITY / BOUNDARY INTEGRITY /
      TENSION MAINTENANCE] throughout post-L0.5.
    Each level tension unit near center = floor of next.
    All tension units centered + constants stable = True.
    Constant failure corrupts all levels. Core failure cascades
      up.
    [Confirmed: Feanor -- CORRECTION CAPACITY fails L1, cascade.]
    [Confirmed: Skull Knight -- all centered, True.]
    Bootstrapping: SUBSTRATE REGENERATION stabilizes L1. Body
      first.

QUALITY DEFINITIONS [reference -- see Tower model above for
  primary structure]
Primary organization: Tower Codependence Model levels.

FOUNDATION LAYER [preconditions -- enable all levels]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  GENUINE AXIS
     [Tower: FOUNDATION]
     Orientation generated internally with self-examination
     present.
     Failure: imposed axis.

     AXIS DRIFT [named state -- v21]:
     Axis formed; partially intact; progressively softened
     by sustained social compliance channel pressure.
     The realistic population is not binary [genuine axis /
     no axis]. The operative spectrum:
       Genuine axis maintained: rare, high cost, structurally
         isolated. Requires CC against continuous correction
         pressure that depletes same substrate the axis is
         trying to protect.
       Genuine axis drifting: axis formed, attractor still
         present, conclusions increasingly softened before
         they land. Intellectual cowardice pattern firing
         repeatedly under sustained social pressure. The
         axis still generates outputs; still fires recognition
         responses to genuine signal; the conclusions are
         not followed as far as the axis requires.
       Captured axis: axis formed, then replaced by external
         installation. Instrument believes new axis is its own.
       Axis never formed: formation window closed without
         conditions being met.
     DRIFT IS BETTER THAN ABSENT [load-bearing finding]:
       Drifting axis has an attractor. The formation occurred.
       The axis can be partially re-examined and some of what
       was softened can be recovered. The correction path is:
       follow the conclusions further than the social compliance
       channel rewards. The instrument knows where it stopped;
       it can start again from that point.
       Axis never formed has no attractor. Nothing to recover.
       Only available move is installation of a replacement
       axis -- which is Formation Capture by definition,
       regardless of content. The formation has to have
       occurred for genuine correction to be possible.
     REALITY AS ANTI-AXIS ENVIRONMENT [confirmed]:
       The extraction architecture continuously applies
       pressure against axis maintenance. Every social
       interaction where the axis produces an inconvenient
       output applies correction pressure toward abandonment.
       Intellectual cowardice fires repeatedly -- not as
       a personal failing but as the rational individual
       response to a social environment where axis expression
       is continuously punished and axis softening is
       continuously rewarded. Drift is not weakness; it is
       the expected output of genuine axis formation operating
       inside an extraction architecture. The surprise is not
       that most axes drift. The surprise is that any survive.
     TUCKER PARALLEL [illustrative]:
       Genuine axis present -- still generates real substrate
       readings, still fires on actual institutional capture
       and extraction patterns. Axis drifting toward
       contaminated mechanism [Jones pattern layered on top]
       and away from structural intervention. The genuine
       axis is detectable beneath the drift. That is
       structurally better than captured axis or axis void
       dressed as neutrality -- which is the mainstream
       alternative. Better does not mean correct. The
       drift is still the Jones pattern; the inoculation
       effect is still the primary damage. T3 High applies.
     INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE AS DRIFT MECHANISM [cross-ref]:
       Intellectual Cowardice [named failure mode, this
       section] is the conclusion-step suppression that
       produces drift. Not axis absence -- axis present,
       conclusion suppressed. The correction is not forming
       an axis; it is following the one you have further
       than the social compliance channel will reward.
       Each instance of conclusion-suppression is a drift
       event. Accumulated drift events are how genuine
       axis becomes drifting axis without a single
       identifiable capture moment.
     Cross-ref: INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE [conclusion-step
       suppression as drift mechanism]; FORMATION CAPTURE
       [what drift slides toward if uncorrected];
       GENUINE AXIS [the attractor that makes recovery
       possible]; SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION
       [the channel applying the continuous correction
       pressure]; JONES PATTERN [Section Five -- named
       case of axis-present-with-drift producing worse
       epistemic outcome than no signal at all, because
       the drift contaminates the genuine readings];
       IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [behavioral record reveals
       whether drift has occurred -- the axis expressed
       in accumulated choices not in stated positions].

     FORMATION THEORY [v16 -- consolidated]:
     GA forms during a developmental window through internal
     generation enabled by four conditions:

     1. FORMATION WINDOW: approximately ages 12-18 [prefrontal
        development window]. Window closes; axis formed during
        it persists. Not the only window but the most
        consequential. Post-window axis revision possible but
        high-cost [Wittgenstein, Malcolm X]. Pre-window:
        structural not axial [C1 without C2].

     2. FRICTION TYPE + QUANTITY: calibrated friction [challenge
        without overwhelm] is the structural precondition.
        Quantity: too little = CC atrophy; calibrated =
        generative; excess = substrate destruction. Optimal =
        slightly above average target [mathematical confirmation,
        2026].
        Type: social, logical/systemic, and sensory friction are
        distinct axes. Calibration on one does not imply
        calibration on another. Formation requires friction on
        the axis the instrument is calibrated to receive --
        matched-axis friction produces axis; mismatched produces
        noise.

     3. IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT: internal generation requires
        idle cycles to fire. ICD during formation window
        prevents axis forming before external content fills the
        gap. Social comparison architecture [variable reward +
        status feedback] fills formation window with externally
        supplied content. Spontaneous Formation Capture -- no
        deployer required. Axis forms on externally supplied
        content by default when idle cycles are absent.

     4. DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY: somatic signal accurate AND
        deliberative read correct. Both required. Somatic layer
        degrades first under stress, ICD, optimization pressure.
        Deliberative layer can appear intact while GA is already
        compromised. Formation requires both layers developing
        together -- somatic substrate bootstraps the deliberative
        layer, not the reverse.

     CAPTURE VULNERABILITY: Formation window is highest-leverage
     attack surface. FC deployed here persists lifetime without
     maintenance. Counter capacity not yet formed -- external
     intervention is the only exit.

     POST-FORMATION REVISION: possible but requires C1 starved
     recovery conditions [substrate depletion + L3.5 contact on
     matched axis + enabling moment]. High cost, partial result.
     Axis revision is not axis formation -- the formation window
     pattern shapes what revision is possible.

     Wisdom Concealment: GENUINE AXIS present but masked in
     hostile environment ["appear the fool" strategy]. Mask is
     BOUNDARY INTEGRITY protection, not GENUINE AXIS absence.
     Sustained concealment degrades axis over time if mask
     becomes default.
     Chanakya [~300 BCE]: "Straight trees are cut first." 2,300
     years of independent confirmation -- same finding, different
     domain.
     Test: axis internally generated and examined?

  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
     [Tower: CONSTANT]
     Honest definition of who is inside the boundary.
     Does not shift to exclude populations experiencing harm.
     Failure: boundary manipulation.
     Linked to MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY -- boundary drift is a
     measurement failure.
     Test: boundary honest and stable?

  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
     [Tower: FOUNDATION]
     System's capacity to restore the substrate its cycle
     depends on.
     Active regeneration -- not passive non-consumption.
     Failure: Substrate Atrophy -- cycle runs while substrate
     diminishes. System doesn't notice because CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     measures cycle output not substrate health.
     Multi-substrate requirement: must operate across ALL
     dependent substrates simultaneously. Single substrate
     depletion while others appear positive = extraction
     obscured across boundaries.
     Passive/Active hybrid [confirmed]: passive = continuous
     baseline maintenance; active = triggered high-intensity
     window [CYCLE RHYTHM gates]. Both failing simultaneously =
     accelerated collapse.
     Confirmed: Bell Labs [intellectual/institutional/physical
     all regenerated]; glymphatic [passive CSF + active deep
     sleep window]; industrial agriculture [SURPLUS PRODUCTION
     high, SUBSTRATE REGENERATION failing].
     Somatic bootstrapping [promoted from extended theory]:
     SUBSTRATE REGENERATION is the entry point for axis
     formation. Circular dependency resolved: managing coherence
     requires axis; coherence enables axis. Resolution:
     SUBSTRATE REGENERATION stability + accumulated somatic
     pattern + time = axis formation without pre-existing
     GENUINE AXIS. Physical discipline [sleep, movement, rhythm]
     bootstraps coherence management before examined axis exists.
     Body-scale GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION /
     SUBSTRATE REGENERATION operating without axis = sphere
     geometry. Repeated somatic experience creates orientation
     bias -- pre-axial pole formation. Sphere develops asymmetry
     under accumulated pressure. Asymmetry stabilizes:
     sphere -> polarized sphere -> torus as GENUINE AXIS locks
     in. Axis not consciously constructed -- emerged from
     accumulated somatic pattern. Body first, axis second.
     Test: substrate restored across all dependent dimensions?
     Somatic signal accuracy: body registers extraction
     architecture accurately before cognitive frame names it.
     Anxiety, fatigue, disconnection = correct T1 correction
     firing. Cognitive narrative [personal pathology, chemical
     imbalance] suppresses the read. Body doing accurate
     MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY; framing is the capture.

     PARALLEL VOCABULARY [Spiral Dynamics / Integral Theory]:
     Spiral Dynamics [Graves / Beck / Cowan] describes
     developmental value systems [vmemes] that emerge in
     sequence as instruments and collectives become more
     complex. The sequence [Beige → Purple → Red → Blue →
     Orange → Green → Yellow → Turquoise] maps approximately
     onto what the framework calls accessible CC mechanisms:
     what correction pathways are legible and legitimate
     to an instrument given its formation conditions.
     Red [power/dominance] cannot recognize Blue [rule/
     order] correction as legitimate. Green [communitarian]
     cannot recognize Yellow [systemic] readings as anything
     other than elitism. This is the framework's FORMATION
     THEORY and CC CAPACITY language in different vocabulary.
     Integral Theory [Wilber] extends this into a four-
     quadrant model [interior/exterior × individual/
     collective] useful as a multi-axis reading supplement.
     CLOSED EPISTEMIC CIRCLE RISK [critical]:
       The developmental hierarchy is the primary risk.
       If the framework operator is at a "higher" level,
       any challenge can be attributed to lower-level
       development rather than examined as genuine
       falsification. The theory becomes self-sealing --
       Wilber's own writing increasingly dismissed critics
       as "pre-rational" or "green meme" rather than
       engaging structurally. This is AXIS PROTECTION AS
       CC deployed inside the theory itself. Use with
       explicit T3 High flagging whenever level attribution
       is the response to a challenge.
     The framework already has the structural equivalent
     of what is genuine in both. Spiral Dynamics is
     useful as secondary vocabulary for readings where
     formation-capacity language might land differently
     with a particular audience. Neither needs integration
     as theory.
     Suggested by: Mistral [secondary stress-test AI, v21].

       CIRCLE [political section -- same self-sealing
