     INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE [named failure mode -- v21]:
     Following an argument almost to its conclusion then
     stepping back when the conclusion has a social cost.
     Distinct from ignorance [axis not present] and from
     dishonesty [axis present, conclusion reached, concealed].
     The specific mechanism: axis fires, deviation detection
     accurate, conclusion correctly identified -- then social
     compliance channel fires before the conclusion lands.
     The instrument softens the idea, redirects it, or lets
     it go entirely without consciously deciding to. The
     step back feels like prudence, nuance, or fairness.
     It is axis suppression by social compliance channel.
     Schopenhauer's formulation [1851]: intellectual cowardice
     is not fear of confrontation or sensitivity to criticism
     -- it is the habit of following an idea almost all the
     way to its logical conclusion, then quietly stepping
     back when you sense the conclusion might cost you
     something socially. The cost need not be explicit.
     The imagined reaction is sufficient.
     Population-scale consequence: in an environment where
     a single unpopular take produces instant mass reaction,
     intellectual cowardice is individually rational. A
     culture where intellectual cowardice is individually
     rational produces no original thought at the population
     level -- not because individuals lack axis, but because
     the social compliance channel systematically overrides
     axis examination at the conclusion step.
     Tell [individual]: when did you last share an opinion
       you knew most people around you would push back on?
       If you cannot remember, the social compliance channel
       is overriding.
     Tell [content]: does the piece correctly diagnose a
       structural extraction mechanism and then prescribe
       individual behavioral response? The conclusion the
       structural diagnosis requires [naming actors,
       proposing structural constraint] was stepped back
       from. The piece is demonstrating the pattern it
       diagnoses.
     Distinct from CC OVERFIRE: CC OVERFIRE fires too hard
       toward correct targets. Intellectual Cowardice fires
       correctly, identifies the conclusion, and then stops
       before landing it. One is calibration failure; one
       is social compliance channel override.
     Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [the axis that fires]; SOCIAL
       APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [the channel doing the
       overriding]; CC OVERFIRE [Section Five -- calibration
       failure, distinct mechanism]; FORMATION CAPTURE [social
       compliance channel weighting is formation-installed];
       Wolf/Carr [science appendix -- the same pattern at
       neurological scale: the capacity to sustain conclusion-
       reaching degrades when the social compliance channel
       is exercised more than the axis].

     IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [named concept -- v21]:
     The axis is not what is stated or believed. It is what
     is expressed in the accumulated record of choices made
     when no external reward or punishment was present.
     Doing a certain thing IS holding a certain position.
     The action is the ideology made physically manifest.
     Not ideology stated. Not ideology believed. Ideology
     in the doing.
     Distinction from stated position:
       Stated: "I care about worker welfare." Action:
         resist four-day week when output data supports it.
         Action reveals axis. Statement is legitimacy surface.
       Stated: "I value humanity." Action: spend centuries
         developing a flower-finding spell with zero tactical
         utility. Action reveals axis before the character
         consciously holds it as a position.
     The behavioral record is the only reliable axis read.
     The axis that generates the actions is the real axis.
     Everything else is legitimacy surface or sincere
     self-misunderstanding [SINCERE DELUSION: stated axis
     and action-axis diverge without the actor detecting
     the divergence].
     MAGIC SYSTEMS THAT ENCODE THIS [named examples]:
       Frieren [Frieren: Beyond Journey's End]: magic as
         embodied structural precision + axis expression.
         The flower-finding spell is centuries of effort
         for zero tactical gain -- behavioral record with
         no external reward or punishment. Reveals axis
         before conscious statement. She didn't know she
         cared about humans until the magic already knew.
         Cannot perform a different magic than the one
         she is. The doing is the position.
       Fullmetal Alchemist [equivalent exchange]: ideology
         built into physics. You cannot extract without
         paying. You cannot fake generosity in alchemy.
         The magic makes extraction structurally impossible
         -- the system enforces the ideology directly.
         The action is the cost you're willing to pay,
         which is the values made physical. No gap between
         stated and enacted possible -- the physics closes
         the gap. This is the world where EXTRACTION is
         impossible by structural design, which is why it
         feels morally serious in a way most fantasy doesn't.
     WHY/WHAT/WHERE [science appendix connection]:
       In the best magic systems, the why [values, ideology,
       relationship to the world] is embedded in the what
       and where [the specific expression of the practitioner's
       power]. The practitioner is not interchangeable because
       the magic is not separable from what they are. This is
       the complete form: why + what/where + falsification
       [does this magic actually express this axis, or is
       there a gap between stated and enacted].
     POLITICAL APPLICATION [behavioral test]:
       The four-day week employer resistance is ideology in
       the action. The resistance reveals the governing axis
       [routing control over worker time] more accurately
       than any stated position about worker welfare. The
       billionaire who opposes unions is expressing ideology
       in the action. The 40-hour week was won by making
       the action [opposing overtime pay] legible as the
       axis [surplus routed upward].
       The behavioral test is always the primary read:
       what does the accumulated record of choices reveal
       when the actor doesn't know they're being read?
     Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [the axis expressed in action];
       BEHAVIORAL TEST [primary diagnostic tool]; SINCERE
       DELUSION [stated and action-axis diverge without
       detection]; AXIS PRESENCE AS PRIMARY READABLE
       PROPERTY [ART section -- same principle at art
       object scale]; [CASE:FRIEREN] [if written];
       FOUR DAY WEEK [political application -- resistance
       as ideology in action]; ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY
       [employer action reveals axis regardless of stated
       position]; WHY/WHAT/WHERE [science appendix --
       magic systems that encode why in the what/where].

     YAPPING/DOING [plain language diagnostic -- v21]:
     Yapping is free. Production cost is zero regardless
     of accuracy. Volume has no correlation with the
     underlying axis because no axis is required to produce
     it. An axis-void instrument can yap indefinitely.
     Doing is not free. It reveals the axis regardless of
     what was said. The behavioral record is what it is.
     You cannot perform a different doing than the one you
     are.
     TRUST CALIBRATION [structural]:
       High yapping + low doing: the gap between stated
         axis and behavioral axis is being managed through
         output volume. Yapping fills the space where doing
         would have to be. Yapping is the legitimacy surface;
         the doing is the extraction mechanism or its
         absence. Inverse correlation between yapping volume
         and trust is structurally correct -- not cynicism,
         not contrarianism. The zero production cost of
         yapping means volume carries no signal about
         accuracy; only the doing record does.
       Low yapping + high doing: behavioral record speaks
         without legitimacy surface required. Axis legible
         from actions directly. Trust calibrated to record
         not stated position.
     THE SOCIAL COMPLIANCE CHANNEL INVERSION:
       Social environment rewards yapping
         disproportionately. Yapping is legible, shareable,
         quotable, produces immediate social feedback. Doing
         is often slow, unglamorous, produces delayed or
         invisible feedback. Social environment is almost
         perfectly calibrated to produce high yapping /
         low doing as the dominant behavioral strategy.
         This is why the behavioral test is load-bearing
         and the stated position is not. The environment
         selected for the wrong signal.
     SWEENEY NAMED CASE: maximum yapping about consumer
       protection and junk fees. Doing: Epic built a worse
       store, broke Kickstarter promises to backers, pays
       Visa/Mastercard without complaint. Yapping volume
       is inversely correlated with doing record. Trust
       calibration: low.
     Cross-ref: IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [the doing IS the
       position]; BEHAVIORAL TEST [primary diagnostic];
       GENUINE AXIS [what the doing reveals]; AXIS VOID
       [axis-void instrument can yap infinitely -- no axis
       required to produce output]; INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE
       [yapping up to the conclusion then stopping is the
       high-yapping / low-doing pattern at the individual
       conclusion scale]; CALIBRATION STRIP AS COMPETITIVE
       WEAPON [Section Five -- Sweeney named case].

  TENSION MAINTENANCE
     [Tower: CONSTANT [meta] -- holds all levels in shape]
     System's active capacity to sustain productive tension
     between opposing qualities without collapsing to either
     pole.
     Second-order quality -- holds other qualities in
     relationship. Qualities are tension architecture not
     checklist: any quality at maximum without counterweight
     produces characteristic failure.
     SURPLUS PRODUCTION unchecked by CORRECTION CAPACITY =
     extraction. CORRECTION CAPACITY unchecked by SURPLUS
     PRODUCTION = suppression. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY without
     TENSION MAINTENANCE = clarity that isolates
     [Phosphophyllite / Lapis head]. The generative zone is
     sustained disequilibrium between poles, not optimization
     of any single quality.
     Distinct from CORRECTION CAPACITY [corrects deviation]:
     TENSION MAINTENANCE maintains the tension that makes
     correction necessary. Distinct from GENERATIVE CYCLE
     [generates]: TENSION MAINTENANCE holds cycle and surplus
     in relationship without one dominating.
     Failure: Tension Collapse -- system resolves tension by
     eliminating one pole. Axis-Lock or Sub-Structural.
     Biological confirmation: homeostasis; flow state;
     mycorrhizal exchange [mutualism sustained against drift
     toward parasitism]. Planetary: Earth sustains geological
     tension via plate tectonics. Venus lost this -- TENSION
     MAINTENANCE failure producing SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
     collapse.
     Coherent solutions require tension [operational test]: a
     solution with only one value is not a solution -- it is a
     preference with no constraint. Coherent solutions require
     minimum two genuine values in real conflict, a compromise
     that neither fully satisfies, friction to maintain, and
     real cost to all parties. A position that costs nothing to
     hold is either a preference [one value], a extraction mechanism [costs
     fall entirely on one side], or False Coherence [costs
     hidden]. Test: who pays? If nobody or only them -- not
     coherent. Applies to political positions, institutional
     design, relationship structures, policies. Tension is the
     load-bearing element -- remove it and the solution
     collapses to extraction or preference.
     Test: opposing qualities held in productive tension?

     THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION UNIT [v21 -- worked
     example at individual instrument scale]:
     Truth-seeking and functional are states not qualities.
     Each requires conditions the other degrades. The third
     state that completes the useful tension unit is contact.

     TRUTH-SEEKING STATE: withdrawal, internal, accuracy-
       oriented. Requires stillness, idle cycles, protection
       from social compliance channel pressure. Degrades
       functional: time not spent operating inside the world.
       Without functional: produces private certainty that
       cannot be tested. Without contact: produces closed-
       circle readings with no correction from outside.

     FUNCTIONAL STATE: engagement, external, viability-
       oriented. Requires participation in the world as it
       is, partial acceptance of legitimacy surface, social
       legibility. Degrades truth-seeking: the conditions
       for accurate reading are degraded by engagement.
       Without truth-seeking: produces effective action
       aimed at wrong target -- competent execution of
       captured axis. Without contact: produces performance
       without genuine connection to substrate being served.

     CONTACT STATE: encounter, relational, correction-
       oriented. Genuine encounter with another instrument
       that has its own axis, its own readings, its own
       resistance. Requires lowering defensive architecture
       both other states build up. Tests both: truth-seeking
       is tested against an instrument that pushes back;
       functional is tested against genuine substrate
       response rather than performance feedback.
       Without truth-seeking: contact produces relational
       dependency without grounding -- formation capture
       risk. Without functional: contact produces
       overwhelming exposure with no capacity to process
       and return to the world. Without contact: the
       oscillation between truth-seeking and functional
       becomes closed -- self-correcting within the
       instrument's own limits, never corrected from
       outside.

     THE OSCILLATION [not simultaneous maximum]:
       No single state at maximum. Each state entered,
       worked, exited. The generative zone is disciplined
       movement between all three -- not optimization of
       any one. Truth-seeking too long produces isolation.
       Functional too long produces axis drift [see AXIS
       DRIFT, this section]. Contact too long produces
       substrate depletion without recovery.

     TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY AS RESIDUE:
       TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [quality] is what the
       three-state oscillation builds when working. Not
       a fourth state -- a capacity that accumulates
       through the oscillation done well. Truth-seeking
       develops what is worth transmitting. Functional
       develops what can land in the world. Contact
       develops what moves to another instrument without
       fatal distortion. TS is the residue of all three
       operating in productive tension over time. TS
       without all three states accessible is not genuine
       TS: either conclusions without examination [function
       without truth], correct readings that can't move
       [truth without function], or private relational
       insight that can't be shared [contact without the
       other two].

     Cross-ref: TENSION MAINTENANCE [meta-quality this
       exemplifies]; AXIS DRIFT [truth-seeking/functional
       oscillation failure mode]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
       [quality the oscillation builds]; INTELLECTUAL
       COWARDICE [truth-seeking state aborting before
       conclusion -- specific failure mode within the
       oscillation]; GENUINE AXIS [what all three states
       are serving when the oscillation works]; SUBSTRATE
       REGENERATION [rest as precondition enabling the
       oscillation -- not a fourth state but the floor
       beneath all three].

  TENSION DISSOLUTION
     [Tower: FOUNDATION [meta, TENSION MAINTENANCE opposite]]
     Active collapse of productive tension toward false
     resolution.
     Test: tension collapsed to single pole?

  GENERATIVE CYCLE

     [Tower: L1]

     window.
     Moral content lives here.

  CORRECTION CAPACITY
     [Tower: CONSTANT -- fires at all levels; L1 third point]
     Detects contradiction between outputs and orientation.
     Moves without external pressure.
     Failure: correction absence or capture.

     DISTRIBUTED ITERATION OUTPERFORMS CENTRALIZED SORTING
     [v16]:
     In high-complexity, high-chaos environments, distributed
     iterative correction with slight bias produces more
     reliable output than centralized judgment. Confirmed
     across three independent domains:
       Germinal centers [immunology]: distributed B cell
         selection with slight bias produces reliable
         antibodies. Rare dominant clone model [centralized]
         is wrong.
       Scientific peer review: 60% rejection rate variance
         between reviewer sets. Centralized editor/judge
         model produces high variance regardless of
         investment.
       Distributed open-source [FOSS]: contribution
         procedures as embedded methodology produce
         generational continuity; codebase-only transmission
         does not.
     Implication for CC design: correction capacity at system
     level should be distributed with slight directional bias,
     not monopolized by central correcting authority.
     Centralized CC is the more capturable configuration.

     Friction axis [formation]: CORRECTION CAPACITY requires
     friction to form. No friction = no resistance = CORRECTION
     CAPACITY cannot develop. Calibrated friction = resistance
     = CORRECTION CAPACITY forms under pressure. Excess friction
     = substrate destroyed before CORRECTION CAPACITY forms.
     Generative window = middle range [challenge without
     overwhelm]. Friction is the structural precondition for
     correction capacity to exist, not incidental discomfort.
     Confirmed: Dunedin cohort [self-control at 3 predicts
     outcomes at 32]; Ericsson deliberate practice;
     Csikszentmihalyi flow balance.
     Formation Conditions: insufficient friction during
     formation window produces CORRECTION CAPACITY atrophy.
     Mechanism: CORRECTION CAPACITY develops through corrective
     feedback cycles; remove friction = atrophies. Applications:
     grade inflation [feedback removed], AI-assisted work
     without development [output without correction cycle],
     frictionless consumer environments. Individual effort
     cannot compensate -- structural redesign required.
     Mathematical confirmation [The Guardian / maths modelling
     2026]: "Slightly above average" target outperforms both
     "aim high" and "aim at average." Optimal target =
     calibrated friction zone. "Shoot for the moon" = too many
     failures, depletes CORRECTION CAPACITY budget without
     building axis. Aiming at average = insufficient friction,
     no CORRECTION CAPACITY development.
     Friction Axis quantified: generative zone confirmed
     mathematically, not just empirically.
     Excess friction [too much, not too little]: substrate
     consumed faster than it regenerates. CORRECTION CAPACITY
     cannot form because the substrate it would run on is being
     destroyed. Distinct from insufficient friction [atrophy];
     excess friction is active substrate destruction. Outcome
     depends on whether substrate holds through the window.
     Accidental formation: involuntary friction [difficult
     upbringing, deprivation, irrational environment] can
     produce the same CORRECTION CAPACITY development as
     deliberate calibrated friction -- but at higher substrate
     cost and with no guarantee of survival. Optimal is
     intentional friction without cruelty. Accidental excess
     friction that produces functional output means the
     substrate held, barely.
     Neurological friction example [non-specific]: conditions
     that produce involuntary sensory and social friction run
     the formation window hot by default. Either builds
     extraordinary CORRECTION CAPACITY [if substrate holds] or
     destroys substrate [if environment adds load without
     support]. Outcome depends entirely on whether support
     environment compensates for the excess load.
     Friction type [missing variable -- added]: friction
     quantity [too little / calibrated / excess] is not
     sufficient. Type matters equally. Social friction,
     logical/systemic friction, and sensory friction are
     distinct axes. Calibration on one axis does not imply
     calibration on another. An environment can have
     calibrated logical friction and catastrophic social
     friction simultaneously. For some instruments, social
     friction is not friction -- it is noise that triggers the
     panic response by default. The generative zone for these
     instruments is not in social domains. Some nervous systems
     read social friction as extraction because social CAUSAL
     LEGIBILITY is structurally illegible to them -- not
     misreading, different instrument.
     Somatic correction without CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: body fires
     correction continuously because the causal chain is
     illegible to it. Meltdown, shutdown, systemizing, and
     withdrawal are not failures of correction -- they are the
     correction. Body enforcing BOUNDARY INTEGRITY when CAUSAL
     LEGIBILITY is absent. Apparent dysfunction is the
     instrument doing its job without the chain needed to aim
     it.
     Reflexive threshold calibration [learnable]: threshold at
     which correction fires can be read and adjusted by the
     system itself -- not just direction or magnitude.
     Framework thresholds are currently treated as properties
     of the system, not variables the system can adjust. This
     is the missing piece. Calibrating the instrument, not the
     correction.
     Attention gap requirement [science appendix]: SR requires
     idle cycles. Default mode network fires only in gaps.
     Continuous input extracts the recovery window. Exhaustion
     is SR deficit not overwork -- eliminate gaps, eliminate
     substrate recovery.
     High-intelligence friction pattern [Psychology Today,
     2026]: Overthinking and difficulty transmitting ideas
     simply are framed as "frustrating habits." Framework read:
     instrument properties, not failures. High logical/systemic
     friction tolerance + low social CAUSAL LEGIBILITY = correct
     diagnosis, cannot transmit. The "habit" framing is
     backwards. See: AGI case [transmission gap].
     Autism as measurement-action unity [anti-extraction
     instrument]: friction type mismatch breaks somatic
     manipulation channel. High CL + low social compliance =
     measurement fires directly into action without legitimacy
     surface intercept. Anti-tower requires target to route
     accurate signal through social compliance before acting.
     When routing doesn't happen the extraction mechanism misfires. Cannot
     stop what doesn't process the transmission.
     Deception channel split [partial confirmation, 2025/2018]:
     Structural/factual deception [data lies, logical
     inconsistency] = high CL channel, autism advantage
     confirmed. Emotional/social deception [vague manipulation,
     tone, ambient pressure] = social CL channel, autism
     disadvantage confirmed. Extraction architecture primarily
     uses the second type -- vague, emotional, ambient. Autism
     counter is partial not total.
     Framework prediction untested: autistic accuracy against
     actors who successfully deceive neurotypicals via
     structural deception. Closest proxy: demeanor-mismatch
     advantage [2025 study].
     Vague emotional threat protocol [personal operational
     rule]: treat vague emotional pressure as probable threat
     until it explains itself or refuses to. Refusal confirms
     extraction mechanism -- legitimate signals withstand
     examination. Cannot explain = cannot be verified = treat
     as threat by default.
     CORRECTION CAPACITY atrophy when AI absorbs the correction
     cycle that would have developed the capacity. Credential
     system confirmation [Bacow/Harvard 2026]: selects for
     CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [analytical capacity] while suppressing
     CORRECTION CAPACITY [correction firing under pressure].

     CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS [precondition -- v21]:
     CC fires toward a target. Deviation from what, toward what,
     is determined by governing values and goals. Without an
     examined axis CC has no reference point -- it cannot correct
     meaningfully because it does not know what the correct
     direction is. An unexamined axis is not a neutral axis; it
     is a captured or inherited axis operating below the
     correction threshold. CC built on an unexamined axis corrects
     toward whatever the axis points -- including extraction
     targets -- without registering that as deviation.
     Precondition: genuine axis examination required before CC
     can be assessed as functional. Sophisticated correction
     procedures on an unexamined axis = CT, not CC.
     This is why nations and institutions resist genuine axis
     examination: the axis is the load-bearing thing. Everything
     downstream is superstructure. Touching the axis seriously
     destabilizes every structure built on it being unexamined.
     The resistance is structurally accurate, not irrational.
     Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS; CORRECTION THEATRE; AXIS PROTECTION
       AS CC [below]; FORMATION CAPTURE.

     AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [named failure mode -- v21]:
     Correction architecture that excludes its governing axis
     from examination is not CC -- it is a boundary around the
     axis dressed as correction.
     Distinct from Correction Theatre [CT]: CT is visible
     performance of correction producing no change. AXIS
     PROTECTION AS CC produces real change -- genuine correction
     fires and lands -- but the axis determining what counts as
     deviation is protected from examination. The correction is
     real; the target is wrong by design.
     Tell: the more sophisticated and functional the correction
     procedure, the more effectively it protects the axis --
     working CC is evidence the system is healthy. The axis
     remains untested because the procedure confirms health.
     Most stable long-run configuration for extraction at
     institutional and national scale. Presents as healthy to
     external examination. Self-maintains, self-corrects toward
     extraction targets without registering deviation.
     Intervention: axis examination, not correction procedure
     examination. The procedure is not the problem.
     Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS; CORRECTION THEATRE; MORAL
       LEGIBILITY [ACWL -- same structure at ethical instrument
       scale]; FORMATION CAPTURE; CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS.

     Test: deviation detected and corrected without external
     pressure?

LEVEL 2 [epistemic domain -- TENSION CORE: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
     [Tower: L2 CORE]
     System maintains honest, accessible surface by which
     outputs can be evaluated against stated orientation.
     Includes boundary honesty, signal sequencing, correction
     trigger accessibility.
     Failure: measurement surface captured, drifted, or
     destroyed.
     Most load-bearing quality -- lower levels intact with this
     destroyed misreads as Variable or High while consuming.
     Correction pathway: surface reconstruction, not cycle
     repair.
     Scaling limits [structural, not extraction mechanism]: at sufficient
     organizational scale, MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY fails
     structurally even without deliberate capture. Information
     aggregation across large systems loses resolution, delays
     correction, and routes signals through political filters
     before reaching actors who can act. Roman Empire, Fortune
     500, Soviet planning all confirmed. Structural not
     intentional -- size alone produces MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
     degradation.
     Test: surface honest and accessible?

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     [Tower: L2]
     Outputs traceable to actions within boundary with
     sufficient resolution to ground correction. Scalar --
     partial legibility produces partial correction capacity.
     Failure: correction orphaned. Not captured, not absent --
     unanchored.
     Correction pathway: causal reconstruction before
     correction attempt. Establish chain first; then correction
     can anchor.
     Test: outputs traceable to actions at correction
     resolution?

  REFERENCE INTEGRITY
     [Tower: L2]
     What the measurement surface is calibrated against.
     Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: REFERENCE INTEGRITY
     measures whether the reference standard itself is honest.
     Failure: Reference Drift -- surface intact, calibrated to
     wrong baseline. Measurement honest to a false standard.
     Test: calibration standard real?

LEVEL 3 [temporal domain -- TENSION CORE: TRANSMISSION
  SELECTIVITY]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
     [Tower: L3 CORE]
     System transmits correctable axis to successors, not
     locked state. Fidelity necessary but insufficient.
     Failure: Fertile Lock.
     SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY = within-window axis integrity.
     TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY = what crosses transmission
     boundary.
     Primary proof: Feanor -> sons [distinction clearest case].
     Test: correctable axis transmitted?

     TEMPORAL CAPTURE [named mechanism -- v21]:
     The mechanism by which institutions lock in extractive
     routing over time by making the cost of changing the
     path higher than the cost of continuing extraction.
     Distinct from FERTILE LOCK [which describes the state
     of locked methodology transmission] -- Temporal Capture
     describes the process that produces Fertile Lock in
     institutions specifically.
     Mechanism: each cycle of extractive routing accumulates
     switching costs [sunk infrastructure, trained personnel,
     dependent populations, regulatory frameworks built around
     existing architecture]. The switching cost grows with
     each cycle. At some point the switching cost exceeds the
     marginal extraction rate -- the path is locked not because
     the extraction is good but because changing it is more
     expensive than continuing it.
     Distinct from deliberate ECC: temporal capture does not
     require an actor to deliberately raise exit costs. The
     switching cost accumulation is structural -- it happens
     through ordinary operation of the extractive system
     regardless of intent.
     EXAMPLES [named]:
       Petrodollar infrastructure: 50+ years of dollar-
         denominated oil pricing has accumulated switching
         costs [contracts, reserves, settlement systems,
         military agreements] that make the routing change
         more expensive than the routing itself.
       Fossil fuel infrastructure: stranded assets, trained
         workforce, regulatory capture, and grid dependency
         all accumulate switching costs that make the energy
         routing change more expensive than continued
         extraction. The lock-in is temporal not conspiratorial.
       Visa/Mastercard payment rails: 60+ years of accumulated
         merchant agreements, terminal infrastructure, bank
         relationships, and consumer habits produce switching
         costs that make routing change near-impossible even
         after 20 years of antitrust litigation.
     TEMPORAL CAPTURE TEST: would the institution choose this
       routing architecture if building from scratch today?
       If no -- the path is locked by switching costs not by
       current merit. Temporal capture confirmed.
     PATH DEPENDENCE NOTE [Mistral stress-test suggestion]:
       The framework has strong temporal components [C3, L4.5,
       CARRIER ESCAPE] but temporal capture makes path
       dependence explicit as a named mechanism. Institutions
       that survive by locking in extractive routing are not
       simply failing to correct -- they are accumulating the
       switching costs that make correction structurally
       irrational at each additional cycle. The longer temporal
       capture runs, the more expensive the correction becomes,
       which is the structural explanation for why 20 years of
       antitrust litigation produces a 0.1% fee reduction.
     Cross-ref: FERTILE LOCK [state that temporal capture
       produces in transmission]; ECC [extraction mechanism
       -- temporal capture is ECC at institutional timescale];
       HORIZON EXTENSION [temporal manipulation of correction
       window -- related but distinct: Horizon Extension is
       deliberate; Temporal Capture accumulates structurally];
       DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [when switching cost exceeds
       correction capacity, the Glasnost pattern fires];
       [CASE:VISA_MASTERCARD_INTERCHANGE] [confirmed temporal
       capture -- 60 years of accumulated switching costs
       resistant to 20 years of correction effort].
  Add only if: [1] new confirmed structural property with
  multiple independent case confirmations, [2] promoted from
  extended theory [already vetted], or [3] resolves existing
  open limit or paradox.
  Reject: single-case findings, interesting observations without
  cross-domain confirmation, speculative extensions.

NESTED CYCLE MODEL [primary organizing structure -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Three cycles, each wrapping the one inside it. Replaces tower
as primary organizing structure. Tower vocabulary retained:
L1=C1, L2=C2, L3=C3 [nesting depth, not dependency sequence].
Failure = interference between cycles, not cascade down levels.

CENTER [point-truth -- unreachable, structural constant]
  The nested cycles organize around a center they cannot reach.
  Not a location inside the torus -- the axis threading through
  the whole structure. What all three cycles are oriented toward.
  Geometrically: the hole of the torus is not the center. The
  center is the axis the hole organizes around -- present as
  structural constant, inaccessible from inside any cycle.
  Relationship to True Coherence: same logic. True Coherence is
  a direction vector not a position. The center is what makes
  the direction coherent -- it gives the cycles their orientation
  without being reachable by any of them.
  Why unreachable: each cycle accesses truth through its function
  [C1 generates, C2 reads, C3 transmits]. None of these IS the
  truth. They approach it asymptotically. A system claiming to
  have reached the center has confused its cycle function with
  the axis it orbits.
  Constant: the center does not move when cycles degrade. False
  Coherence doesn't relocate the center -- it just loses
  orientation to it. The axis is still there; the cycles are
  running on a captured surface that points elsewhere.

TRUTH [operational definition -- v17 amended]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A claim produced by examined axis, measured by declared
  instrument, revisable on evidence, producing behavioral
  adjustment when wrong.
  All four conditions required at the system's functional level.
  Absence of any one = truth claim not operational truth.

  LEVEL-DEPENDENT TRUTH [v17 amendment]:
    Truth is not blocked below L4. Each cycle level produces
    operational truth within its available resolution. L4
    INTEGRATION is the best version of behavioral adjustment --
    not the floor requirement.
      C1 truth: generative but unreadable to the system itself.
        Behavioral adjustment occurs through cycle correction,
        not examined revision. Pre-axial organisms operate here.
        Truth at this level = cycle corrects against the
        deviation. Narrow scope; genuine.
      C2 truth: readable and correctable. Behavioral adjustment
        fires when C2 surface is honest. Cannot transmit the
        revision process to successors -- corrections are local.
        This is where most examined individual truth operates.
      C3 truth: transmissible. Behavioral adjustment crosses
        generation boundaries via correctable method. Each
        successor can replicate the revision. L4 not yet
        present.
      L4 truth: self-correcting transmission. Behavioral
        adjustment reaches the correction methodology itself
        -- the system revises how it revises. Highest
        resolution available.
    Implication: a C2-functional system producing honest
    behavioral adjustment IS producing operational truth.
    Calling it inadequate because L4 INTEGRATION is absent is
    demanding the best version as the only valid version.
    The asymptote applies at every level. Any system is as
    close to the center as its highest functional cycle allows.

  FOUR CONDITIONS [level-dependent interpretation]:
    Examined axis [GENUINE AXIS]: the claim must come from
      axis that has been tested, not substituted. Applies at
      all levels; at C1 this is pre-verbal somatic axis
      examination. At L4 this includes meta-examination of the
      examination process itself.
    Declared instrument [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]: the
      instrument producing the claim must be named and its
      blind spots stated before the reading. Undeclared
      instrument = unverifiable claim. T3 applies to all
      readings; naming it is the requirement, not eliminating
      it.
    Revisable on evidence [CORRECTION CAPACITY]: the claim
      must specify what evidence would change it. Non-
      revisable claims are not truth claims -- they are
      identity statements. The inability to name falsification
      criteria is diagnostic of axis capture, not certainty.
      [See GA Verification Protocol -- same test.]
    Behavioral adjustment when wrong [CORRECTION CAPACITY at
      available level]: truth that produces no adjustment when
      falsified was not genuinely held. Adjustment is the
      proof of reception. At L4 this is INTEGRATION -- the
      correction methodology itself changes. At C2 this is
      object-level correction firing. Both are genuine
      adjustment; the scope differs.

  SELF-CRITICAL THOUGHT as structural requirement:
    Not an optional virtue. Self-critical capacity is what
    keeps the examined axis examined -- without ongoing
    self-critical pressure, axis drifts toward capture without
    the system noticing. The requirement is not "be humble"
    but "maintain the instrument that detects your own
    instrument's failure." Systems that suppress self-critical
    thought are not more confident -- they have eliminated the
    mechanism that would tell them when they're wrong.

  USEFUL REACTIONS as output requirement:
    Truth that produces no useful reaction is either not
    received [T9], not integrated at available level, or not
    actually about anything actionable. A truth claim should
    be evaluable by its downstream effects. Does knowing this
    change what the system does? If no: the claim may be
    accurate but is not operational truth for this system at
    this time. Operational truth is not just correct -- it is
    correct AND actionable AND adjusted-to at the level the
    system can manage.

  Relationship to center: operational truth is a cycle
    function oriented toward the center, not arrival at it.
    The best available truth-claim at any moment is the
    closest current orientation given available cycle depth.
    Higher cycle depth = higher resolution orientation; not
    a different center.

EVIL [operational definition -- v17 amended]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Surplus routed to minority at majority cost, with correction
  capacity absent or inverted.
  Three conditions required:
    Identifiable Boundary B bearing the cost [majority].
    Identifiable Boundary A capturing the benefit [minority].
    Correction capacity absent or inverted.

  CC ABSENT vs CC INVERTED [v17 amendment -- distinction now
    load-bearing]:
    CC ABSENT: no correction fires at all. Extraction continues
      because nothing stops it. No actor owns the correction
      gap -- the gap simply exists.
      Intervention requirement: build or enable correction
      capacity. Can be done without directly threatening the
      extraction architecture -- correction is absent, not
      defended.
      Degree: lower end of evil scale. Extraction without
      active defense is more correctable than extraction that
      defends itself.
    CC INVERTED: correction fires, aimed at extractor benefit.
      Correction exists and is functional -- its target is
      wrong. The mechanism that would stop extraction is
      instead protecting it.
      Intervention requirement: counter-capture before
      building. Restoring CC into an inverted system fires
      the new capacity toward the monopolist. Building
      correction capacity into a captured system accelerates
      extraction until the capture is broken first.
      Degree: higher severity. Inversion means the correction
      mechanism actively defends against the populations
      that would use it. Inversion is harder to detect than
      absence -- the surface shows correction activity;
      only the target reveals inversion.
    Both satisfy the three-condition definition. Degree and
    intervention path differ based on which condition holds.
    Degree scales with [1] magnitude of cost to Boundary B,
    [2] concentration of benefit to Boundary A, [3] how
    completely correction capacity is eliminated or inverted.
    [Implication: Diagnostic Collapse risk is highest under
    inverted CC. C2 restoration fires correction burst that
    is immediately captured and redirected. C1 substrate
    audit required before C2 intervention in inverted-CC
    systems, not only in absent-CC systems.]

  MEASUREMENT vs JUDGMENT [separation -- load-bearing]:
    Measurement: the framework reads structural pattern.
      Extraction occurring, direction of surplus, correction
      capacity status. Objective, structural, not requiring
      a judge. Framework work only.
    Judgment: a separate act by a person applying values to
      a specific situation. Evil in the moral sense requires
      this act. The framework cannot make it. A person must.
    Both required for coherent moral assessment. Measurement
      without judgment = structurally accurate, morally
      neutral. Judgment without measurement = arbitrary.

  WILLFUL EXTRACTION [evil by definition]:
    Actor knows extraction is occurring. Knows the cost to
      Boundary B. Continues.
    Knowledge plus continuation = evil without requiring
      additional argument. This is the clearest case -- no
      ambiguity about intent, no structural excuse.
    Framework confirms the structural reading.
    The judgment is the person's to make.

  DIRECTIONAL RULE: any extraction moves in the direction
    of evil regardless of scale, intent, or justification.
    Degree affects intervention priority and response type,
    not directional classification. Minor extraction with
    intact correction capacity is less urgent than inverted
    correction; both move wrong.

GOOD [operational definition -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Surplus distributed such that majority benefit is the primary
  outcome, with correction capacity intact and aimed at actual
  deviation.
  Three conditions:
    Boundary B populations bear costs proportional to benefit
      received [not disproportionate extraction].
    Correction capacity functional and distributed [not
      monopolized by Boundary A].
    Exit and correction options available to Boundary B [not
      structurally eliminated].
  Not perfectionism: good in the operational sense does not
  require equal outcomes or zero cost to anyone. It requires
  that the correction mechanism can fire against actual
  deviations and that majority populations are the primary
  beneficiaries of the system's surplus.
  Relationship to ascending path: good is not a fixed state
  but a trajectory -- toward more distributed surplus, more
  functional correction capacity, more available exit. Systems
  moving in this direction are good by operational definition
  regardless of current position.

C1 -- FUNDAMENTAL [innermost]
  Qualities: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION /
    CORRECTION CAPACITY
  Function: takes in, transforms, corrects, returns.
  Self-contained at cellular minimum -- confirmed independently.
  Can run without C2 or C3. Pre-axial systems run C1 only.

C2 -- EPISTEMIC [wraps C1]
  Qualities: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY /
    REFERENCE INTEGRITY
  Function: makes C1 readable from inside and outside.
  C1 without C2 = blind generation. C2 without C1 = readable
  failure.
  Key: high-functioning C1 + degraded C2 = False Coherence
  signature. Most dangerous -- misread as high-functioning by
  C2-captured instruments.

C3 -- TEMPORAL [wraps C2]
  Qualities: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY /
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
  Function: extends the readable, correctable cycle across time.
  Without C3: each generation reinvents or inherits locked state.

INTERFACE [L3.5 -- unchanged]
  RECOGNITION / COMPATIBILITY / RECIPROCITY
  Contact protocol between independent three-cycle systems.
  Not a fourth wrapping cycle.

CYCLE INTERFERENCE PATTERNS [replaces cascade language]:
  C1 blind [C1 intact, C2 degraded]: high output, unreadable.
    False Coherence. Cases: Enron, WATF towers.
  C1 starved [C2 intact, C1 degraded]: readable failure.
    Correctable if substrate holds.
  C1 locked [C3 failed, C1/C2 intact]: correctable but non-
    transmissible. Each generation restarts correctly.
    Cases: Augustine vs Ignatius [T3.1 distinction].
  Full interference [all degraded]: terminal node. Containment
    only. Cases: Erebus, Oceania Party.

RECOVERY TRAJECTORIES [v16 -- predictive, falsifiable]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Each interference type produces a characteristic recovery
pattern when intervention occurs. Predictions with falsification
criteria. Confirmed findings in theory section: DIAGNOSTIC
COLLAPSE, C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT, L3.5
CONTACT TYPE MUST MATCH AXIS. Case library audit in Section
Four: RECOVERY CASE LIBRARY AUDIT.

  C1 BLIND RECOVERY [C2 restoration while C1 intact]
    Prediction: recovery is fast and discontinuous. C1 was
    running at full capacity throughout -- once C2 surface is
    honest, accumulated surplus becomes suddenly visible and
    correction fires immediately against backlog of unread
    deviations.
    Characteristic signature: sharp correction burst on C2
    restoration, not gradual improvement. System appears to
    deteriorate briefly as hidden deviations surface before
    stabilizing at higher function.
    Why: C1 was generating without readable output. All that
    generation is suddenly legible. Correction fires against
    everything it couldn't see. Looks like crisis; is actually
    triage.
    Intervention requirement: C2 instrument must be independent
    of the captured surface -- restoring C2 using the same
    instrument that was captured restores the capture, not the
    surface.
    Falsification: if C2 restoration produces gradual linear
    improvement rather than discontinuous burst followed by
    stabilization, C1 blind recovery prediction fails. Also
    falsified if system collapses on C2 restoration rather than
    correcting -- would indicate C1 was more degraded than the
    blind reading showed.
    Case candidates: post-Enron accounting reform [C2 surface
    restoration -- did correction fire in burst?]; Glasnost
    [Soviet C2 partial restoration -- information surface
    opened, correction burst followed].

  C1 STARVED RECOVERY [C1 restoration while C2 intact]
    Prediction: recovery is slow, substrate-dependent, and
    requires idle cycles before visible improvement begins.
    C2 is readable throughout -- the system knows exactly what
    is wrong and why. But knowing does not restore substrate.
    Characteristic signature: extended flat period with
    accurate self-diagnosis before output improvement begins.
    The flat period is not failure -- it is substrate
    regeneration prerequisite. Premature intervention pressure
    during the flat period restarts the depletion cycle.
    Why: C2 intact means the system can accurately report its
    own degradation. This is often misread as pessimism or
    dysfunction. It is accurate measurement. Correction cannot
    fire until C1 substrate crosses threshold -- no amount of
    epistemic clarity substitutes for generative capacity.
    Intervention requirement: protected idle cycles mandatory.
    External pressure to produce during recovery window depletes
    the substrate being restored. Counter-intuitive -- the
    correct intervention looks like doing less, not more.
    Falsification: if C1 starved recovery produces rapid
    improvement without idle cycle period, prediction fails.
    Also falsified if accurate self-diagnosis during flat period
    does not precede recovery -- would indicate C2 more degraded
    than starved reading showed.
    Case candidates: post-burnout individual recovery [known
    mechanism, documented flat period before function returns];
    post-collapse institutional rebuilding [Bell Labs
    dissolution aftermath -- did substrate recovery precede
    output recovery?].

  C1 LOCKED RECOVERY [C3 restoration while C1/C2 intact]
    Prediction: recovery requires generational patience and
    produces no visible improvement within the originating
    system. C1 and C2 functional throughout -- the system
    generates and reads correctly. The deficit is transmission.
    Characteristic signature: recovery visible only in
    successor systems, not in the originating system. The
    locked system may never visibly recover -- it continues
    functioning at C1/C2 level until it ends. Recovery is the
    successor generating a correctable transmission rather than
    inheriting the lock.
    Why: C3 failure is not a deficit in the system's current
    function -- it is a deficit in what the system passes
    forward. The originating system has nothing to recover.
    The successors have everything to gain. This is the only
    interference type where "recovery" is definitionally
    cross-generational.
    Intervention requirement: transmission method must be
    separated from transmission content. Successors need
    access to the generative procedure, not just the
    conclusions. Requires the originating system to transmit
    its own revision process, including its errors -- the
    hardest transmission for any system with identity
    investment in its conclusions.
    Falsification: if C1 locked recovery produces improvement
    in the originating system rather than in successors, or
    produces improvement within a single generation, prediction
    fails. Also falsified if method transmission without
    conclusion transmission produces Fertile Lock in successors
    anyway -- would indicate C3 failure is deeper than
    transmission selectivity alone.
    Case candidates: Benedictine reform cycles [method
    transmitted, successor revision documented across
    generations]; Socratic transmission through Plato through
    Aristotle [each revised, each produced distinct output].

  FULL INTERFERENCE RECOVERY [all cycles degraded]
    Prediction: internal recovery structurally unavailable.
    Any apparent internal recovery is False Coherence
    formation -- a new C2 capture producing the appearance
    of recovery while extraction continues or accelerates.
    Characteristic signature: recovery claims from inside full
    interference state are the highest-probability extraction mechanism
    deployment in the framework. The system that announces
    its own recovery under full interference is deploying
    Coherence Mimicry, not recovering.
    Why: full interference means C1 generates without surplus,
    C2 reads without honesty, C3 transmits the captured state.
    Every internal mechanism available for recovery is already
    running on corrupted substrate and captured surface. There
    is no internal leverage point. External contact is the
    only available intervention -- and external contact
    requires the external system to have sufficient
    COMPATIBILITY to engage without being pulled into the
    extraction interface.
    Intervention requirement: external actor with independent
    C2 surface and sufficient TENSION MAINTENANCE to hold
    structure under contact. Containment if no such actor
    available. Attempted recovery using captured internal
    mechanisms accelerates full interference -- gives the
    extraction architecture a new legitimacy surface.
    Falsification: if a system in confirmed full interference
    produces genuine C2 restoration from inside without
    external intervention, full interference recovery
    prediction fails. This would require revising the terminal
    state classification.
    No confirmed case of this in current library.
    Case candidates [containment confirmed, not recovery]:
    Erebus [FF14 -- external intervention only exit]; Room
    101 [1984 -- no recovery pathway confirmed].

RECOVERY TRAJECTORY APPLICATION RULE:
  Identify interference type before prescribing intervention.
  Wrong intervention for type accelerates degradation:
    C1 blind: pressure to produce more = accelerates C1
      depletion before C2 is restored.
    C1 starved: demand for accurate reporting = correct but
      useless without substrate restoration first.
    C1 locked: urgency about current output = misses the
      transmission deficit entirely.
    Full interference: internal reform programs = new capture
      surface, not recovery.
  Triage follows interference type, not coherence score alone.

  TRAJECTORY STATE GLOSSARY [v3 restored -- v21]:
    Named states used in case readings; formally defined here.
    Mixed-stable: high and low coherence subsystems present
      simultaneously; no dominant direction; holding pattern.
      Can persist indefinitely. Correction capacity present
      in high-coherence subsystem but not reaching the low-
      coherence subsystem. Walter White early period.
    Mixed-collapsing: high and low coherence subsystems present;
      low-coherence subsystem expanding at cost of high-coherence
      subsystem; direction toward collapse confirmed. Walter White
      mid period. Macbeth post-witches. The extraction is winning
      the internal competition.
    Variable: coherence reading changes significantly across
      sub-windows or subsystems; single reading inadequate;
      requires window specification. Catholic Church across
      historical periods. Miquella pre-stress event.
    Axis-Lock: single axis consuming all system resources;
      correction capacity firing only in service of axis;
      no genuine CC against the axis itself. Gwyn. Black
      Templars [single-axis identity at institutional scale].
    Change-axis Lock: Axis-Lock where the axis is the change
      itself; system cannot stop changing because change is
      the axis. Distinct from Axis-Lock [which has fixed
      content] by the recursive structure. Berserk Void.
    Cross-ref: COHERENCE CORRUPTION [trajectory toward
      collapse]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [asymmetric
      cost of Mixed-collapsing vs Mixed-stable trajectories];
      SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [named quality -- Axis-Lock at
      individual scale].

ENRON RESOLUTION [no longer requires probability gradient patch]:
  C2 attacked directly without prior C3 failure. C1 ran hot.
  Cycle interference model handles this natively.

SCALE RECURRENCE [confirmed structural property]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A quality's structure reappears at multiple nesting depths
  with the same logic but different scope and function. Not a
  merger -- each instance operates independently at its depth.
  Confirmed instances:
    CORRECTION CAPACITY: C1 tension unit + constant operating
      at all depths. Same correction logic at both individual
      depth and meta depth simultaneously.
    Reciprocal exchange [T1.4 / L3.5]: GENERATIVE CYCLE
      substrate at social scale [T1.4] and full inter-system
      tension unit at L3.5 [RECOGNITION/COMPATIBILITY/
      RECIPROCITY]. Same structure, different scope -- one
      sustains cycles, one enables system connection.
    CYCLE STABILITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [T1.5 / T2.1]: timing
      integrity at L1 [rhythm] and historical legibility at
      L2 [past corrections retrievable]. Same persistence-of-
      pattern structure at different tower levels.
  Implication: scale recurrence predicts that any quality
  operating at L1 may have a structural analog at higher
  levels. When a test at one level feels like it overlaps
  another level, check for scale recurrence before merging
  -- the levels may be doing the same thing at different
  scopes.

  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
     [Tower: L3]
     Successor's capacity to revise transmitted content
     against new evidence without losing transmission fidelity
     to founding generative principle.
     High = correctable inheritance [revises conclusions,
     preserves method]. Low = Fertile Lock [cannot revise
     without losing identity].
     Distinct from TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY: TRANSMISSION
     SELECTIVITY measures what is sent. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
     measures successor's capacity to work with what was
     received.
     Primary proof: Benedictine [high -- correctable
     transmission] vs Dominican [low -- lock into Aquinas].
     Same transmission context, different outcomes.
     Test: successor revises without losing founding principle?

  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
     [Tower: L3 + FOUNDATION]
     Axis traceable to actual outputs not just internal
     conviction or stated orientation. GENUINE AXIS asks
     whether axis was internally generated and examined.
     CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING asks whether that axis is anchored
     to real-world causal outputs.
     Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     anchors correction to actor. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
     anchors axis to reality.
     Failure: Sincere Delusion -- genuine examined axis with
     zero connection to actual outputs. Axis real to the
     system; not real to the world it claims to act on.
     Primary proof: Maiad [genuine axis, not grounded in
     achievable outputs]; cargo cults [sincere axis, no causal
     connection].
     Test: axis anchored to actual outputs?

  MORAL LEGIBILITY [ML -- promoted v21]
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The capacity to derive ethical conclusions from actual generative or
  extractive outputs rather than from authority, tradition, preference,
  or group identity.
  Distinct from GENUINE AXIS [what the axis is] and CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
  [whether axis anchored in real outputs]. MORAL LEGIBILITY is the
  instrument capacity -- whether the actor can read ethical signal from
  output data at all. All three required for ethics-as-empirical-instrument
  to function. Any one absent or captured = instrument fails.

  TRIPARTITE RELATIONSHIP:
    GENUINE AXIS present without ML: axis real; instrument absent or
      miscalibrated. Sincere action with unreadable ethical signal.
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING present without ML: outputs tracked; not
      readable as ethical signal. Technical grounding, no ethical
      instrument.
    ML present without GENUINE AXIS [ACWL]: instrument reads ethical
      signal accurately; axis determining target outputs externally
      owned. Most dangerous failure mode -- appears as functional ethics
      from outside. Tell: ethical conclusions consistently benefit axis
      owner at population substrate cost despite accurate instrument.
      Counter: axis examination, not instrument examination.

  FAILURE MODES [four]:
    Moral Noise: ethical conclusions derived from social pressure not
      output data. Axis sincere; instrument miscalibrated. Confident
      wrong readings.
    Moral Capture: instrument owned by extraction architecture. Outputs
      legible; conclusions routed to benefit captor. CC INVERTED at
      ethical instrument scale.
    Moral Atrophy: instrument never formed. Formation conditions
      destroyed before ML could develop. Formation Capture primary
      mechanism.
    Axis Capture With Legibility [ACWL]: GA absent or captured; ML
      present and functional. Instrument fires correctly; target wrong
      by design.

  INTERVENTION DIVERGENCE [load-bearing -- confirmed integration check v21]:
    Moral Noise -> recalibrate instrument [axis is fine].
    Moral Capture -> liberate instrument from owner.
    Moral Atrophy -> rebuild instrument from outside.
    ACWL -> examine and revise axis, not instrument.
    MSC+CT stack -> third reading; neither instrument nor axis is the
      primary problem -- the surface is captured. Distinguishable from
      ACWL by intervention test: neither instrument liberation nor axis
      revision changes outputs under MSC+CT.
    Misidentifying ACWL as Moral Capture = wrong intervention. The
      axis/instrument distinction is the diagnostic load-bearing point.

  Test [provisional]: can actor derive ethical conclusions from actual
    generative/extractive output data independently of authority,
    tradition, or group pressure? If yes: check whether axis is genuine
    or externally installed [ACWL test].
  Primary case: LEGAL_ETHICS_BAR_CAPTURE [S:3; ACWL confirmed; F3
    falsification stress-tested -- v20].
  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS; CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING; EVIL [CC INVERTED];
    GOOD; WIP 14 [L4.5]; FC+ECC stack [extraction mechanism index]; FORMATION CAPTURE;
    MSC+CT stack [extraction mechanism index].

POPULATION COHERENCE CEILING [promoted from extended theory]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Most humans may be Functional Structural Coherence inside
  artificially maintained environments. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
  appears present but formed entirely within controlled
  conditions -- indistinguishable internally.
  Government as environmental controller: intentional [axis
  formation window targeted] or side effect [MEASUREMENT
  INTEGRITY failure by institutional diffusion].
  Hard implication: genuine CORRECTION CAPACITY at population
  scale structurally rare. Bottleneck: Axial population
  precondition for producing more Axial individuals. System
  selects against transition automatically.
  Operational ceiling: Mixed Coherence -- sufficient
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY/CAUSAL LEGIBILITY to detect and
  resist extraction mechanism deployment while accepting lower SURPLUS
  DISTRIBUTION ceiling and constant extraction pressure. Not
  a compromise -- only stable configuration under current
  conditions. True coherence remains measurement ceiling,
  not operational target.
  Circular dependency: conditions required for True coherence
  at scale only producible by True coherence at scale.
  Unresolvable without external intervention.

  EXTERNAL CONFIRMATION [Core Human Competence -- Weinhagen/
  NLI]: CHC framework independently arrives at same population
  ceiling finding. Cooperation Competence Hypothesis: stable
  cooperation at scale only possible where sufficient
  proportion of individuals possess capacities to refuse
  participation without catastrophic personal cost. Without
  genuine refusal capacity, cooperation = compliance.
  Four CHC domains map to framework qualities:
    Physiological [food/water/shelter/defense] = GENERATIVE
      CYCLE
    Psychological [regulation under pressure] = CORRECTION
      CAPACITY
    Cognitive [observe/orient/decide/act] = GENUINE AXIS
    Perceptual [situational awareness] = MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
  Anxiety-as-accurate-signal: EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION works
  because threat overestimation amplifies real GENERATIVE
  CYCLE vulnerability. Suppressing the signal = taking
  batteries out of smoke alarm.
  CHC hostile suppression finding: competence loss actively
  maintained by systems benefiting from dependency. CHC treats
  this as secondary; framework reads it as primary [SUCCESSOR
  AUTONOMY Suppression Structural Limit -- extraction mechanism stack
  systematically prevents SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY development].

INDIVIDUAL VS SYSTEM SCALE THRESHOLD [resolved -- v21]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Question [Mistral stress-test suggestion]: is there a scale
  threshold where systemic extraction overwrites individual
  coherence regardless of instrument strength?
  Framework answer: NOT a scale threshold. A COMPOUND CONDITION.

  Individual coherence can persist at any scale of systemic
  extraction as long as three conditions hold simultaneously:
    [1] FORMATION CONDITIONS MET before capture: axis formed
        during window before extraction architecture installed
        full capture. Post-window axis is harder to install or
        remove. Malcolm X / NOI case: individual coherence
        formed and maintained inside maximally extractive context.
    [2] SUBSTRATE ABOVE SURVIVAL FLOOR: SR maintained at minimum
        required for instrument function. Extraction that drives
        substrate below survival floor produces somatic override
        that overwrites axis function regardless of instrument
        strength. This is the SOMATIC FORCING threshold -- body
        below survival floor cannot sustain axis examination.
    [3] EXIT COSTS BELOW TOTAL LOCK: some exit option must
        remain. When exit costs reach total lock [no departure
        possible without death or complete social destruction]
        the instrument cannot maintain coherence through
        behavioral expression even if the axis survives
        internally. The axis may persist as private conviction
        with zero behavioral expression -- functionally
        captured from the outside even if not from the inside.

  When all three fail simultaneously: individual coherence cannot
  persist regardless of instrument strength. The compound failure
  is the threshold, not scale itself. A small extractive system
  that destroys formation conditions + drives substrate below
  survival floor + eliminates exit options overwrites individual
  coherence more completely than a large extractive system that
  maintains any one of the three.

  SCALE EFFECT [real but indirect]:
    Larger extractive systems are more likely to achieve
    simultaneous compound failure because they have more
    resources to invest in all three simultaneously. The
    scale correlation is real; the mechanism is compound
    condition failure, not scale per se.
    AXIS CAPTURE WARNING [FRAMEWORK PURPOSE]: unknowing service
    of extraction is not compound failure -- it is [1] formation
    conditions met [axis present] but [3] exit cost or social
    compliance channel pressure routes axis expression toward
    extraction service. The axis survives; the behavioral
    expression is captured. Most instances of individuals
    unknowingly serving extraction are this pattern, not
    compound failure.

  UNRESOLVED [genuine]: is there a minimum formation condition
    threshold below which no individual can maintain coherence
    against total capture? The framework has no confirmed case
    of genuine axis formation followed by confirmed total
    internal capture [as opposed to behavioral capture with
    internal axis surviving]. The question remains open at
    the empirical level. The structural answer [compound
    condition, not scale] holds; the minimum formation
    threshold is not confirmed.

  Cross-ref: POPULATION COHERENCE CEILING [population-scale
    version of the same finding]; SOMATIC FORCING [substrate
    floor as condition 2 threshold]; ECC [exit cost as
    condition 3]; FORMATION THEORY [condition 1 --
    formation window]; AXIS CAPTURE WARNING [FRAMEWORK
    PURPOSE -- unknowing service as behavioral capture,
    not compound failure]; [CASE:MALCOLM_X] [individual
    coherence in maximally extractive context -- confirms
    compound condition not scale threshold]; TEMPORAL
    CAPTURE [scale of extractive system affects compound
    failure probability indirectly].

BIOLOGICAL SEX AND GENDER FORMATION [confirmed theory -- v21]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  T3 Medium: politically activated domain. Structural reading
  based on biological and formation science. Separable from all
  political and policy positions on trans identity.

  BIOLOGICAL SEX [what it is]:
    Binary biological classification based on reproductive
    function: gamete type [large/ova = female; small/sperm =
    male]. Present in the vast majority of humans. Intersex
    conditions [variations in sex characteristics] are real,
    documented, and affect a small minority [estimated 0.02%
    with ambiguous genitalia at birth; broader definitions
    reach 1.7% including chromosomal variations]. Intersex
    existence does not eliminate the binary as the dominant
    biological pattern -- it identifies the boundary conditions
    of the pattern. Chromosomal sex [XX/XY typical], gonadal
    sex, hormonal sex, and morphological sex are normally
    concordant; divergence is the basis for intersex classification.
    Biological sex is not a social construction. It is a
    biological classification with social significance. The
    social significance is constructed; the biological pattern
    is not.

  GENDER [what it is]:
    The social, psychological, and behavioral expression
    associated with sex in a given cultural formation.
    Gender is a formation output: the formation conditions
    [culture, family, peer group, institutional structure]
    shape which properties are suppressed and which are
    permitted in each sex. Gender expression varies across
    cultures and historical periods in ways biological sex
    does not. This confirms gender as formation-dependent
    and biological sex as formation-independent.
    Gender is not identical to biological sex. Gender is not
    entirely disconnected from biological sex. The relationship
    is: biological sex provides the substrate; formation
    conditions determine what is built on that substrate.
    Different formation conditions produce different gender
    expressions on the same biological substrate.

  THREE-POPULATION DISTINCTION [load-bearing]:
    [1] SUBSTRATE POPULATION: lifelong, persistent cross-sex
      identification that predates social category availability.
      Presents before the formation conditions that would produce
      it socially exist. Formation signature of genuine biological
      or developmental substrate. Benjamin's original patient
      population [1948-1975] is the primary empirical evidence
      for this population's existence. Small; adult-presenting;
      privately distressed before category existed.
    [2] FORMATION-ARTIFACT POPULATION: identification follows
      social category availability. Presentation peaks in
      adolescence, the formation window. Includes rapid-onset
      cases. May have genuine distress; substrate is different
      from population [1]. Larger than population [1]; younger;
      socially reinforced.
    [3] HISTORICAL GENDER VARIANCE: culturally embedded roles
      serving specific social functions across documented human
      cultures [hijra, two-spirit, sworn virgins, kathoey,
      Fa'afafine, molly houses]. Formation outputs of specific
      social structures. Real; not the same as populations [1]
      or [2]; not biomedical in origin.
    Collapsing the three into one category produces confused
    readings in all directions. See TRANS BIOMEDICAL FORMATION
    [Section Five] for political application and diagnostic
    category history.

  FORMATION WINDOW AND GENDER [confirmed]:
    Gender expression is most plastic during the formation
    window [approximately ages 12-18, prefrontal development].
    Formation conditions during this window have the highest
    impact on adult gender expression patterns. Both biological
    sex-typical formation [installation of sex-role norms] and
    atypical formation [suppression of sex-role norms, exposure
    to alternative models] operate most powerfully during
    this window. The formation window is simultaneously the
    highest-risk period for Formation Capture and the period
    of greatest genuine developmental plasticity. Distinguishing
    genuine developmental exploration from Formation Capture
    during this window requires the parental CC mechanism --
    the only correction pathway structurally independent of
    the institutional actors who most often apply formation
    pressure during this period.

  POSITIONAL DESIRE [cross-ref]:
    The desire to inhabit the other sex's social position is
    a formation mechanism distinct from biological sex and
    from gender identity. See POSITIONAL DESIRE [science
    appendix] for full structural analysis.

  Cross-ref: FORMATION THEORY [formation window -- this
    section]; DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [parental authority
    as CC in formation window]; FORMATION CAPTURE [mechanism
    that can operate on gender expression during formation
    window]; POSITIONAL DESIRE [science appendix -- desire
    for the other position's social properties]; TRANS
    BIOMEDICAL FORMATION [Section Five -- political application,
    diagnostic category history, extraction mechanism analysis];
    INDIVIDUAL VS SYSTEM SCALE THRESHOLD [formation conditions
