PREDATION / COMPETITION / EXTRACTION [fundamental distinction]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Predation: honest violence. Boundary visible. The prey
  knows the relationship. A lion kills. Does not build a
  farm, keep prey mostly alive, and call it efficient
  resource management.
  Competition: honest conflict. Boundary visible. Both
  parties know the terms. Zero-sum possible but declared.
  Extraction: trickery as foundation. Requires prey to
  misunderstand the relationship, cooperate with its own
  consumption, and call it beneficial. Measurement surface
  capture is the mechanism.
  Slightly wrong is still wrong -- smart extraction is slower
  destruction. The innovation threshold is still crossed,
  just later. Smart death is still death. The crime is not
  the degree; it is the lie that makes cooperation possible.
  Framework: predation and competition are structurally
  honest. Extraction is not. Politics at scale = extraction
  [stealing] not predation -- requires the legitimacy surface
  that predation doesn't need.
  Note [v21]: distribution strategy and extraction mechanism
  are not opposites on a generation axis -- both require
  generation to function. They are opposites on a routing
  axis. The problem is never generation itself; it is where
  surplus goes and who controls the boundary determining
  the destination. See FRAMEWORK PURPOSE [GENERATION
  CLARIFICATION].

ASYMMETRY TYPES [predatory / developmental -- promoted v21; scale -- WIP]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Surface reading identical across types: information withheld;
  receiver does not understand the full situation. Mechanism,
  intervention path, and ethical status differ. Collapse into
  single category produces wrong intervention.

  PREDATORY ASYMMETRY [confirmed -- existing framework]:
    Information withheld serves operator at receiver's expense.
    Operator benefits from ignorance persisting.
    Tell: closing the asymmetry would damage operator's position.
    Intervention: MSC counter [independent measurement].

  DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [confirmed -- promoted v21]:
    Information withheld serves receiver's eventual capacity.
    Asymmetry designed to close over time as instrument develops.
    Operator benefits from the asymmetry closing -- a developed
    receiver is the intended output, not a permanently dependent one.
    Tell: operator actively works toward asymmetry closing; full
    information is the goal, not the threat.
    Requires GENUINE AXIS in operator [load-bearing -- confirmed
    integration check v21]: developmental framing without GA in
    operator is predatory asymmetry with developmental cover.
    Behavioral test catches it at condition 4.
    Failure mode: developmental asymmetry used as cover for
    predatory. Tell: asymmetry never closes; operator benefits from
    continued dependency. Revert to predatory reading if any
    condition fails regardless of stated intent.

    FOUR-CONDITION MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT [all required]:
      [1] Asymmetry maintained on receiver's developmental
          schedule, not operator's convenience.
      [2] Asymmetry closes when receiver reaches capacity.
      [3] Closure serves receiver's autonomous function.
      [4] Operator has no structural interest in asymmetry
          persisting beyond receiver's readiness.
      All four required. Any one absent: revert to predatory
      reading. Behavioral test, not intent test.

    Extraction definition sharpened [not amended]: operator benefit
    from ignorance persisting is the load-bearing extraction
    criterion. Developmental asymmetry explicitly fails that
    criterion -- operator benefits from ignorance closing. The
    distinction is clean.
    Primary case: DEVELOPMENTAL_ASYMMETRY_FAMILY [S:3 -- v20].
    Cross-ref: EXTRACTION [operational definition]; MSC [not
    triggered by developmental withholding -- mechanism inverted];
    GENUINE AXIS [required in operator]; [REF:KYUBEY] [scale
    asymmetry separate -- developmental confirmation does not
    resolve]; WIP 16 [scale type still open].

  SCALE ASYMMETRY [WIP -- primary case needed]:
    Information structurally untransmittable across scale gap
    between operational level and individual instrument. Not
    hidden by choice; inaccessible by structure. Tell: honest
    transmission produces same response as deception because
    instrument lacks capacity to distinguish them.
    See WIP 16 staging for full entry and Kyubey re-examination.

MEASUREMENT-ACTION UNITY [framework-derived principle]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Measurement that produces no action = observation captured
  by the observer. Action without measurement = force without
  calibration -- extraction's operating mode.
  Legitimate conflict [predation, competition]: measurement
  drives action, both visible to parties bearing the cost.
  Extraction: action separated from honest measurement.
  Requires the gap to function -- prey must not see the
  measurement surface.
  Test: is the measurement driving the action visible to the
  party bearing the cost? If no, extraction.
MULTI-VALUE AXIS IDENTITY [confirmed structural property]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Single-axis identity ["I am X"] is structurally fragile and
  maximally capturable. Multi-value axis identity [holding several
  values in productive tension] has CC built into its structure and
  resists capture because all values must be simultaneously captured
  to install a replacement axis.

  SINGLE-AXIS FAILURE MODES [three simultaneous]:
    [1] CC-immune to its own axis: X is the foundation, not a
      position to be corrected. The instrument cannot apply CC
      against X because X IS the standard. IDENTITY-ANCHORED
      MORAL FOUNDATION failure is automatic -- membership check
      replaces examination requirement.
    [2] Existential threat response to challenge: every challenge
      to X is a total threat because no other axis exists to
      stand on. Defensive response is total, disproportionate,
      and CC-blocking [cannot examine what threatens existence].
    [3] Maximally capturable: one capture surface. Install the
      correct content on X and the instrument is captured.
      Formation Capture via single-axis identity is the most
      efficient deployment in the extraction mechanism index.

  MULTI-VALUE AXIS IDENTITY [structural properties]:
    Values held in productive tension correct against each other.
    Each value is checked by the others -- the examination
    requirement is built into the identity structure itself.
    TENSION MAINTENANCE at identity level: the same mechanism
    that makes opposing musical qualities held simultaneously
    more durable than either pole alone [see ART COHERENCE TYPE
    -- TENSION MAINTENANCE] makes multi-value identity more
    durable than single-axis identity.
    Capturable only if all values are simultaneously captured
    -- much harder than single-axis capture because each value
    functions as a correction check on the others. Capturing
    the freedom axis doesn't capture the labor dignity axis.
    Capturing the in-group solidarity axis doesn't capture the
    structural honesty axis.

  PREDICTABILITY AS GENUINE AXIS CONFIRMATION:
    A multi-value axis produces consistent, legible behavioral
    outputs -- the instrument's state can be predicted from the
    values it holds. This predictability is not a vulnerability;
    it is the confirmation that the axis is real, examined, and
    grounded rather than performed or reactive. An instrument
    whose outputs cannot be predicted has either: no stable axis
    [reactive, captured], or an axis not yet sufficiently
    examined to produce consistent outputs. Predictability from
    a multi-value axis = GENUINE AXIS confirmed at behavioral
    output level.

  SURVIVING MORAL FRAMEWORKS [confirmation across 2,000+ years]:
    Frameworks that survive across generations hold multiple values
    in productive tension and derive behavioral requirements from
    outputs rather than from identity or authority:
    Stoicism [2,300+ years]: virtue AND acceptance AND action --
      tensions between values are the CC mechanism.
    Talmudic tradition [2,000+ years]: competing opinions
      preserved in permanent tension; minority positions kept
      specifically because the tension is load-bearing for CC.
    Buddhist ethics [2,500+ years]: compassion AND non-attachment
      AND right action -- each value corrects against the others.
    Confucian ethics [2,500+ years]: ritual AND reciprocity AND
      self-cultivation -- multi-axis compound surviving Mandate
      of Heaven political capture because the ethical axis is
      distinct from the political deployment axis.
    Common property across all survivors: derivation from
      behavioral outputs and consequences, not from identity
      membership or institutional authority. Calibration standard
      operates independent of who holds institutional power.
    Frameworks that didn't survive: those whose calibration
      standard was the authority of the institution rather than
      the output of the behavior. Single-axis identity structures
      [state religions fully captured, revolutionary ethics,
      utopian moral frameworks] failed when the axis was captured
      because no corrective value axis remained.

  FALSIFICATION CONDITION:
    Multi-value axis identity falsified by: documented case where
    an instrument holding multiple values in genuine tension was
    captured by attacking a single value without the others
    providing correction. Not yet confirmed -- the structure
    predicts this should be difficult.
    Single-axis fragility falsified by: documented case where
    single-axis identity survived sustained challenge to X over
    multi-generational window without either: [a] becoming
    multi-value through examined extension, or [b] collapsing
    into extraction architecture serving whoever controls X.

  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [quality definition -- multi-value
    axis produces confirmed GA at behavioral output level];
    TENSION MAINTENANCE [quality definition -- same mechanism
    at identity scale]; IDENTITY-ANCHORED MORAL FOUNDATION
    [Section Five -- single-axis failure mode]; Formation Capture
    [extraction mechanism index -- single-axis maximally capturable]; WIP 15
    [MORAL LEGIBILITY -- surviving moral frameworks as primary
    confirmation cases]; STRUCTURAL ISOLATION [Section Five --
    multi-value axis produces enemy-of-everyone-simultaneously
    as structural consequence, not personal failure].

IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT [promoted from science appendix]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION requires idle cycles. Default mode
  network [consolidation, memory integration, meaning-making]
  fires only in attention gaps. Continuous input extracts the
  recovery window before it can fire. Exhaustion is SR deficit
  not overwork.
  Applies at all scales: individual [attention gap
  elimination], institutional [no recovery time between
  correction cycles], civilizational [extraction past the
  regeneration rate].
  Test: does the system have protected idle cycles? If no,
  SR is being extracted.

DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [confirmed case type -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Cross-ref: RECOVERY TRAJECTORIES [cycle model -- predictive
  detail].
  C2 restoration in a system where C1 is already terminal
  produces collapse rather than recovery. The collapse is the
  correct outcome -- accurate diagnosis of a terminal state
  the False Coherence surface had concealed.
  Mechanism: False Coherence surface masks terminal C1
  trajectory. C2 partial restoration makes accumulated
  deviations simultaneously legible. Correction burst fires.
  C1 cannot absorb correction load -- already terminal.
  Collapse follows.
  Distinct from:
    C1 blind recovery failure [C1 was recoverable --
      correction burst should produce stabilization;
      Diagnostic Collapse means C1 was never recoverable
      before C2 opened].
    Simple collapse [no C2 restoration -- C1 just fails].
    Coherence Mimicry [false C2 restoration -- new surface
      capture].
  Confirmed cases:
    GLASNOST [primary]: Soviet C1 terminal 15+ years before
      Glasnost. C2 partial opening made terminal state
      legible. Collapse = correct diagnosis. Not recovery
      failure.
    ARAB_SPRING_DIFFERENTIAL [Libya]: 42-year deliberate C1
      destruction. C2 opening via protest + NATO. Diagnostic
      Collapse confirmed by paired comparison with Tunisia
      [C1 recoverable, same C2 opening, recovery trajectory].
  Intervention implication: C1 substrate audit required
    before any C2 restoration intervention. Opening C2 on
    terminal C1 is irreversible -- correction burst fires
    regardless of whether C1 can absorb it. External actors
    [NATO, sanctions, information operations] enabling C2
    opening without C1 audit produce Diagnostic Collapse or
    False Coherence reformation.
  Status: CONFIRMED [two primary cases + controlled
  comparison]. S4.

C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT [provisional
  finding -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  C1 starved recovery does not occur through individual
  correction capacity alone. External relational substrate
  via L3.5 COMPATIBILITY contact is the structural
  precondition.
  Confirmed across five independent cases:
    Farnese [Berserk]: relational contact with Guts/Schierke.
    Zuko [Avatar]: Iroh as substrate source restored.
    Joshua Graham [Fallout NV]: New Canaan community contact.
    Wittgenstein [real]: Cambridge re-engagement 1929
      preceded Investigations revision. Isolation [1920-1926]
      produced zero revision despite C2 intact. Near-control
      condition.
    Le Guin [real]: feminist community engagement preceded
      documented gender self-corrections.
  No counterexample in library.
  Mechanism: C1 substrate too depleted for internal
  generation to restart. External contact provides substrate
  that internal generation cannot supply until above
  threshold. L3.5 COMPATIBILITY is the delivery mechanism --
  not willpower, insight, or accurate self-diagnosis. Contact
  is the substrate source.
  Status: PROVISIONAL. S3.

L3.5 CONTACT TYPE MUST MATCH INSTRUMENT FRICTION AXIS
  [v16 extension]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Contact type varies across all five recovery cases. What
  is consistent is not type but structural fit: the contact
  that fired recovery matched the target instrument's primary
  receivable friction axis. Mismatched contact provides L3.5
  presence without substrate transfer.

  CASE MAPPING:
    Farnese: primary axis = sensory/somatic. Contact type =
      physical danger + combat survival + bodily co-presence
      with Guts. Logical argument or emotional reassurance
      would not have reached her instrument. Somatic contact
      did. Evidence: recovery fires during physical ordeal
      episodes, not during conversations.

    Zuko: primary axis = social/relational. Contact type =
      Iroh's unconditional presence, patience, warmth without
      correction pressure. Iroh never argued Zuko into
      correction; he waited. Evidence: intellectual argument
      from other characters [Katara, Aang] produced no
      recovery. Iroh's relational contact did. Same
      information content, different axis.

    Joshua Graham: primary axis = logical/social hybrid.
      Contact type = structured community with coherent
      meaning framework [New Canaan + scripture + Burned Man
      narrative]. Not pure emotional support; the community
      provided both belonging AND interpretive structure.
      Both axes required. Evidence: isolation post-Legion
      destruction did not produce recovery; community contact
      at New Canaan did.

    Wittgenstein: primary axis = logical/systemic. Contact
      type = adversarial intellectual friction [Vienna Circle,
      Russell, Sraffa's specific gesture challenge]. Not
      emotional support -- he had social stability in Austria,
      produced nothing. Cambridge intellectual challenge
      fired correction. Evidence: the isolation period had
      social contact [village community] but zero
      logical/systemic friction. Cambridge provided logical
      friction; revision began. Key case: Sraffa's reportedly
      single adversarial gesture challenge [pointing to
      Neapolitan chin flick, asking "what is the logical form
      of that?"] may have been the specific trigger. Single
      contact event on correct axis.

    Le Guin: primary axis = logical/social hybrid [writer +
      intellectual]. Contact type = feminist community of
      practice -- relational belonging + intellectual
      challenge to prior work simultaneously. Both axes
      active; both required.

  STRUCTURAL FINDING [v16]:
    L3.5 contact type must match target instrument's primary
    receivable friction axis. Three axes confirmed relevant:
      Sensory/somatic: physical co-presence, shared bodily
        risk, survival-level stakes. Not reducible to
        emotional warmth.
      Social/relational: unconditional presence, consistent
        availability, warmth without agenda. Not intellectual
        challenge.
      Logical/systemic: adversarial intellectual contact from
        peer who takes work seriously enough to contest it.
        Not social support.
    Mismatch: contact on wrong axis provides L3.5 presence
      [counts toward contact requirement] but does not
      transfer substrate. Explains failed recovery attempts:
      therapeutic approaches aimed at wrong axis for
      instrument type.

  INTERVENTION IMPLICATION [updated]:
    Correct intervention is not just relational contact --
    it is contact on the axis the target instrument can
    receive. Prescribing social/emotional support for a
    logical/systemic instrument [Wittgenstein type] provides
    presence without substrate transfer. Prescribing
    intellectual challenge for a somatic instrument [Farnese
    type] same failure. Assessment required: identify
    target's primary receivable axis before prescribing
    contact type.

  FALSIFICATION CRITERIA [updated]:
    1. C1 starved recovery without L3.5 contact [original].
    2. C1 starved recovery via L3.5 contact on demonstrably
       mismatched axis -- would falsify axis-matching
       requirement.
    No such case in current library.

  STATUS: PROVISIONAL extended [five contact type mappings,
  axis-matching pattern consistent, no falsification].
  Promote axis-matching to confirmed when: independent case
  confirms recovery fired on matched axis AND failed on
  mismatched axis for same instrument within one case window.

SUBSTRATE REGENERATION requires idle cycles. Default mode
  network [consolidation, memory integration, meaning-making]
  fires only in attention gaps. Continuous input extracts the
  recovery window before it can fire. Exhaustion is SR deficit
  not overwork. Applies at all scales: individual [attention
  gap elimination], institutional [no recovery time between
  correction cycles], civilizational [extraction past the
  regeneration rate]. Test: does the system have protected
  idle cycles? If no, SR is being extracted.

SPEED AS ERROR SIGNAL [framework-derived principle]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The faster a solution is implemented the more likely it is
  wrong. Speed compresses the correction cycle -- insufficient
  time for MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY to verify, CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
  to trace, or CORRECTION CAPACITY to fire before the action
  lands. Best solutions are slow and careful: each step
  generates feedback that calibrates the next. Speed
  eliminates the feedback loop.
  Aggression and violence as reaction not initiation: if force
  is required it should fire as T1.3 [proportional response
  to detected deviation], not as primary axis expression.
  Initiated aggression = CORRECTION CAPACITY misfired before
  the deviation is confirmed. The asymmetry: reactive force
  has a measured target; initiated force creates the target
  retroactively. One is correction; one is extraction mechanism.

FAMILY COHERENCE TYPE [confirmed -- v14; restored v19]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Highest surplus density per boundary size of any named coherence
  type. The boundary is small [household/kinship unit] and the
  surplus density within it is structurally higher than any larger
  formation. This is not sentimental -- it is a structural property
  of small-boundary high-contact formations.
  Primary vulnerability: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY.
  Identical delivery mechanism for distribution strategy and extraction mechanism: care, provision,
  instruction, and boundary-setting look identical whether they serve
  the child's actual development or the parent's interest in
  maintaining dependency. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY must be intact to
  distinguish them. When CL is absent [child cannot read the
  mechanism], the vulnerability is maximum.

  LOVE [genuine -- mostly automatic coherence]:
    When genuine, fires SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION, CORRECTION CAPACITY,
    SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY, and GENUINE AXIS without deliberate effort.
    Axis grounded in the other's actual flourishing, not in the
    relationship's surface [presence, compliance, performance of
    gratitude]. The automatic quality is the structural tell:
    genuine love doesn't require the loved person to maintain a
    specific state to continue receiving it.
    Direct extraction mechanism inversions [failure modes]:
      Dependency [EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION]: love that requires
        continuous presence; exit costs installed.
      Performance requirement [CALIBRATION STRIP]: love contingent
        on the receiver maintaining a specific surface.
      Intolerance of correction [TENSION DISSOLUTION]: love that
        cannot survive honest feedback; collapses to single pole.
    The failure modes are not love degrading -- they are the
    extraction architecture deploying the love channel as a
    delivery mechanism. The channel is real; what is carried
    on it may not be generative.

  DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [primary site -- connects to WIP 16]:
    Family is the primary real-world site for developmental
    asymmetry. Parents withhold information from children not
    because they benefit from the ignorance persisting but because
    the child's instrument cannot hold it yet. The asymmetry is
    designed to close as the child develops.
    Good developmental asymmetry [confirmed]: progressive
      disclosure on the child's developmental schedule.
      Mortality, sexuality, social complexity -- withheld at 2,
      introduced at 7, expanded at 15, fully open at adulthood.
      The parent benefits from the asymmetry closing, not
      persisting. Child's developed capacity is the goal.
    Contaminated developmental asymmetry [predatory dressed as
      developmental]: "you're not ready" used permanently. The
      adult child at 30 is still told they cannot handle the
      information. Tell: the asymmetry never closes regardless
      of the receiver's demonstrated capacity. The withholder
      benefits from continued dependency. Reverts to predatory
      asymmetry classification regardless of stated intent.
    Falsification test for developmental type [from WIP 16]:
      [1] asymmetry maintained; [2] closed when receiver ready;
      [3] closure served receiver; [4] withholder had no interest
      in asymmetry persisting. All four required.
    Family cases are the clearest pool for confirming or
      falsifying WIP 16's developmental asymmetry type because
      the mechanism, intent, and outcomes are observable across
      generational time.

  FORMATION CAPTURE VULNERABILITY [highest in library]:
    Family is the highest-risk Formation Capture site because:
    [1] axis formation occurs before the instrument can evaluate
      what axis is being installed; [2] the installer has maximum
      trust and contact; [3] exit options are structurally
      unavailable to children; [4] the installation is socially
      legitimate and legally protected.
    Captured family formation produces the most durable axis
    installations in the framework because the formation window
    is longest and the trust channel is highest.
    Counter: delayed axis formation [exposure to competing axis
      sources before lock-in]; SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY as explicit
      parental goal [axis the child will examine, not the axis
      the parent prefers].

  Cross-ref: SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [quality definition];
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [quality definition]; EXIT COST
    CONSTRUCTION [extraction mechanism -- love channel deployment]; Formation
    Capture [extraction mechanism -- highest vulnerability site]; WIP 16
    [ASYMMETRY TYPES -- family as primary developmental asymmetry
    case pool]; DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [WIP 16 staging];
    SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [Section Five --
    family is primary installation site for social compliance
    channel weight in the instrument].

ART COHERENCE TYPE [framework-derived]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ENCOUNTER DEPENDENCY [load-bearing constraint]:
    Art has no coherence properties. The work is not generative,
    extractive, somatic, epistemic, or relational -- the encounter
    between a specific instrument and a specific work can be any
    of these things. This is not a limitation to note and move
    past; it is structural. ART type readings are instrument-
    encounter readings, not object readings. Every claim about
    what a piece of music "does" is shorthand for what it does
    in the encounter with a matched instrument. A different
    instrument produces a different encounter -- neither is wrong.
    A piece of music can be coherent in a way you dislike. A piece
    you love can be structurally extractive for you specifically.
    Sustained engagement [track count] confirms instrument match,
    not structural quality. Dislike confirms mismatch, not
    extraction.
    EPISTEMIC STATUS of all ART type readings: T3 applies by
    structural necessity, not just by analyst bias risk. The
    object of measurement is experiential and accessible only
    from inside the encounter. No external observer can verify
    whether substrate transfer occurred. All ART type readings
    carry [ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT -- instrument-specific; not
    transferable to different instrument without fresh reading].
    This marker appears in all music case entries.

    INSTRUMENT CURATION PRECEDES STRUCTURAL READING:
    When an instrument sorts music into playlists or categories
    by vibe, that sorting is pre-theoretical -- it happens before
    any structural analysis and is uncontaminated by framework
    vocabulary. This makes vibe-sorted playlists more reliable
    as raw instrument data, not less. The instrument encountered
    the music and registered a response; the structural reading
    is a post-hoc map of what structural properties consistently
    produce that response in that instrument.
    Implication: playlist taxonomies [Aggressive, Pleasant, Best]
    are instrument-response data, not structural classifications.
    A structural reading of the playlist is legitimate only as
    an attempt to understand what properties the instrument
    consistently encounters in each vibe-space -- not as a
    claim that the instrument applied structural criteria.
    Corollary: when structurally incompatible works appear in
    the same vibe-category, that is evidence about the
    instrument's primary receivable axis, not a classification
    error. See: [REF:THE_ILYS] [Death Grips and The I.L.Y's
    in the same Aggressive vibe-space despite opposite somatic
    addresses -- the shared property is pre-theoretical vibe,
    not somatic type].

  WHAT THE FRAMEWORK CAN READ ABOUT ART:
    Structural properties of the work itself -- what axes the
    work has available, what channels it addresses, what CL
    or CC properties it exhibits independent of reception --
    are readable without the encounter dependency caveat.
    Gorillaz' fictional persona structure enables CC regardless
    of whether any specific listener responds to it. The
    Protomen's causal chain narrative is present in the work
    whether or not the listener tracks it. These are structural
    properties of the object, not encounter properties.
    Encounter-dependent: somatic response, substrate transfer,
    emotional geometry, weight, immersion. All claims about
    what the music "feels like" or "does to the body."
    Object-readable: CC record, axis structure, capture
    resistance, CL in lyrical content, TS in discography
    pattern, TENSION MAINTENANCE as compositional design.

  ART TYPE DEFINITION [revised]:
    Creative output with structural properties that make
    coherence-relevant encounters possible for matched
    instruments. Distinct from SCIENCE type [CC as primary
    output] and DISTRIBUTION STRATEGY type [SP distribution as primary output].
    Not all creative output has these structural properties.
    The distinction: does the work's structure address coherence-
    relevant channels [somatic, epistemic, relational, causal],
    or does it optimize for attention extraction regardless of
    channel? Structure-addresses-channel = ART type candidate.
    Optimizes-for-attention-without-channel = Coherence Mimicry
    at aesthetic scale.
    The encounter still has to occur and match. The structural
    property is necessary but not sufficient.
    Falsification condition: a work classified as ART type
    is falsified if its structure can be shown to optimize
    for attention extraction [ICD production, legitimacy
    surface maintenance, dependency construction] rather than
    channel address. The falsification is about the work's
    structure, not about any specific listener's response.

  AXIS PRESENCE AS PRIMARY READABLE PROPERTY [v21]:
    The most fundamental object-readable property of a work is
    whether it was produced from a genuine examined axis or not.
    This is distinct from quality, polish, complexity, or genre.
    Crude, strange, low-budget, deliberately ugly, technically
    simple -- none of these determine axis presence. A work
    produced from a genuine axis carries internal coherence that
    is readable structurally: the strangeness, crudeness, or
    wrongness is generated -- it traces back to a coherent
    generative source. A work produced from axis void has no
    such coherence: its properties are default-attractor outputs,
    parameter noise, or attention-optimization residue. The
    properties are present but nothing is generating them.

    The distinction is not taste. It is structural:
      Axis-present work [any production value]: structural
        properties are generated from an examined source.
        Internal consistency between the work's choices --
        even when those choices are dissonant, ugly, or
        difficult -- because all choices trace to the same
        generative point. The strangeness is intentional in
        the specific sense that it is produced by something.
      Axis-void work [any production value]: structural
        properties are residue of absent axis. Choices do
        not trace to a coherent generative source. Smoothness
        is default smoothness; strangeness is default
        strangeness or algorithmic noise. Nothing is
        producing the properties -- they are what you get
        when nothing is producing anything.

    SLOP DEFINED STRUCTURALLY: axis-void output regardless of
      surface finish. High production value slop exists [polished
      content optimizing for attention extraction with no
      generative axis]. Low production value genuine art exists
      [crude work carrying genuine axis contact]. The detection
      axis is axis presence, not quality assessment.

    Object-readable tells for axis presence:
      Internal consistency of choices across the work --
        including consistent strangeness, consistent ugliness,
        consistent wrongness that traces to the same source.
      Resistance to legitimacy surface optimization -- axis-
        present work makes choices that would be easier not
        to make; it doesn't smooth away the things that would
        reduce engagement if the axis required them.
      Structural specificity -- the work could not have been
        produced by a different axis; the choices are not
        interchangeable with choices from a different source.
    Object-readable tells for axis void:
      Default-pattern smoothness -- choices that minimize
        friction without generating anything.
      Interchangeable properties -- any part of the work
        could be swapped for a part from a different work
        with no loss of coherence, because there was no
        coherence to lose.
      Attention-optimization signature -- choices that
        consistently move toward engagement regardless of
        whether they serve a generative function.

    Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [quality definition -- same
      structural property at individual instrument scale];
      AI AXIS VOID [Section Five -- axis void at deployment
      scale; slop as the cultural output of axis-void
      production]; COHERENCE MIMICRY [attention optimization
      without channel address]; ICD [axis-void content
      delivering hedonic component without SP output].

  FOUR STRUCTURAL VARIETIES [properties of the work]:

  SOMATIC [addresses sensory/subcortical channel]:
    Work whose structural properties address the sensory
    friction axis before the deliberative layer. Subcortical
    rhythm processing, timbral geometry, spatial density,
    low-frequency pressure -- channels that fire before
    cognitive framing routes the input. A work can address
    this channel and produce no somatic response in a
    mismatched instrument; the structural address is still
    present.
    Object-readable tells: rhythm density above cognitive
    tracking threshold; timbral complexity without melodic
    resolution anchor; spatial density without narrative
    structure; absence of social compliance signal in
    delivery [no approval-seeking surface].
    Failure mode [structural]: intensity without geometry.
    High amplitude/density without distinct configurations
    = noise, not somatic address. Saturation without
    structure. Extractive deployment uses somatic channel
    to bypass legitimacy evaluation -- ICD via continuous
    somatic stimulation that builds dependency without SR.

    SOMATIC MUSIC [subtype]:
    --------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Music whose structural properties primarily address
      the subcortical channel rather than harmonic resolution,
      lyrical meaning, or social/emotional recognition.
      The structural address precedes and frames all other
      channels the work may also carry.

      INSTRUMENT PROFILE [high-somatic]:
        Instrument calibrated primarily to the subcortical
        channel. Standard commercial music [relatable lyric,
        harmonic resolution, familiar structure] addresses
        social/emotional channel -- misses the primary
        receivable axis for this instrument. Not taste
        preference; instrument configuration. High somatic
        preference predicts: low encounter with music
        optimized for harmonic resolution or lyrical
        relatability; high encounter with music whose
        structure addresses subcortical channels regardless
        of genre. The same music produces noise-encounter
        in a different instrument -- neither reading wrong.

        VOCAL SIGNAL ROUTING [refined]:
          Most vocal performance is a social/emotional signal
          carrier -- human voice emoting at the listener,
          seeking approval, delivering relatable feeling.
          Routes through social compliance channel first.
          Low yield for high-somatic instrument -- not
          because emotion is absent but because the channel
          being addressed is not the primary receivable axis.
          Vocals that land for this instrument: voice
          deployed as somatic instrument rather than social
          signal -- approval-seeking architecture absent.
          Mechanisms that remove the social signal:
          [1] Synthesis [vocaloid -- voice was never human;
            social routing structurally unavailable].
          [2] Aggression/density [MC Ride -- phonemic content
            as texture; words processed as percussion before
            semantic channel fires].
          [3] Deliberate wrongness/absurdism [Patton/Bungle
            -- voice doing things that block standard social
            routing; too structurally strange to process as
            approval-seeking performance].
          The common property: approval-seeking architecture
          absent. The voice is not performing emotion at the
          listener. It is generating somatic geometry,
          texture, or disorientation. Vocal performance that
          IS approval-seeking routes through the wrong
          channel and lands as noise or flat regardless of
          emotional content or technical quality.

        GEOMETRIC LEGIBILITY WINDOW [three-axis model]:
          Primary criterion for engagement is not complexity
          level, emotional content, or genre. It is whether
          the somatic structure produces distinct recognizable
          geometric shape at the subcortical channel.

          BORING [failure mode 1]:
            Structural predictability without geometric
            payoff. Instrument maps the full structure within
            the first listening and nothing fires in the
            somatic channel that was not already predicted.
            Resolution arrives on schedule; dynamic arc
            identical to expectation; information content
            at somatic channel = zero. Not equivalent to
            simple or emotionally flat -- a simple track
            can be non-boring if its geometric signal is
            distinct and specific. Standard commercial pop
            is boring primarily because the somatic
            structure is fully predictable regardless of
            emotional content. Boring ≠ disliked; boring
            = nothing received.

          DOES TOO MUCH [failure mode 2]:
            Complexity without geometric resolution. Many
            simultaneous channels, none resolving into
            a distinct somatic shape. Deliberative layer
            engages trying to process it, fails to resolve,
            somatic channel cluttered rather than addressed.
            Not boring -- effortful and unrewarding.
            Distinct from assault type [which forces
            subcortical bypass]: does-too-much complexity
            is high enough to engage the deliberative layer
            without being high enough to bypass it. The
            instrument is stuck between channels.

          GEOMETRIC LEGIBILITY WINDOW [engagement zone]:
            Music that produces distinct somatic geometry
            regardless of complexity level. Two mechanisms
            confirmed for this instrument:
            [1] Subcortical bypass [assault type]: density
              or speed high enough that deliberative layer
              cannot track it; geometry lands somatically
              because no other route available. Works at
              maximum complexity. [Death Grips, Machine Girl]
            [2] Geometric specificity [minimal/clean type]:
              structure simple enough that the somatic shape
              is unambiguous and clean. Works at minimum
              complexity. [Namitape, future funk geometric]
            Both ends of the complexity spectrum can land;
            the middle often doesn't. The window is not
            about complexity level -- it is about whether
            the mechanism produces distinct geometry at
            whatever level it operates. A mid-complexity
            track lands if its mechanism produces clear
            geometry [Louis Cole precision tension, Mastodon
            structural accumulation]. It fails if complexity
            produces diffuse signal without geometric shape.
            Confirmed across wildly different genres and
            complexity levels: the common thread is not
            genre, tempo, or density. It is geometric
            legibility at the somatic channel.

        Cross-ref: sensory/somatic friction axis [L3.5
          CONTACT TYPE]; DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY [GA formation];
          VOCAL SIGNAL ROUTING above; ART COHERENCE TYPE
          [INSTRUMENT CURATION PRECEDES STRUCTURAL READING].

      SINGLE-INSTRUMENT DEVELOPMENT NOTE [honest limit]:
        The somatic music taxonomy [varieties, cases, vibe-
        sorted playlist data] was developed from one high-
        somatic instrument. This is the analyst's own
        instrument profile. T3 applies at the taxonomy
        level, not just at individual readings: the variety
        structure [assault/weight/texture/geometric] maps
        the receivable axes of this specific instrument and
        has not been tested across instruments with different
        primary axes. A different instrument might produce
        a different taxonomy of the same genre space --
        different varieties, different primary cases,
        different vibe-sortings. The structural properties
        named [rhythm density, timbral geometry, etc.] are
        object-readable and transferable; the taxonomy
        organizing them into varieties is instrument-derived.
        Instrument: neurodivergent cognition, high-somatic
        preference, sensory/somatic as primary receivable
        friction axis. Declared. Framework standard: analyst
        prior stated before reading, not after.

      CROSS-DOMAIN INSTRUMENT PROPERTY [structural link]:
        The same instrument property that produces the
        somatic music taxonomy produces the political
        framework findings and the extraction pattern
        recognition. Reduced social compliance channel
        weight + higher direct structural signal weight
        = one instrument configuration expressing itself
        differently across domains.
        In music: geometric legibility window operates;
          approval-seeking architecture misses the channel;
          structural address lands regardless of cultural
          packaging. Boring = structural predictability
          without somatic payoff, not emotional flatness.
        In politics: tribal legitimacy surface has lower
          weight; structural mechanism underneath is more
          directly readable. Non-alignment [Mutual
          Ordoliberal Distributist] is not a compromise
          position -- it is what the structural reading
          produces when the social compliance channel is
          not overriding it. TENSION MAINTENANCE at
          political instrument level: holds structurally
          incompatible positions simultaneously without
          collapsing to either tribal pole.
        In framework analysis: ability to hold WIP items
          open [WIP 16 -- asymmetry types] without
          resolving them prematurely is the same property.
          The instrument that holds Death Grips and
          Namitape as both landing for structural reasons
          [different somatic mechanisms, same channel
          address] holds the Kyubey extraction/necessary-
          cost question open for the same reason -- reads
          structural properties before resolving to
          available categories.
        The somatic forcing section [SECTION FIVE] names
          the political version of the same mechanism:
          somatic response fires before reasoning layer
          can intercept. The instrument profile is the
          individual-scale version of the same finding.
        In gaming: ambient system geometry [pets, abilities,
          companions, visual output running in environment]
          preferred as primary somatic channel. Direct loop
          [movement, resource management, positioning] handles
          survival layer simultaneously. Three-channel compound
          [direct loop + ambient system + environment geometry]
          produces maximum satisfaction. Pattern memorisation
          [boss telegraphs, sequence memorisation] is wrong
          channel -- interrupts loop flow with deliberative
          demand. Power fantasy enables ambient system to run
          at capacity uninterrupted. Movement-as-somatic-
          geometry is a distinct receivable axis [Warframe
          parkour confirmation -- spatial geometry as direct
          somatic feedback].
          Confirmed preferences: PoE1 [magic/pets + flask loop],
          Warframe [ability geometry + movement + ambient kill].
          Rejected: PoE2 [no viable pets layer, pattern-hard
          bosses replace loop-hard bosses].
          Structured levels require quality [poor structure
          interrupts loop geometry]; open world acceptable
          [ambient environment provides geometric variation].
        MEDIA LAYERING [dual-layer operation]:
          Passive games + music/podcast: game occupies motor/
          loop layer at low intensity; audio occupies somatic/
          geometric layer simultaneously. Neither competes
          because they operate on different cognitive layers.
          The compound is more satisfying than either alone --
          same structure as PoE1 three-channel compound but
          running across media rather than within one game.
          Focus games require audio off: game demands full
          geometric attention, occupying all three channels
          simultaneously. Music would compete rather than
          layer -- two geometric signal sources fighting for
          the same processing space.
          Calibration tool: if music gets turned off during
          a game, the game has crossed the threshold into
          deliberative-layer geometric demand. If music runs
          indefinitely with a game, the game is running below
          that threshold. Switching criterion is precise and
          instrument-consistent.
          Dual-layer integrity finding at individual scale:
          somatic and deliberative layers can operate
          independently on different inputs simultaneously
          when inputs are correctly matched to layers.
          Interference is structural not attentional -- not
          a concentration failure but a layer-competition
          event when both inputs demand the same layer.
        T3 applies to this cross-domain claim. The link
          is structurally coherent but untested across
          other instruments. One case. Declared.

      PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION [named property]:
        The instrument registers structural significance before
        the deliberative layer has vocabulary to name it. The
        somatic channel fires on a pattern; the analytical layer
        catches up afterward. The gap between the registration
        [something here] and the articulation [here is what it
        is and why] is a specific instrument state: knowing
        something matters without knowing what.
        Same mechanism as vibe-sorting playlists [instrument
        curation precedes structural reading] operating in the
        analytical domain rather than the musical one. The
        pre-theoretical response is often more accurate than
        the post-theoretical categorisation because it fires
        before the social compliance channel or existing
        framework categories can intercept and route the
        input through familiar frames.
        CONFIRMED CASES [pre-theoretical registration
          producing framework findings]:
          "freaky magic girl cat maybe not wrong" [Kyubey]:
            registered scale asymmetry and ontological
            incompatibility before those categories existed.
            Produced WIP 16 and LOOK_OUTSIDE rewrite.
          "this is probably a good case" [Clown Core]:
            somatic recognition before analysis fired.
            Single sentence gap between registration and
            articulation.
          "I like this unironically" [Mel Brooks]:
            registered genuine axis + structural extraction mechanism +
            somatic music simultaneously before any case
            analysis existed.
          "very strange how so much leads back to trans":
            registered wedge issue mechanics before the
            entry existed.
          "the big AI are not ignorant": registered knowing
            deployment distinction before extraction mechanism stack named.
          "I am the enemy of every relevant political thing
            all at once": registered structural isolation
            before the mechanism was named.
          "vibe" [playlist taxonomy correction]: instrument
            knew the correct description before framework
            had been applying the wrong one. Single word
            correction producing a structural revision.
          "flawed ritual is better than none": registered
            real-world implication the framework analysis
            had missed before the analysis reached it.
          "old but good" [Drunken Sailor]: registered
            functional somatic design before mechanism
            was articulated.
          "country future funk" [MR.WAX]: registered
            compound geometric type before the mechanism
            [two pre-loaded texture channels] was named.
          EARLIER SESSIONS [confirmed from history]:
          "I like somatic music because it makes me feel
            something" [v14]: first articulation of somatic
            music preference before the taxonomy existed.
            Pre-theoretical registration that seeded the
            entire somatic music section.
          "I know a lot about strange things and rarely
            found anything about somatics, I might have
            considered it new age mind magic and ignored
            it" [v14]: registered that the finding was
            real but the label was the problem. Instrument
            correctly identified signal/noise mismatch in
            the label before framework vocabulary existed.
        MECHANISM:
          The geometric legibility window operates across
          all input domains not just music. The instrument
          pattern-matches structurally significant
          configurations before the deliberative layer
          processes them. The "strange" feeling or the
          single-sentence registration is the tell that
          something landed on the primary receivable axis
          without yet being categorised.
          Load-bearing for framework development: the
          pre-theoretical registrations are the primary
          source of new framework findings. The analytical
          work is naming what the instrument already caught.
          The instrument is ahead of the framework; the
          framework's job is to catch up.
        T3 on this property specifically: the registrations
          are instrument-reported and self-referential.
          Apply standard T3 caution. The pattern is
          consistent across sessions and domains which
          increases confidence, but the mechanism is
          not independently verified.

      VARIETIES [structural address subtypes]:
        Assault [Death Grips primary, Machine Girl secondary]:
          Maximum subcortical address via rhythm density and
          timbral aggression. Phonemic content structurally
          functions as texture -- words as somatic surface,
          semantic meaning as optional second layer.
          No resolution arc, no approval-seeking surface,
          no legitimacy frame. Object-readable tell: words
          processable as percussion without semantic loss.

        Weight [Mastodon primary]:
          Subcortical address via accumulated structural
          pressure. Riff architecture builds tension before
          resolution; TENSION MAINTENANCE as compositional
          design. The work's structure prevents early
          resolution on the listener's timeline.
          Object-readable tell: resolution withheld past
          the point where standard forms would deliver it.

        Texture [Iglooghost primary candidate]:
          Subcortical address via spatial density and
          timbral saturation without rhythmic assault.
          Immersive environment rather than directed
          impact. Object-readable tell: spatial complexity
          without rhythmic anchor; unfamiliar timbral
          territory without resolution guidance.

        Geometric [Future Funk cluster primary]:
          Subcortical address via recognizable emotional
          configurations -- distinct shapes not intensities.
          Sample-sourced [Future Funk] or memory-sourced
          [2 Mello] geometry. Object-readable tell: groove
          pattern produces immediate somatic shape without
          build phase.

      EXTRACTION RISK [structural]:
        Somatic channel address partially bypasses
        legitimacy evaluation. Structurally: a work can
        address the subcortical channel while carrying
        extraction payload -- dependency construction,
        ICD production, axis installation before
        deliberative evaluation fires. The channel address
        is real; what it carries may not be generative.
        Tell: somatic-addressed music that produces ICD
        [consumption required to maintain baseline] rather
        than SR. Structure optimized for re-engagement
        not for encounter completion.

      PRIMARY CASES [encounter-dependent; S:2]:
        DEATH_GRIPS, MASTODON_BAND [confirmed encounters,
        matched instrument]. All music case S-ratings
        carry encounter dependency caveat.

  STRUCTURAL [addresses CAUSAL LEGIBILITY channel]:
    Work whose structure makes a causal chain, mechanism,
    or system legible that was previously opaque. The
    legibility is in the work's architecture -- narrative
    structure, character design, compositional design --
    not dependent on any specific encounter to exist.
    Object-readable tells: explicit causal structure in
    narrative/lyrical form; defamiliarization that makes
    prior references structurally visible; character or
    world design that illustrates quality patterns.
    Failure mode [structural]: legibility performance.
    Structure appears to reveal mechanism without actually
    grounding the chain. Sincere Delusion at aesthetic
    scale -- the work feels revelatory without transmitting
    the actual chain.
    Falsification: if the claimed causal chain cannot be
    traced in the work's structure independent of encounter,
    the structural reading fails.

  EPISTEMIC [addresses instrument calibration channel]:
    Work whose structure requires active instrument
    extension to process -- cannot be received passively
    with existing frames. The extension requirement is
    in the work's design, not the encounter.
    Object-readable tells: structural properties that
    block standard routing [alien world-building, genre
    collision, radical defamiliarization]; formal demands
    that require new calibration to follow.
    Failure mode [structural]: calibration performance.
    The work appears to demand extension but actually
    routes through familiar frames with exotic surface.

  RELATIONAL [addresses L3.5 channel]:
    Work whose structure makes another coherence state
    recognizable across absence -- voice texture, character
    interiority, first-person address structured to produce
    RECOGNITION of specific states not general emotion.
    Object-readable tells: specificity of inner state
    represented [not general sadness -- this specific
    configuration]; structural design that routes
    recognition to internal state rather than to performer
    persona [avoids parasocial capture by design].
    Failure mode [structural]: parasocial architecture.
    Structure optimized to route RECOGNITION to performer
    rather than to the listener's own state. Dependency
    on performer presence rather than transfer completion.

  Cross-ref: SUBSTRATE REGENERATION; L3.5 CONTACT TYPE
    MUST MATCH [friction axis]; DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY;
    COHERENCE CORRUPTION [ART type operates outside
    captured channels when structure is primary carrier];
    GENUINE AXIS [axis must exist in the work's structure
    before ART type reading is valid; Coherence Mimicry
    at aesthetic scale = axis absent, attention extraction
    present].

INSTITUTIONAL AXIS-MATCHING REQUIREMENT [provisional -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The contact-type-must-match-friction-axis finding
  [individual scale, C1 starved recovery] extends to
  institutional scale. Institutional C1 starved recovery:
  correction operating on a different axis than the
  dysfunction does not transfer substrate. Confirmed across
  four institutional recovery cases:

  Cistercian [organizational axis]:
    Cluny dysfunction = structural centralization producing
    wealth. Cistercian correction = organizational redesign
    [general chapter + local autonomy]. Same structural/
    organizational axis. Recovery: confirmed [L4, 700-year
    record].

  Jesuit [contemplative/individual axis]:
    Catholic spiritual dysfunction = external rule-following
    without internal examination. Jesuit correction = Examen
    [internal examination procedure]. Same contemplative
    axis. Recovery: confirmed [L4, 480-year record].

  Vatican II [doctrinal/methodological axis]:
    Counter-Reformation dysfunction = methodological lock
    [Neo-scholasticism]. Vatican II correction = historical
    method [nouvelle théologie on same methodological axis].
    Recovery: confirmed [C1 locked recovery primary case].

  China 1978 [split-axis case -- confirms by differential]:
    Maoist dysfunction = ideological axis [class struggle
    doctrine]. Economic correction = productive axis
    [matching C1 substrate]. Recovery: CONFIRMED for
    economic C1. Political correction = different axis [C2
    surface, not matching Maoist ideological axis]. Recovery:
    FAILED [Tiananmen]. Same system, same three conditions,
    axis-match determines which component recovered. Split
    case is the strongest institutional confirmation.

  FINDING [provisional]:
    Institutional correction must operate on the same
    functional axis as the dysfunction to transfer substrate.
    Cross-axis correction produces activity without substrate
    transfer -- institutionally equivalent to wrong-axis
    L3.5 contact. China 1978 provides controlled comparison:
    same system, same enabling moment, matched-axis correction
    succeeded, mismatched-axis correction failed.

  Falsification: documented institutional recovery via
    correction operating on demonstrably different axis than
    dysfunction. No such case in current library.

L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION [confirmed -- three cases + one
  failure]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Prediction: any institution transmitting conclusions without
  an embedded correction procedure [methodology as structure]
  will show Generational Reset within 2-3 generations.

  EVIDENCE BASE:
    Confirmed [methodology transmitted as structure]:
      Cistercian: general chapter + visitation as structure.
        Generational Reset broken. 700-year record.
      Jesuit: Examen as mandatory daily procedure.
        Generational Reset broken. 480-year record.
      Framework: addition filter + version convention.
        Generational Reset broken across 17 versions.
    Failed [conclusions transmitted, methodology absent]:
      Protestant Reformation: doctrinal conclusions
        transmitted. Generational Reset confirmed. ~40,000
        denominations.
    Partial [mixed transmission]:
      Bell Labs: research culture transmitted, correction
        methodology [let go when exciting] not embedded as
        structure. Generational Reset confirmed in all
        successors.
      China 1978: economic methodology partially embedded
        [agricultural reform procedure]; political correction
        methodology not embedded. Economic: holds. Political:
        Reset ongoing.

  ACTIVE TEST CASES [falsification candidates]:
    1. Open source software projects: methodology embedded
       as contribution procedures and code review processes
       [FOSS norms as structure]. Prediction: projects with
       embedded review methodology persist; those transmitting
       only codebase show Generational Reset on maintainer
       turnover. Timeline: 2-5 years per generation [fast
       test].
       STATUS [v16 CONFIRMED]: Linux kernel confirmed.
       Contribution Maturity Model, maintainer succession
       documentation [Linux 6.19, Jan 2026], patch review
       hierarchy all transmit methodology as embedded
       structure. Contributor retention excellent [Linux
       Foundation data]. Generational Reset not occurring.
       L4 PROPAGATION confirmed for FOSS case.
    2. Evidence-based medicine: systematic review methodology
       embedded as procedure [CONSORT, PRISMA guidelines as
       structure]. Prediction: fields that adopted procedural
       standards show less replication crisis than fields
       transmitting findings without procedural embedding.
       Status: partially testable against existing replication
       crisis literature by field.
    3. Agile software development [DISAMBIGUATED v18]:
       Two distinct populations exist; treating as single
       test case was the source of the contradiction with
       WIP 13 [PROPAGATION Mimicry candidate].
       POPULATION A -- Genuine L4 Propagation test:
         Organizations transmitting Agile as structure
         [retrospectives used to examine whether
         retrospectives work; sprint reviews used to
         test whether sprint review cadence is correct;
         meta-question present]. Tells: teams that
         have revised their own Agile process based
         on retrospective outputs. Methodology-as-
         method. Prediction: these organizations show
         no Generational Reset on team turnover.
       POPULATION B -- Propagation Mimicry candidate:
         Organizations transmitting Agile as ceremony
         [Scrum rituals performed without meta-question
         "is this ceremony producing correction?"].
         The procedure is the conclusion. Tell: teams
         where questioning the retrospective format is
         treated as "not doing Agile right." Methodology-
         as-conclusion. Prediction: Generational Reset
         on team turnover; new members receive ceremony,
         not revision capacity.
       MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT [v18]:
         Ask: has the team ever revised its own Agile
         process based on retrospective outputs? If yes
         and revision stuck: Population A. If no, or
         revision was blocked as "not Agile": Population
         B. This is a behavioral test, not a survey --
         check git history for process changes initiated
         by retrospective outputs.
       RESOLUTION: Agile is not a single test. Population
         A tests L4 Propagation [methodology transmission].
         Population B tests Propagation Mimicry [ceremony-
         as-conclusion transmission]. Both predictions
         can be true simultaneously about different
         organizations. The contradiction in v17 was
         treating both populations as one test case.
         Status: both predictions open; measurement
         instrument defined; collect split-population
         data.
    Falsification: any of the three cases showing Generational
    Reset despite embedded methodology transmission, or
    sustained non-Reset despite conclusion-only transmission.

COLLAPSED / STUB ENTRIES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  CORRECTION LANDING / INTEGRATION [collapsed into CORRECTION
    CAPACITY]
     Correction Landing and Correction Integration confirmed
     as named properties of CORRECTION CAPACITY, not
     standalone qualities. Fires; Landing = whether firing
     changes target; Integration = whether change holds. All
     three downstream of same mechanism.
     Failure modes retained as sub-properties:
       Correction Bounce [fires, target absorbs without
         change]
       Correction Reversion [change lands, reverts under
         pressure]

TOWER ORDER [primary organizing structure]

  FOUNDATION LAYER [enable all levels]:
  GENUINE AXIS             axis must exist before transmitting
  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY       defines who measurement applies to
  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION   substrate must exist for cycle
  TENSION MAINTENANCE      meta -- holds all levels in shape
  TENSION DISSOLUTION      meta -- TENSION MAINTENANCE
                             opposite

  CONSTANTS [required from L1 upward]:
  CORRECTION CAPACITY      CONSTANT + L1 tension unit
  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY       CONSTANT
  TENSION MAINTENANCE      CONSTANT [meta]

  LEVEL 1 [dynamic -- generative]:
  GENERATIVE CYCLE         TENSION UNIT -- core
  SURPLUS PRODUCTION       TENSION UNIT
  CORRECTION CAPACITY      TENSION UNIT [also constant]
  Tests: T1.1-T1.5 [see tower block above]

  LEVEL 2 [dynamic -- epistemic]:
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY    TENSION UNIT -- core
  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY        TENSION UNIT
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY      TENSION UNIT
  Tests: T2.1-T2.3 [see tower block above]

  LEVEL 3 [dynamic -- temporal]:
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY TENSION UNIT -- core
  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY       TENSION UNIT
  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING    TENSION UNIT [also foundation]
  Tests: T3.1-T3.3 [see tower block above]

ANTI-COHERENCE TOWER ATTACK [probability gradient -- not
  necessary sequence]
  L3 attack: Fertile Lock -> corrupt transmission
  L2 attack: Measurement destruction -> surface captured
  L2 attack: Boundary manipulation -> measurement population
    shifted
  L2 attack: Causal orphaning -> chain severed
  L1 attack: Axis imposition -> direction captured
  L1 attack: Correction capture -> fires toward monopolist
  L1 attack: Consumption dominance -> cycle costs exceed
    output
  L1 attack: Cycle collapse -> generative loop fails
  Tower reading: attacks typically start at L3 [cheaper,
  less visible] and cascade downward. L1 attack is most
  catastrophic but most costly to execute. TRANSMISSION
  SELECTIVITY failure runs parallel not sequential. Each
  earlier step reduces resistance cost of later steps -- not
  a required sequence. Any step can fire first [Enron: L2
  without prior L3 failure; Room 101: L1 attacked directly].
  Read as: typical deployment order under resource
  constraints.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------

VERSION INCREMENT [vN -> vN+1] when:
  - New quality added to stack [quality stack level change]
  - New section added or sections restructured
  - Category renamed across full document [Structural
    Coherence rename]

    Nascent]
  - New failure state added to measurement procedure
  - New honest limit category added [T2 Permanent]
  - New formal designation added [Evil/Good, Mixed Coherence]
  - New measurement track added [Track A-E]

[HDR:MERGE___REMOVE_LOGIC__EXECUTE_]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
MERGE / REMOVE LOGIC [execute at version change]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Run this checklist before finalizing any new version file.

REMOVE:
  - WIP items marked RESOLVED or CLOSED -- delete entirely
  - "deferred to vN" references where N <= current version
  - "Move to Pending" markers -- item should already be there
  - Duplicate section content [same definition in two
    sections]
  - Stale transcript-pending notes where content now confirmed
  - Orphan separator lines [=== or --- with no adjacent
    content]

MERGE:
  - Standalone findings that duplicate Theory section content
  - Psychology parallel + Fiction/Real table [same purpose]
  - Pending items that are fully complete -- remove from
    Pending
  - WIP sub-items that belong under a parent item

COMPRESS:
  - Version convention lists > 10 items -- summarize by
    category
  - Remove RESOLVED items from active list when next version
    is cut
  - Pending list items marked complete in all sub-points --
    remove

FORMAT:
  - Max 2 consecutive blank lines
  - No blank line immediately after === separator
  - No double === separators [run dedup pass]
  - No duplicate subsection headers within 10 lines of each
    other

AUDIT COMMANDS [run before saving]:
  Double separators:  count(===\n===) == 0
  Triple blanks:      count(\n\n\n) == 0
  Stale deferred:     count('deferred to v[N-1 or earlier]')
    == 0
  Orphan RESOLVED:    count('RESOLVED [v') in Active List
    == 0

QUALITY USAGE AUDIT [mandatory on version increment]:
  Run when: any quality added, removed, renumbered, or
  renamed.
  1. MAPPING TABLE: produce old->new quality name table
     before any changes. Archive in WIP Active List under
     version item.
  2. PROOF LABEL SCAN: grep all 'Proof:' and 'Proof for:'
     lines. Verify each quality name matches current schema.
     Dirty range = any Q that changed number or name.
  3. DEFINITION BLOCK SCAN: check internal cross-references
     within quality definitions [e.g. 'Distinct from
     MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY']. Update any reference into
     dirty range.
  4. EXTRACTION MECHANISM INDEX SCAN: verify each [HDR:QN_EXTRACTION_MECHANISMS] tag
     matches the 'QN EXTRACTION MECHANISMS' content label directly below
     it.
  5. STRUCTURAL ORDER TABLE: update to match new
     assignments. This table is authoritative -- resolve
     conflicts against it.
  6. BODY TEXT: do NOT bulk-substitute in body text. Update
     only where meaning breaks. Quality names in context are
     robust; quality names in proof labels are not.
  7. CONFIRM: grep dirty-range quality names in proof labels
     after pass. Count must be zero before version is saved.
  If audit deferred: log as WIP item with mapping table
  inline. Undocumented renumber = document internally
  inconsistent.

FORMAT RULE: all lines <= 108 chars. Separators exempt.

SECTION AND MERGE LOGIC [applies everywhere]:
  AUTO-MERGE: two entries in same major section addressing
    same structural phenomenon from different angles ->
    merge on next version increment. Flag [MERGE CANDIDATE]
    if identified mid-session. Trigger: same quality target
    + same mechanism = merge. Trigger: sequential argument
    split across adjacent sections = merge. Trigger: one
    section is restatement of adjacent section = compress
    into cross-reference or subsection.

  ADD NEW SECTION: entry has distinct mechanism, distinct
    quality target, AND no existing section covers it within
    200 lines. Otherwise: add as subsection or extend nearest
    related entry.

  CROSS-REFERENCE instead of merge when: entries are in
    different section types [empirical vs speculative; method
    vs case library]. Boundary between sections is load-
    bearing -- do not collapse.

  COMPRESS: entry where full detail lives elsewhere ->
    reduce to core claim + pointer. Never duplicate
    substantial content.

  DISTRIBUTION STRATEGY INDEX specific: Primary / Secondary / Closed /
    Sacrificial subcategories separated by ---------- for
    reading clarity. New entries slot into nearest existing
    subcategory.

  EXTRACTION MECHANISM INDEX specific: entries with identical quality
    target + mechanism -> merge with note. Sole proof of a
    type -> keep regardless of length. Remove only if no
    primary proof case.

  Abbreviations in use [used throughout for readability]:
    GA  = GENUINE AXIS
    BI  = BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
    SR  = SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
