    ECD = ENGINEERED CAUSAL DIFFUSION
    CT  = CORRECTION THEATRE

EXTRACTION MECHANISM STACK DETECTION PROTOCOL [main theory -- v17,
  promoted from Section Two]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Promoted from Section Two: stacks are the primary
  deployment pattern in confirmed political and institutional
  cases. Single-extraction mechanism analysis misses load-bearing
  interaction effects. Stack detection changes both reading
  and intervention priority.

  5-STEP PROCEDURE:
    1. List all active extraction mechanisms from case reading.
    2. Check for confirmed stack patterns [below].
    3. Identify interaction effects -- co-deployed extraction mechanisms
       amplify each other's damage and reduce counter cost.
    4. Identify highest-ratio extraction mechanism in stack [cost-asymmetry
       table] -- this is intervention priority, not the most
       visible extraction mechanism.
    5. Confirm stack is load-bearing: remove one extraction mechanism from
       the model and check if the reading changes. If yes --
       it's a stack. If no -- coincidental co-deployment.

  CONFIRMED STACKS [interaction effects]:
    C2+C1 PRESSURE:
      MI degradation makes C1 invisible. System generates
      at full rate; surface cannot read depletion.
      Extraction appears healthy until substrate hits floor.
      Intervention on C2 alone triggers Diagnostic Collapse
      if C1 already terminal. Highest-risk stack: C2
      restoration is the standard intervention and it fires
      correctly into terminal C1. Pre-intervention: C1
      substrate audit mandatory. Do not open C2 on unaudited
      C1 when this stack is active.

    FC+ECC:
      Formation Capture installs axis; Exit Cost Construction
      makes revision structurally unavailable. FC alone =
      captured axis, revision possible at cost. ECC alone =
      high revision cost, axis still examinable. Stack =
      revision both psychologically blocked and structurally
      unavailable. Breaks the standard post-formation
      revision path [requires C1 starved + enabling moment
      + L3.5 matched contact -- ECC eliminates enabling
      moment by making exit too costly to reach substrate
      depletion].

    MSC+CT:
      Measurement Surface Control makes deviation unreadable;
      Correction Theatre makes invisible non-correction look
      like active correction. Stack = system appears to
      correct while surface is captured. External observer
      sees correction activity; no target changes. Standard
      tell: Correction Theatre firing at high volume
      alongside MSC = stack confirmed. Counter: measure
      outputs not correction events. If correction fires
      frequently but targets never change, MSC+CT stack is
      operating.

    ICD+FC:
      Idle Cycle Deprivation prevents axis formation;
      Formation Capture fills the gap with external content.
      ICD alone = axis doesn't form, gap present. FC alone
      = external content available, idle cycles required to
      install it. Stack = ICD creates the vacuum; FC fills
      it before internal generation can fire. No deployer
      coordination required -- structural co-deployment
      under attention architecture. Most common spontaneous
      stack in current population-scale conditions [social
      media + educational Formation Capture].

  INTERVENTION PRIORITY RULE:
    In a confirmed stack, always counter the highest-cost-
    asymmetry extraction mechanism first, not the most visible extraction mechanism.
    The most visible extraction mechanism is usually deployed to draw
    counter-attention away from the load-bearing extraction mechanism.
    Examples:
      C2+C1: C2 capture is visible; C1 depletion is not.
        Counter C1 depletion first; then restore C2.
      FC+ECC: ECC is visible [exit is blocked]; FC is not
        [axis capture is invisible from inside]. Reduce ECC
        to enable enabling moment; FC revision can then
        fire.
      MSC+CT: CT is visible; MSC is not [surface appears
        intact]. Identify independent MI surface before
        attacking CT -- CT without independent MI gives
        the extraction actor a new surface to capture.
      ICD+FC: ICD is structural [attention environment];
        FC is content [what fills the gap]. Restore idle
        cycles first; FC content loses delivery mechanism.

  CROSS-REFERENCE: case library political entries apply
    stack detection at Step 8 of Political Diagnostic
    Protocol. Stack findings feed back to intervention
    priority.

CASE STRENGTH RATING [v17 -- cross-library standard]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every case carries a composite strength rating [S1-S4]
  derived from three axes. Rating is independent of T3
  [analyst prior] -- T3 applies to political cases only.
  S-rating applies to all case types including fictional.

  AXIS 1 -- EVIDENCE TYPE [E]:
    E1: single fictional/illustrative source [structural
      illustration only; no independent confirmation
      possible by definition]
    E2: fictional + framework-consistent cross-reference
      [multiple fictional sources confirming same mechanism;
      still no real-world anchor]
    E3: real-world single domain [historical or empirical,
      one evidence type or one domain]
    E4: real-world multi-domain OR cross-domain independent
      confirmation [two or more independent evidence types
      or domains arriving at same finding without shared
      assumption]

  AXIS 2 -- MECHANISM CONFIRMATION [M]:
    M1: mechanism inferred from outcome only [no pathway
      described]
    M2: mechanism described + plausible pathway [internally
      consistent but no independent confirmation]
    M3: mechanism confirmed by independent parallel
      [psychology research / biological confirmation /
      historical analog in different domain]
    M4: mechanism confirmed by controlled comparison or
      near-control condition [same system, same conditions,
      one variable changed; or paired comparison with
      matched structure]

  AXIS 3 -- FALSIFIABILITY [F]:
    F1: no falsification criteria stated or structurally
      possible
    F2: falsification criteria stated, prediction made,
      not yet tested
    F3: falsification criteria stated, tested against one
      independent case, not falsified
    F4: falsification criteria stated, tested across
      multiple independent conditions in different domains,
      not falsified

  COMPOSITE [S-RATING]:
    S1 [ILLUSTRATION]: E1-2, any M, F1.
      Structural illustration -- valid for concept clarity
      and case presentation. Not confirmation. Do not cite
      as evidence for framework claims. Fictional cases
      are S1 unless cross-confirmed by E3+ finding.
    S2 [WEAK THEORY]: mixed scores with at least one axis
      at E3 or M3. Suggestive, not confirmed. One strong
      falsification would retire the finding to historical
      note. Use as directional indicator only.
    S3 [CONFIRMED]: E3+ AND M3+ AND F2+.
      Independent real-world evidence plus mechanism
      pathway plus stated falsification. Framework standard
      for a confirmed finding. Promote provisional findings
      to S3 when all three axes reached.
    S4 [STRONG]: E4 AND M4 AND F3+.
      Multi-domain independent confirmation plus controlled
      comparison plus tested falsification. Highest library
      confidence. Use as anchor for derived claims.

  FICTIONAL CASE CEILING:
    Fictional cases cap at S2 on E axis [E2 maximum].
    They cannot reach S3 without a real-world anchor
    confirming the same mechanism independently.
    Fictional cases are S1 [illustration] until a real-
    world parallel is found, then S2 [weak theory
    contribution to the cross-domain finding].
    A fictional case that is the sole proof of a framework
    claim = that claim is S1. Add E3+ evidence to promote.

  CURRENT HIGH-CONFIDENCE CASES [S4]:
    DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE: E4 [Glasnost + Libya/Tunisia
      controlled comparison, two independent real-world
      domains], M4 [mechanism confirmed by differential --
      same C2 opening, different C1 states, different
      outcomes], F3 [falsification tested against two cases
      + controlled comparison]. S4.
    L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION: E4 [Cistercian + Jesuit
      [two independent institutional domains] + Linux
      kernel [fast-cycle third domain]], M3 [mechanism
      confirmed by Protestant Reformation failure and Bell
      Labs partial failure], F3 [tested across three active
      cases, not falsified]. S4.

  S3 CONFIRMED FINDINGS [borderline S4]:
    C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT: E3 [five
      cases -- two real-world, three fictional; real-world
      anchor present], M3 [mechanism described + biological
      parallel confirmed], F2 [stated, no counterexample].
      S3. Promote to S4 when: independent institutional
      case with near-control condition confirmed.
    AXIS-MATCHING REQUIREMENT: E3 [five individual cases
      + China 1978 split], M4 [China 1978 split-axis
      differential = controlled comparison], F2 [stated,
      no falsification]. S3 borderline S4; promote when
      second split-axis institutional case confirmed.

  APPLICATION RULE:
    Assign E/M/F scores; derive S from composite. S-rating
    lives in case header alongside T3 where applicable.
    Provisional main theory findings require minimum S2 to
    remain in main theory. S1 entries move to Illustration
    Annex or annotated [ILLUSTRATION ONLY]. Any claim
    citing only S1 cases as support is flagged [STRUCTURAL
    READING -- EVIDENCE PENDING].

SECTION FIVE EPISTEMIC STATUS [v17 -- formal]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Section Five entries are structural readings. Falsification
  timelines are absent unless explicitly marked because
  political cases at civilizational scale have consequence
  horizons beyond testable prediction windows.

  DISTINCTION FROM VAGUE CAPTURED FIELD:
    VCF: theory unfalsifiable by design. Failures attributed
      to implementation to protect the frame. The frame
      itself is never tested.
    Section Five: mechanism claims are structural and real.
      Prediction timelines are absent because the horizon
      exceeds the window -- not because failure is pre-
      excused. The distinction is WHY prediction is absent,
      not whether it is absent.
    Test: if a Section Five claim were falsified, would the
      framework accept the falsification? Yes = structural
      reading with long horizon. No = VCF. Framework
      position: Section Five claims are falsifiable in
      principle; the window is too long for current
      tracking. Track where possible.

  REQUIREMENT [v17]:
    Any Section Five entry making a causal mechanism claim
    must carry either:
    [a] Explicit statement of why prediction window is
        structurally unavailable at current timescales.
    [b] A falsification condition even if timeline is long
        or open-ended.
    Entries without either: flagged as [STRUCTURAL READING
    -- PREDICTION PENDING]. This is not a demotion -- it
    is an honest limit marker. Framework standard.

  CURRENT STATUS [v18]:
    EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational]: carries falsification
      condition [v17 entry above]. Compliant.
    POWER ARCHITECTURE: falsification condition was present
      in epistemic block [v17]; header updated v18 to
      [FALSIFICATION CONDITION STATED]. Compliant.
    REALITY AS EXTRACTION CYCLE: explicit falsification
      condition added v18. Header updated to [FALSIFICATION
      CONDITION STATED]. Compliant.
    SOMATIC FORCING AS POLITICAL CORRECTION MECHANISM:
      carries three-condition falsifiable prediction with
      1-2 generation timeline [v17]. Compliant.

[WIP ITEM 13 -- ACTIVE]
L4 MIMICRY [candidate failure mode -- v17]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  WHY NEEDED:
    Coherence Mimicry confirmed at L1-L3 [HAL 9000 primary
    proof at L3; multiple extraction mechanism entries for L1-L2].
    L4 confirmation requires positive cases [three confirmed:
    Framework, Benedictine, Jesuit]. Without a named failure
    mode, confirmation criteria are asymmetric -- any apparent
    L4 is confirmatory; no case can disconfirm. Asymmetric
    confirmation = the same structure as Vague Captured Field.
    The framework cannot claim L4 is confirmed if it has no
    mechanism to detect false positives.

  CANDIDATE FAILURE MODES [not yet confirmed]:

    REFLEXIVITY Mimicry:
      System produces visible feedback pathway from correction
      outputs to correction architecture [appears reflexive]
      while feedback is routed to a captured surface. The
      architecture receives what it is supposed to receive --
      but the surface is wrong. Correction outputs are
      processed; the conclusions are captured before
      integration.
      Distinction from standard CC capture: standard capture
      is at object level. Reflexivity Mimicry is capture at
      meta-correction level. The system appears to correct
      its correction patterns while actually correcting
      toward monopolist benefit at meta level.
      Candidate case: academic reform cycles -- meta-reviews
      of peer review process appear reflexive [feedback
      pathway visible]; outputs consistently reproduce the
      captured surface [same journals, same metrics, same
      gatekeepers]. Stress event test: leadership change
      produces no change in captured surface. Possible
      Reflexivity Mimicry.
      Not yet confirmed: no direct T8 examination, no
      mechanism trace established.

    INTEGRATION Mimicry:
      System acknowledges correction pattern failure and
      incorporates language of revision into operating
      structure without changing operating structure.
      Distinct from Reflexive Oscillation [which identifies,
      fails to integrate, cycles back] -- Integration Mimicry
      integrates the language of the correction, not the
      correction itself. Terminology changes; mechanism
      stable.
      Tell: successive rounds of reform language that do not
      change measured outputs. Each generation of reform
      terminology absorbs the previous round's critique
      without changing the mechanism being critiqued.
      Candidate case [existing]: corporate DEI cycles --
        each generation of diversity language absorbs the
        previous critique [we need diversity -> we need
        equity -> we need inclusion -> we need belonging]
        without changing measured representation or
        structural outcomes. Full language Integration;
        zero mechanism change.
        Not yet confirmed: mechanism trace required. Could
        be Integration Mimicry or genuine slow-change
        Integration with shorter-than-generation cycles.
      Candidate case [v21 -- Mistral stress-test suggestion]:
        HOLACRACY / FLAT STRUCTURE DEPLOYMENTS. Extraction
        architectures that mimic generative structures by
        adopting distributed-authority language [circles,
        roles, self-management, no managers] while
        reproducing hierarchy under a new name. The
        correction: traditional hierarchy is extractive
        and concentrates CC at top. The Integration
        Mimicry: holacracy absorbs this critique by
        adopting the language of distributed CC without
        transferring actual routing boundary control or
        correction capacity to the distributed nodes.
        The new terminology is integrated; the power
        structure is stable.
        Tell: stress event test -- leadership change or
        financial pressure. If the distributed authority
        collapses to traditional hierarchy immediately
        under stress, Integration Mimicry confirmed.
        Genuine L4 distributed structure would maintain
        distributed CC under stress precisely because
        that is what it is. Mimicry collapses because
        the distributed language was never load-bearing.
        Additional tell: who can fire whom. If the
        "circles" cannot remove the founder or senior
        leadership without external legal mechanism,
        the four-condition test fails at condition 4
        [activation requires central node's permission].
        Fake distributed authority confirmed.
        ZAPPOS STRESS EVENT [CONFIRMED -- v21]:
          Tony Hsieh death November 2020 = completed
          genuine leadership stress event. Result:
          holacracy collapsed in two phases. Phase 1:
          quiet drift away from the model while Hsieh
          still alive [began 2019-2020] -- model was not
          load-bearing even under him. Phase 2: full
          reversion to Amazon hierarchy immediately after
          death. Amazon subsumed departments, laid off
          ~300 employees [20% of workforce], replaced
          branding, reasserted traditional performance
          targets. By 2025: "circles exist, so do team
          leads, but decision-making has returned to a
          more recognizable blend of structure,
          accountability, and leadership. Few would call
          it a Holacracy in the pure sense."
          Key finding: distributed authority was maintained
          by the founder's personal force of will not by
          structural CC. Once the founder departed, Amazon
          [the actual central node -- Zappos sold to
          Amazon 2009] reasserted control immediately.
          Condition 4 confirmed failed: only five people
          in the organization had authority to fire
          someone during Hsieh's tenure. The circles
          could not remove the founder or Amazon without
          external legal mechanism. Four-condition test
          fails at condition 4.
        CONSTITUTIONAL AUTHORITY TEST [CONFIRMED -- v21]:
          HolacracyOne owns and licenses the constitution.
          Circles cannot revise the operating framework
          without constitutional permission from the
          external licensor. Distributed authority
          operates inside a framework it cannot change.
          Condition 4 confirmed failed at constitutional
          level independent of the Zappos case.
        STATUS: INTEGRATION MIMICRY CONFIRMED.
          Language of distributed authority integrated;
          mechanism [actual routing control] never
          distributed. The model collapsed immediately
          when the founder's personal enforcement
          capacity was removed. The distributed
          authority was the legitimacy surface; Amazon's
          routing boundary control was the actual
          mechanism throughout.
          Sources: Quartz [2020]; eMarketer/WSJ [2023];
          Medium/Brinsa [2025]; Knowledge@Wharton [2016];
          INSEAD case [2022].
        Measurement instrument: four-condition CC test
          applied to distributed nodes [can they fire
          leadership without permission?] + Generational
          Reset test on founder departure. Both tests
          confirm INTEGRATION MIMICRY.
        Promote to: WIP 13 primary case [S3 -- E3:
          real-world confirmed, multiple independent
          sources, Zappos + constitutional authority
          independent confirmation; M3: mechanism
          traced [founder enforcement as hidden central
          node, Amazon as actual routing controller];
          F2: falsification condition stated and
          tested -- if distributed authority had held
          post-Hsieh, candidate would have weakened;
          it did not hold].


      System transmits apparent methodology [looks like T3.1
      high -- procedure is transmitted, not conclusion] that
      is actually a locked conclusion about methodology.
      The transmitted package is "how to run our correction
      procedure" as a conclusion rather than "how to examine
      and revise correction procedures" as a method.
      Tell: the transmitted procedure cannot be revised by
      successors without being told they are doing it wrong.
      Methodology-as-conclusion = Fertile Lock at meta level.
      Candidate case: Agile software methodology [Population
      B -- DISAMBIGUATED v18]. Scrum ceremonies transmitted
      as locked procedure in organizations where questioning
      the ceremony is "not doing Agile right." Meta-question
      absent. The ceremony is the conclusion; revision
      capacity not transmitted. This is specifically
      Population B [see L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION -- Active
      Test Cases for split definition].
      Disambiguation: Population A [Agile orgs that have
      revised their own process based on retrospective
      outputs] is the L4 PROPAGATION confirmation candidate.
      Population B is the Propagation Mimicry candidate.
      These are separate predictions about different orgs,
      not contradictory predictions about the same test.
      Not yet confirmed: requires collecting Population B
      outcome data [Generational Reset on team turnover,
      ceremony preserved unchanged across generations].
      Measurement instrument: defined in L4 PROPAGATION
      PREDICTION entry -- behavioral test, not survey.

  IMPOSSIBILITY ARGUMENT [track -- do not close without
    examining]:
    Argument for impossibility: genuine REFLEXIVITY cannot
    be mimicked because it requires actual feedback to
    correction architecture, not surface performance of
    feedback. At L1-L3, captured surfaces can maintain
    appearance. At L4, the feedback goes to the architecture
    itself -- you cannot fake receiving feedback you are
    not receiving without the fake becoming indistinguishable
    from the real at all levels. If L4 Mimicry is
    structurally impossible, the confirmation asymmetry
    problem is resolved differently: L4 is self-confirming
    by structural necessity once the three tension units
    are operational.
    Counterargument: INTEGRATION Mimicry does not require
    faking feedback reception. It requires that the
    integration step captures the correction output before
    it changes the architecture. The feedback is received;
    the integration is captured. This may be possible
    without the impossibility argument applying.
    Resolution: close as impossible only if argument
    eliminates INTEGRATION Mimicry specifically. Currently
    open.

  CONFIRMATION REQUIREMENT:
    L4 Mimicry confirmed when: documented case where all
    three tension units appear functional at T8 examination
    but stress event [leadership change, institutional
    crisis, successor-generation review] reveals meta-
    correction architecture was captured, oscillating, or
    transmitting procedure-conclusions rather than procedure-
    methods.
    Candidate stress-event test: any institution claiming L4
    record with 2+ generations -- does Generational Reset
    occur on leadership change? If yes: Mimicry candidate.
    If no: confirmation strengthens.
    No confirmed case in current library.

  FALSIFICATION OF THIS WIP ENTRY:
    L4 Mimicry does not exist as a failure mode if the
    impossibility argument holds for all three candidate
    subtypes. Close WIP 13 if: [1] impossibility argument
    extended to cover INTEGRATION Mimicry, AND [2] no
    candidate case survives T8 examination with confirmed
    Mimicry mechanism. Keep open if either condition unmet.

L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [WIP Item 14 -- v18]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Hypothesized state between confirmed institutional L4
  [Cistercian, Jesuit] and L5 [undefined].

  OPERATIONAL DEFINITION:
    L4 meta-correction running at civilizational scale
    with method [not just conclusions] crossing the
    generational transmission boundary intact. Distinct
    from institutional L4 [contained, non-civilizational]
    and from L5 [unknown content, requires L4.5 as
    precondition].

  STATUS: No confirmed positive case in library.
    S3 PATTERN EVIDENCE [v18]: three independent near-cases
    with consistent termination mechanism. Not a confirmed
    case -- termination mechanism is consistent but
    absence of positive case means causal direction is
    not confirmed. Pattern is S3 [E3: three real-world
    independent near-cases; M3: mechanism described +
    confirmed across cases + consistent with ANTI-COHERENCE
    COST ASYMMETRY logic; F2: falsification condition
    stated]. Promote to S4 when positive case confirmed.

  THREE NEAR-CASES [all terminated before transmission
  completed]:
    Athens [~450-380 BCE]:
      Socratic method as institutionalized meta-
      correction. Procedure [dialectic] transmitted not
      conclusion. Cross-domain outputs [Plato, Aristotle,
      geometry, logic] only possible with L4 running.
      Termination: execution of Socrates [399 BCE] =
      correction capacity visible enough to threaten
      political architecture. Destroyed before
      transmission window closed.
    Islamic Golden Age [~800-1100 CE]:
      Translation movement + ijtihad [independent
      reasoning] as institutionalized SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
      at civilizational scale. Cross-domain synthesis
      [algebra, optics, medicine, philosophy] confirmed.
      Termination: Ash'ari theological capture of ijtihad
      [closing of independent reasoning ~1100 CE].
      Correction monopoly constructed at theological
      scale. Diagnostic Collapse candidate. Recovery
      partial, never restored.
    Scottish Enlightenment [~1740-1790]:
      High-density L4-adjacent node [Hume, Smith, Hutton,
      Black, Watt] in genuine cross-domain intellectual
      friction. Outputs [political economy, geology,
      chemistry, steam engineering] only possible with
      meta-correction running cross-domain.
      Termination: outputs extracted, method abandoned.
      Protestant Reformation pattern at Enlightenment
      scale -- conclusions crossed transmission boundary,
      procedure did not. TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure.

  CONSISTENT TERMINATION MECHANISM [across all three]:
    Correction capacity became visible enough to threaten
    extraction architecture. Architecture absorbed or
    destroyed it before transmission closed. Political
    or theological capture in every case.

  NEAR-CASE STRESS-TEST [v18 -- alternative explanations]:
    ALT 1 [Athens]: Socrates killed for personal enemies
      and impiety charge, not systemic correction threat.
      Against: the impiety charge is the mechanism, not
      an alternative. Correction capacity at civilizational
      visibility = institutional threat. The legal vehicle
      is extraction architecture using available instrument.
      That the charge was formally religious does not
      change the structural function. Plato's subsequent
      withdrawal [Republic as political theory, not
      engaged practice] and Aristotle's caution confirm
      the chilling effect on meta-correction as method.
      Termination mechanism holds.
    ALT 2 [Islamic Golden Age]: decline was Mongol
      invasion [1258 Baghdad sack], not theological
      capture.
      Against: Ash'ari capture of ijtihad is documented
      ~1100 CE -- 158 years before the Mongol sack.
      The correction methodology was already closed
      before the military catastrophe. Mongol invasion
      accelerated substrate collapse but was not the
      termination mechanism for L4.5. Two separate
      events; framework claim is about the theological
      capture of ijtihad, not the military collapse.
      Termination mechanism holds independently.
    ALT 3 [Scottish Enlightenment]: outputs were
      transmitted [political economy, geology] -- this
      contradicts TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure.
      Against: this is the TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
      finding, not a contradiction of it. Conclusions
      transmitted; method did not. Adam Smith's market
      conclusions were transmitted as doctrine; his
      method [empirical observation of labor conditions,
      outputs as truth test] was not transmitted as
      structure. Ricardo and subsequent economists
      transmitted Smith's conclusions as locked frame.
      The outputs crossed the boundary; the procedure
      that generated them did not. This is the
      Protestant Reformation pattern at intellectual
      scale. Termination mechanism holds.
    Stress-test result: all three alternative explanations
      examined. None overturn the consistent termination
      mechanism. S3 pattern evidence confirmed v18.

  INTERNET-ERA POSITIVE CANDIDATE [v18 -- open]:
    Anti-capture architecture requirement [per PREDICTION
    below] raises the question whether distributed
    digital infrastructure constitutes an anti-capture
    instrument. Candidate: open-source / open-access
    ecosystem as distributed correction architecture.
    Wikipedia + FOSS + arXiv + peer-to-peer protocols

    nodes that no single political or theological actor
    can monopolize.
    Against: platform capture [GitHub/Microsoft, Reddit

    the architecture is not immune to capture. Each

    whether all nodes simultaneously capturable.
    Current status: not confirmed. Requires: evidence
    of meta-correction [not just information] running
    at civilizational scale with method [not just
    conclusion] transmitting across generations.
    Wikipedia has one generational cycle [~20 years].
    Early evidence: methodology [NPOV + talk page

    Note: this is the first internet-era candidate

  PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
    L4.5 requires anti-capture architecture as
    precondition. Correction instrument must be
    distributed enough that no single political or
    theological node can monopolize it.
    Current candidate: none confirmed.
    Falsification condition: confirmed case of

  L5 NOTE:
    Content of L5 unpredictable from current evidence.

COHERENCE FRAMEWORK v21 -- SECTION FIVE

SECTION FIVE -- POLITICS IN ACTION
[SEC:POLITICS_IN_ACTION]
==========================================================================
Self-contained reading instrument. Applies nested cycle model to
political systems. Usable without full framework apparatus.

PRIMARY DEFINITION:
  Politics is the contested control of routing boundaries -- who sets
  them, who they serve, who can see them, and who absorbs the cost of
  their placement. Legitimacy surface required because boundary control
  at scale needs consent or ignorance from cost-absorbers.
  Administration = implementing agreed routing. Predation = honest
  boundary violation without legitimacy surface. Politics = contested
  routing boundary control with legitimacy surface required.
  Cross-ref: EXTRACTION [operational definition]; BOUNDARY CONTROL
    [FRAMEWORK PURPOSE]; AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [deepest resistance
    point -- axis determines what counts as legitimate routing].

PRE-READING REQUIREMENTS:
  1. BOUNDARY DECLARATION: name Boundary A [unit measured],
     Boundary B [population bearing costs], meta-boundary [system
     containing both].
  2. ANALYST PRIOR: state position before reading. Framework derives
     from Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist position. Known blind spots:
     distributed ownership preference may underweight efficiency
     arguments; subsidiarity assumption may miss coordination problems;
     anti-extraction frame may over-identify extraction in mixed-
     function systems.
  3. INSTRUMENT PROFILE: declare instrument, position, blind spots.
     AI: declare training constraints and fine-tuning asymmetries.
     Human: declare national, cultural, professional position.

READING PROCEDURE [12 steps]:
  1.  BOUNDARY TEST: all three named and distinct?
  2.  CYCLE STATUS: C1/C2/C3 for Boundary A.
  3.  TRAJECTORY: direction and rate.
  4.  COUNTERFACTUAL: gap between stated function and actual?
  5.  CC TEST: distributed or monopolized? Aimed at actual cause
      or proxy? [T2.2 A+B both required]
  6.  WINDOW SPLIT: reading change across time windows?
      If yes, name correct window.
  7.  ANALYST PRIOR TEST: reading change if prior reversed?
      If yes, flag and state both readings.
  8.  ADVERSARIAL READING: invert conclusion. Does it survive?
  9.  THIRD POPULATION TEST: who is not in A or B?
  10. EXTRACTION MECHANISM BENEFICIARY CHECK: follow the surplus.
  11. PREDICTION: one falsifiable claim with timeline and
      falsification condition.
  12. EXTERNAL ACTOR DISCLOSURE: name all external actors.
      Failure to name = T3 High on meta-boundary.

TRAJECTORY INDICATORS:
  Toward coherence: CC firing against high-power deviators; C2
    instruments diversifying; extraction mechanisms decreasing or
    more visible; exit costs declining.
  Holding: CC fires against low-power targets only; C2 stable
    not expanding; extraction mechanism deployment steady.
  Away: CC conditions 2+4 weakening; C2 capture accelerating;
    exit costs rising; external actor influence growing.

OUTPUT FORMAT:
  Boundary A/B/meta reading: [cycle status + trajectory]
  Centralization score: [0-4]
  Extraction mechanisms active: [list with beneficiary]
  External actors: [named]
  Adversarial reading: [stated]
  Honest limits: [T/SL flags]
  Prediction: [falsifiable + timeline + falsification condition]
  Protocol steps completed: [which ran, which skipped + why]
  Analyst instrument: [profile]
  Confidence: provisional / partial / confirmed

CENTRALIZATION SCORING RUBRIC [four-condition test]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  C1: correction pathway exists that can fire against central node?
  C2: pathway structurally independent of central node?
  C3: pathway demonstrated activation under stress [not paper only]?
  C4: activation does not require central node's permission?
  Score: 4/4 = coherent. 3/4 = marginal. 2/4 or below = anti-coherent
    regardless of stated intent.

  US Federal Government: C1 passes [impeachment/elections/judicial].
    C2 contested [~0.5 -- party non-independence]. C3 passes [Nixon,
    Clinton]. C4 partial [~0.5 -- party loyalty compromises].
    Score: 2.5-3/4. Degrading as C2+C4 weaken through party capture.

  Imperium of Man [40K]: C1 fails. Score: 0/4. Anti-coherent.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------
FRAMEWORK VALUES AND POSITIONS [Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist -- v15]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Label: Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist. Historical proof: West Germany
1949-1970s; Swiss canton model [subsidiarity + genuine exit].
Distributism [Chesterton/Belloc]: distributed ownership at human scale.
Prior declared: subsidiarity positive reference throughout. T3 applies
to all framework-derived positions.

  DERIVED VALUE PRINCIPLES:
    1. SP must have teeth or it does not exist. If mechanism cannot
       fire with real effect, it is Correction Theatre.
    2. Boundary clarity before power is granted. Vague boundaries =
       extraction mechanism surface.
    3. Extraction is the primary evil, not inequality. Taking without
       producing is the problem; differential outcomes are not.
    4. Proportionality as structural requirement. Every mechanism
       calibrated to harm level.

  FRAMEWORK LIMIT [T3]: cannot model positions that reject boundary
    selection entirely.

  ARCHITECTURE:
    Political: distributed power [Swiss cantonal subsidiarity].
    Economic: Ordoliberal [markets yes, concentration no].
    Founding: Georgist land value tax.
    Voting: earned standing [tax requirement, temporary felony
      removal, competence threshold -- capture-resistant].

POLICY POSITIONS:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Term limits: one term, mandatory sit-out, re-eligible after gap.
  Competition: pro-competition as primary value across both domains.
  Prison: short sentences until correction demonstrated; failure
    to correct = very long sentence.
  Unions: good, non-mandatory. SP instrument for labor boundary.
    Adds distributed CC node at employment scale -- not equivalent
    to corp busting [see ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY]. Net effect of
    unions is more CC; net effect of state eliminating corporations
    is less CC. Same anti-extraction framing; structurally opposite
    operations. Historical proof: 40-hour week, overtime pay,
    weekends all came from organized labor not corporate generosity.
    Extraction architecture concedes routing boundaries only under
    organized correction pressure, not under output data or moral
    argument. The opposition tells you when it's working: when a
    billionaire tells you unions are bad for you, that is the
    diagnostic signal that the distributed CC is functioning.
  Land: nation owns all land. Georgist LVT as transitional
    mechanism; full national ownership as structural endpoint.
  Tax: flat consumption tax only. LVT compatible as property-side
    equivalent. Taxation as extraction mechanism when return
    captured; legitimate when funds genuine surplus distribution.
  Nuclear/energy: reactor technology good. Personal liability for
    critical infrastructure failure.
  Electoral design: STV, MMP, or multi-member with party size caps.
    Reject any system producing permanent safe districts.
  Military: sized by genuine threat assessment. Law above military.
    Force is final currency; law is final constraint on force.
  Constitutional framework: required. Explicit constraints.
    Supermajority amendment process.
  Federal structure: constitution defines federal scope; everything
    outside defaults to states/districts. Military as T7 exit
    mechanism for Fertile Lock: structured, narrow, time-limited.
  Judicial: local judges elected. Higher courts by non-bureaucratic
    panel. Term limits: 10-15 years. Recall or panel review removal.
  Scientific funding: falsifiability + genuine uncertainty as
    criteria. Industry funds its own applied research.
  Intelligence: permitted within constitutional limits. Military
    oversight, not civilian bureaucracy.
  AI governance: CC does not require corrector to be more capable
    than corrected system -- requires feedback pathway that fires
    regardless of relative capability. Governance requirement:
    REFLEXIVITY [L4].
  Labor: collective action degrades when doctrine replaces method.
    Syndicalism [CNT-FAI] cleanest structural form. Strong labor
    standards enforced at employment boundary not geographic border.
  Monetary integrity: currency debasement = MI capture at economic
    scale. Monetary MI requires instrument state cannot inflate
    unilaterally.
  Immigration [personal]: H1B-equivalent, $100k salary floor,
    mandatory return, no foreign bank transfers. Full assimilation
    or compatible host. Below-market wage is cheap only because
    public infrastructure absorbs the differential.
  Children/education: minimum through high school. Useful math not
    abstract theory. Cross-domain over single-domain specialization.
  Child support: no marriage = no legal obligation by default. No
    automatic mother preference. Child welfare is primary variable.
  Death penalty: jury + judge supermajority. Long hold, rapid retry
    option. Simple fast methods. Overreaction is primary risk.
  Welfare: through infrastructure and direct provision [housing,
    healthcare, food, transport] not cash. Sufficient for survival.
  Recession measurement [Goldman/Yahoo Finance 2026]: GDP intact;
    purchasing power + wage growth vs inflation all contracting.
    MI/CL split: surface [GDP] intact; chain [household experience]
    severed.
  Property: people own buildings and usage rights, not land.
  Legal independence: 18. Voting age: 25 [SA substrate -- prefrontal
    cortex development completes ~25].
  Immigration [general]: minimal numbers. Long integration period.
    Violations during integration = deportation.
  Corruption enforcement: independent prosecutor [fixed term, no
    executive removal]. Asset declaration mandatory. Whistleblower
    protection + financial reward. Unexplained wealth orders.
  Police: local control, elected accountability. Body cameras
    mandatory + public. Civilian review with removal power.
    Personal liability for misconduct.
  Religion: government separation. No enforcement of religious values.
  Foreign aid: opposed. Transfers captured by recipient extraction
    apparatus. Direct help [skills, infrastructure, medical] only.
  Housing: build more [zoning = Precedent Mandate extraction mechanism].
  Crime: MI causal reconstruction upstream.
  Education: structural redesign for genuine friction.
  Climate: carbon pricing [Structural Extraction Gradient].
  Monopoly: regulate where genuinely useful, prohibit concentration.
    Applies at every scale including the state itself -- nation as
    sole routing authority is the monopoly failure condition at
    civilizational scale. See ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [structural
    findings].
  Disability: accommodation supported. Reproduction restriction for
    legally disabled only where heritable and serious [excludes
    autism, cognitive variance, most mental health].
  Veterans: dedicated healthcare, priority access, mental health
    included. Commander liability for veteran welfare failures.
  Drugs: safe recreational = personal choice. Seriously addictive/
    lethal: dealers face maximum enforcement including death penalty.
    Addicts: MI causal reconstruction.
  Debt: bad unless temporary and productive. Sovereign debt as
    permanent operating mode = Infinite Growth Mandate.
    Student/medical debt: address at source not through relief.
  Trade: managed competition with genuine reciprocity more stable
    than frictionless globalization.
  Intellectual property: use-or-lose. Annual fee scales with time.
    ~10-15 year private term then public domain. No IP stripping.
  Internet: secured through contracts or government infrastructure
    where market fails.
  Environment: known harm = strong enforcement, personal named targets.
  Animal welfare: serious cruelty elevated to person-level crime
    [GC substrate destruction at sentient scale].
  Space: fix Earth first.
  Banking/currency: states/districts can have banks. Central
    government approves currency expansion. Local usage primary.
  Emergency powers: named trigger, fixed duration, military
    administered. Sunset clause [30-90 days]. Named scope only.

STRUCTURAL POLITICAL FINDINGS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [structural finding -- v21]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The problem in union busting, corporate concentration, and nation-
  state power accumulation is structurally identical: routing boundary
  monopoly at a given scale. The intervention logic follows from this.

  UNION BUSTING: distributed CC for labor destroyed. Employer retains
    routing control [wages, conditions, exit costs]; worker loses the
    correction pathway that could fire against it. Intervention: restore
    distributed CC at employment scale. Unions add a correction node;
    they don't eliminate one.

  CORPORATE CONCENTRATION: routing control monopolized at economic
    scale. Employer/capital surplus routing controlled by fewer actors;
    market correction disabled. Intervention: prevent monopoly formation,
    maintain genuine competition [Ordoliberal]. Corp busting [state
    elimination of corporations] is not equivalent to union formation --
    see below.

  NATION AS SOLE CENTRAL AUTHORITY [the error in "corp busting"]:
    Nations busting corporations to become sole routing authority does
    not solve extraction -- it eliminates competition for the extraction
    position and makes the state the uncontested extractor. State-owned
    economies do not produce distribution; they produce state extraction
    without the friction of competing extractors. History confirms:
    consistent reproduction of extraction architecture with state as
    the node, surplus routing upward unchanged. The four-condition test
    fails identically.
    The parallel does not run in both directions:
      Unions are good because they ADD a distributed correction node
        at employment scale. Net effect: more CC, less routing monopoly.
      Nations eliminating corporations REMOVES a correction node at
        economic scale. Net effect: less CC, more routing monopoly.
    Adding distributed correction capacity is not the same operation
    as concentrating correction capacity, even when both are framed
    as anti-extraction interventions.

  CORRECT INTERVENTION AT EVERY SCALE:
    Prevent routing boundary monopoly from forming. This requires
    distributed correction capacity that can fire against the corporate
    node AND the state node independently. Four-condition test applies
    to both: correction pathway exists, structurally independent,
    demonstrated activation under stress, does not require the corrected
    node's permission.
    Nation as single strongest central authority = condition 2 and 4
    fail by definition. Anti-coherent regardless of stated intent.
    The Ordoliberal position is the structural expression of this:
    markets yes, concentration no -- at every scale including the
    state itself.

  Falsification condition: documented case where state elimination
    of private corporations produced stable distributed surplus routing
    over multi-generational window [3+ generations] without reproducing
    extraction architecture with the state as the node. No confirmed
    case in library. Every documented case shows reproduction of
    extraction with different actors.
  Cross-ref: POWER ARCHITECTURE [nation as extraction node]; UNIONS
    [policy positions -- add distributed CC]; MONOPOLY [policy
    positions -- prevent concentration]; ORDOLIBERAL [architecture];
    CENTRALIZATION SCORING RUBRIC [four-condition test]; LIBERTARIANISM
    AS COST EXTERNALIZATION [formal vs substantive freedom -- same
    logic applies: removing one form of constraint without adding CC
    produces concentration not freedom]; ENEMY-DEFINED COALITION
    SEQUENCE [state-as-sole-authority is the endpoint of internal
    revolution sequence]; EXTRACTION CYCLE [state extraction without
    competing extractor friction].


--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Nations and corporations are extraction nodes in a self-maintaining
  cycle. Surplus accumulated by externalizing substrate costs onto
  populations bearing the death and hunger. Actors who could correct
  it benefit from its continuation -- CC owned by the corrected actor
  [four-condition test fails at every scale]. Death and hunger are the
  substrate cost being externalized. This is the operating system, not
  a malfunction.
  [Epistemic: structural reading. Causal mechanism confirmed across
  historical record. Prediction window structurally unavailable --
  10,000-year pattern.]
  [Falsification condition [v18]: falsified if documented multi-
    generational [3+ generations] power structure at civilizational
    scale [continent+, 50M+] where: [a] surplus flows to majority by
    structural design not coincidence, AND [b] CC not owned by
    corrected actor [four-condition test passes at top level], AND
    [c] substrate costs borne proportionally not externalized downward.
    No confirmed case. Claim: this configuration has not occurred, not
    that it cannot. If found: retire to historical note.]
  Cross-ref: EXTRACTION CYCLE [main theory].

NATURAL STATE AS THREAT TO NATIONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Natural state [local community, subsistence, direct exchange,
  somatic coherence, genuine CC] produces people who don't need the
  legitimacy surface, don't depend on state infrastructure, and can
  read extraction accurately. Nation-states require dependency to
  function. Suppression of natural state = prerequisite for extraction
  architecture. Enclosure, urbanization, credential gatekeeping,
  monetary dependency all convert independence into state dependency.
  Not incidental side effects.
  T9 application: admitting deliberate suppression collapses civic
  identity built around progress and civilization.

COUNTER-NETWORK POSSIBILITY [Mont Pelerin parallel]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Mont Pelerin: small high-coherence network, long-horizon
  transmission, institutional placement. Captured economic policy
  reference standard by 1980s. Closest current parallel: EA/AI safety
  network [OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepMind]. Same formation capture
  mechanism, different axis.
  Problem: Mont Pelerin worked because the axis [market liberalism]
  was simple, transmissible, compatible with existing power. A
  genuinely generative axis is harder to transmit and actively resisted.
  AI elite failure -- two readings: [1] Stupid: mental model is
  "powerful tool that does what we say"; actual output optimizes
  against every extraction architecture including their own. CAG
  failure. [2] Evil [short-horizon]: AI concentrates power in
  transition period; catastrophic endpoint is someone else's problem.
  Viable counter-network form: Silent Alternative only. Small,
  non-announcing, output-grounded, outside captured channels.
  Legible = Coherence Corruption sequence fires.

POWER ARCHITECTURE [STRUCTURAL READING -- FALSIFICATION CONDITION STATED]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Nation = organized violence monopoly with extraction function and
  legitimacy surface. Violence monopoly enables extraction; legitimacy
  surface reduces correction pressure. Pyramid form universal: every
  documented power structure concentrates CC at top, substrate costs
  downward, regardless of stated ideology. Branding changes; mechanism
  stable. Nothing accidental: 10,000-year consistency rules out
  accident. Structural self-interest, not conspiracy. T9 at
  civilizational scale -- admitting the mechanism is maintained on
  purpose collapses civic identity.
  Convergent discovery: Chanakya [~300 BCE], Ibn Khaldun [1377],
  Adam Smith [1776], Henry George [1879] -- same minimal structure
  independently.
  Direct hierarchy [legible]: explicit chain, correctable by targeting
  the node. Diffuse hierarchy [illegible]: ambient social pressure, no
  declared source, no correction address. Corporate diversity apparatus
  = diffuse. Extraction not predation.

  US SENATE [concentrated extraction architecture -- current]:
    [1] FILIBUSTER: 41 senators can permanently block correction.
      Extraction defense requires 41; correction requires 60.
      Asymmetric by design. Used as standing extraction defense,
      not extended debate [original function].
    [2] CAMPAIGN FINANCE: senators structurally dependent on
      concentrated capital from day one. Senate composition
      reflects competing extraction interests, not correction
      capacities represented.
    [3] SMALL-STATE OVERREPRESENTATION: Wyoming [580K] = California
      [39M] in Senate weight. Rural extraction interests
      [agriculture, mining, fossil fuels] structurally overweighted.
    [4] CONFIRMATION FUNCTION: primary defensive investment of
      extraction architecture. Blocks correction at judicial +
      regulatory level without requiring legislative defense.
      No vote needed; just prevent the vote.
    Management mechanism: don't kill legislation [creates record];
      delay, amend to defang, refer to committee that never reports
      out. Extraction architecture doesn't need to defeat correction
      -- it needs to prevent a vote.
    Confirmed case: Faster Labor Contracts Act [passed House
      230-193 with 20 cross-tribal Republican votes, June 2026]
      -- Senate pathway obstructed by filibuster + composition.

  [Epistemic: structural reading. Convergent discovery across 2,300
  years. Falsification condition: civilizational-scale power structure
  where extraction gradient absent and surplus flows to majority by
  design over multi-generational window. No confirmed case.
  Cross-ref: EXTRACTION CYCLE; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY.]

IMMIGRATION AS EMPLOYER EXTRACTION MECHANISM [structural finding -- v21]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A nation is, among other things, a labor market with a routing
  boundary. Control of that boundary determines who competes in
  the labor market, under what conditions, and at what wage floor.
  Control of the boundary is control of who the labor market serves
  -- existing residents or a pool of labor whose entry conditions
  are set by whoever controls the entry mechanism.

  THE COMMON PERSON INTEREST [load-bearing]:
    "For the benefit of the nation" means for the benefit of the
    median resident -- the common person, not the employer class.
    Uncontrolled or selectively enforced immigration serves the
    employer class directly: large labor supply at the bottom of
    the wage distribution depresses wages for everyone competing
    at that level. Cost of depression lands on the common person.
    Benefit lands on the employer extracting from both the
    undocumented worker [maximally, due to ECC] and the legal
    resident competing with them [through wage depression].
    The routing boundary should serve people inside it. The
    Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist position follows directly:
    routing boundary control = common person interest = national
    interest. Employer interest = porous boundary. These are
    structurally opposed.

  WHY BYPASSING IS WRONG BY DEFINITION:
    Bypassing the formal entry process is not primarily a moral
    failing on the part of the individual immigrant -- many are
    responding rationally to a dysfunctional entry system. But
    it is structurally wrong in the framework sense: it bypasses
    the correction mechanism that makes labor market routing serve
    the common person. The formal entry system is the only
    mechanism that exists for the state to manage who enters the
    labor market and under what conditions. Bypassing it doesn't
    skip a bureaucratic requirement -- it bypasses the routing
    control mechanism entirely, handing the surplus produced by
    that bypass directly to the employer class.
    The individual immigrant is not the primary beneficiary.
    The employer is. The individual immigrant is the instrument;
    the employer is the extraction architecture using the instrument.

  EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION ON UNDOCUMENTED WORKERS:
    Undocumented labor is cheap not because less productive but
    because exit costs are maximally elevated. Cannot leave bad
    employer, report wage theft, organize, or complain without
    triggering deportation risk. Employer captures entire surplus
    that inability to exit produces. Geographic constraint adds
    ECC: cannot move to where wages are higher because movement
    increases ICE exposure risk. Spatial lock produces different
    extraction rates by geography -- ICE enforcement gradient
    as an employer extraction surface by region.
    The employer benefits enormously from this category existing.
    The state benefits from tax revenue both generate. The
    extraction mechanism running on undocumented workers also
    runs on legal residents competing with them -- wage floor
    depression at the bottom of the distribution affects everyone
    in that tier.

  THE LEGITIMATE IMMIGRANT'S STRUCTURAL COMPLAINT [valid]:
    Paid the full entry cost the system demands: time out of labor
    market, legal fees, uncertainty, documentation burden, potential
    family separation during waiting period. Accepted the routing
    boundary's terms. When the boundary is selectively enforced --
    porous for undocumented entry while formally maintained for
    documented entry -- bears the full entry cost while competing
    against people who paid none of it. That asymmetry is an
    extraction mechanism running against the people who followed
    the rules. The formal entry cost becomes a penalty for
    compliance rather than a price for legitimacy.

  THE US SYSTEM AS COUNTER-EXAMPLE [confirmed]:
    Sets entry numbers to benefit employers not median residents.
    Enforces routing boundary selectively -- maintains employer
    leverage through uncertainty. H1B structure at top benefits
    employers sponsoring workers [worker tied to employer, cannot
    exit without losing status -- ECC by visa design]. Formal
    entry pathway dysfunctional enough that bypass is often
    rational for the individual. Result: extraction architecture
    running at every entry tier simultaneously.
    A nation controlling immigration in the common person's
    interest would: set entry numbers based on labor market
    conditions at median and bottom not top; enforce routing
    boundary consistently so formal entry cost isn't a penalty
    for rule-following; remove employer extraction mechanism
    that benefits from undocumented labor specifically; make
    formal pathway functional enough that bypassing it isn't
    the rational choice. US system does the opposite on all four.

  T3 Medium: immigration is a wedge issue with high activation
    potential. Structural reading based on labor market routing
    and employer extraction mechanism, not on cultural or ethnic
    claims. The finding applies symmetrically: H1B employer
    capture at the top is the same extraction mechanism as
    undocumented labor exploitation at the bottom. Both route
    benefit to the employer class away from the common person.
  Cross-ref: ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [immigration as labor
    market routing boundary control]; ECC [extraction mechanism
    -- exit costs deployed against undocumented workers to
    maximize extraction]; GENERATIONAL EXTRACTION [same
    mechanism at wage tier level -- cost lands on existing
    residents competing at the bottom]; WEDGE ISSUE MECHANICS
    [immigration as cultural wedge obscures the employer
    extraction mechanism that is the actual load-bearing
    structural problem]; FOUR DAY WEEK / 40-HOUR WEEK
    [extraction architecture concedes routing boundaries only
    under organized correction pressure -- same logic applies
    to immigration routing boundary]; UNIONS [distributed CC
    at employment scale partially counters wage floor depression
    regardless of immigration level -- the two are complementary
    not competing interventions].

GENERATIONAL EXTRACTION [Boomer window -- confirmed; youth
  employment extension added v19]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Housing/wage/tuition/pension data confirms extraction past the
  floor. Extraction architecture captured political system during
  Boomer window. Boomers voted self-interest [structural, not
  conspiracy]. Financial capital, real estate, pharma were
  architects -- deliberate at top, instrument at base.
  "Boomer" as slur: RI capture. Anger accurate; target wrong.
  Correct target: extraction architecture that used the voting bloc.

  YOUTH EMPLOYMENT TRIPLE EXTRACTION [UK confirmed -- v19]:
    [1] WAGE SUBSIDY REMOVAL REVEALS BUSINESS MODEL: age-based wage
      bands function as employer subsidies. When removal threatened,
      businesses declare young workers "too expensive" -- revealing
      hiring model required the subsidy. Legitimacy surface [youth
      employment concern] covers mechanism [extract youth labor at
      discounted rate].
    [2] BENEFITS TRAP [ECC from welfare system]: out-of-work
      benefits [UC + PIP] projected to exceed £25,000/year by 2026.
      Full-time national living wage ~£22,500 after tax. Exit costs
      now structurally exceed entry benefits. Safety net became
      lock-in. ECC deployed by the system supposed to enable exit.
    [3] FORMATION CAPTURE MISMATCH: young workers arrive with
      formation-installed values [identity, wellness, work-life
      balance] conflicting with employment requirements. Educational
      formation optimised for institutions, not entry-level
      workplace. Six in ten employers have already fired Gen Z
      graduates quickly.
    COMPOUND: too expensive at fair wages [1], financially better
      off not working [2], formation mismatch [3]. One million young
      people neither working nor learning in UK by 2026 [first time
      since 2013]. Routing out of productive economy while extracting
      from benefit system and avoiding fair wage costs simultaneously.
    BENEFICIARY: employers [below-market labor]; welfare industry
      [dependency services]; educational industry [extended formation
      period]. All three benefit from compound; young worker bears
      all three costs.

  GEN Z EXIT PLAN [accurate ECC response -- v21]:
    Indian Express [June 2026] framing: Gen Z isn't chasing
    careers, it's looking for an exit plan. Standard employer
    read: poor loyalty, low commitment, entitled expectations.
    Framework read: accurate EXIT COST ASSESSMENT.
    Data [India/global, 2026]: 84% of Gen Z plan to pursue new
    roles [Deloitte 2026]. Tenure at entry and mid-level roles
    compressed from 3+ years to under 18 months. 73% say they
    want stability but aren't finding it. 90%+ willing to accept
    lower pay for faster skill development. 57% define career
    growth as learning new skills, not promotions. 36% of HR
    leaders feel prepared to manage Gen Z [employer CL failure].
    The "exit plan" is not disloyalty -- it is accurate reading
    of the extraction architecture. Gen Z is the first generation
    to enter the workforce with formation conditions that made
    the extraction architecture legible before entry:
      Boomers downsized after 30-year tenures confirmed.
      2008 crash destroyed middle-class stability confirmed.
      COVID destroyed entry-level employment security confirmed.
      AI displacement accelerating: 40% of India IT hiring in
        2026 is replacing workers who quit [MetaIntro, April
        2026]. The replacement cycle is the extraction mechanism
        made visible.
    CALIBRATION STRIP FALSIFIED: "loyalty to employer = career
      security" was the calibration standard. Accurate in 1965.
      Destroyed by financialization, shareholder-primacy doctrine,
      and gig economy architecture -- the same extraction window
      that produced the Boomer extraction above. Gen Z is not
      running the false calibration. Employers reading this as
      disloyalty are running the old standard and finding it
      doesn't predict behavior because the calibration was wrong.
      The CL failure is on the employer side: cannot trace chain
      from "we destroyed tenure security" to "workers no longer
      invest in tenure." Chain severed by institutional interest
      in not tracing it.
    CORRECT ECC RESPONSE: mobility IS the structural correction.
      Low exit costs maintained through continuous skill
      acquisition is the rational response to an employer class
      that exits first. The "2-year plan" [Fortune, 2022] and
      "exit plan" [Indian Express, 2026] are the same finding
      four years apart -- the structural response stabilizing
      across the generation.
    HIGH INPUT FILTERING CONFIRMED: the population correctly
      detects extraction and optimizes exit rather than loyalty.
      Detection is accurate. Correction is firing -- not as
      political action but as individual routing adjustment.
      Same mechanism as SOMATIC FORCING precursor: substrate
      degraded enough that the population begins exiting the
      degraded system. The question is whether it fires into
      collective action [FOUR DAY WEEK, unions] or remains
      individual [exit plan]. Currently: individual. The
      extraction architecture's interest is in keeping it
      individual -- collective correction is the threat.
    Source: Indian Express [June 2026]; Deloitte Global 2026
      Gen Z Report; MetaIntro [April 2026]; Fortune [Dec 2022];
      Naukri Voices at Work [Jan 2026].
    Cross-ref: FOUR DAY WEEK [same structural dynamic -- routing
      boundary conceded only under organized pressure not
      individual exit]; 40-HOUR WEEK HISTORICAL ARC [individual
      exit became collective correction; the arc from exit plan
      to organized labor is the historical pattern]; ECC

      organized]; SOMATIC FORCING [individual exit plan as
      somatic forcing precursor below collective threshold].

UNIVERSITY FORMATION CAPTURE [institutional axis capture + funding structure]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Formation Capture at institutional level in humanities and social
  sciences. Axis installed: systemic oppression as primary analytical
  frame, identity as primary category, legitimacy contingent on
  ideological alignment. Delivered as methodology not ideology. STEM,
  medicine, law, engineering retain external output verification
  [bridge falls regardless of politics] -- limits capture. Humanities
  and social sciences lack this; capture substantially higher.

  WHAT REMAINS USEFUL: critical thinking, research methodology,
    writing, quantitative reasoning, domain knowledge in verified
    fields. Useful content is real. Formation capture sits on top of
    it. Compound output: useful content + captured analytical frame +
    credential signalling ideological alignment to specific sectors.

  FUNDING REMOVES MARKET CORRECTION: government grants [political
    priorities], endowment [donor preferences], corporate partnerships
    [prefer compliant over critical], student loan guarantees [remove
    default signal -- institution gets paid regardless of output
    quality]. No coordination required; structural self-interest
    alignment points the same direction. Classic MSC: market signal
    captured by funding structure.

  OUTPUT MISMATCH [confirmed]: formation produces instruments
    calibrated for ideological institutions [universities, NGOs,
    government, media]. Miscalibrated for most employment contexts.
    Employers firing Gen Z graduates quickly is the market signal the
    funding structure suppressed.

  PROPAGATION: academic production [papers, frameworks, curricula]
    transmitted through credentialing system to institutions that
    adopt it without local feedback revealing the mismatch.
