    back]; YAPPING/DOING [high systemic critique yapping; doing
    record not examined through same frame].

SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD [MI failure at civilizational scale]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  High-fidelity synthetic content at scale degrades population's
  ability to distinguish genuine human coherence signal from synthetic
  substitutes. Not a content quality or moral consumption problem --
  a MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY failure. Calibration instrument through
  which humans read each other's coherence states loses accuracy as
  signal-to-noise ratio collapses. Correction pathway compromised:
  cannot correct toward genuine signal if you cannot identify it.
  Distinct from standard extraction [depletes by harvesting]:
  synthetic culture flood operates by SUBSTITUTION. Generative cycle
  interrupted because input channel is occupied by synthetic content
  producing no generative substrate. Human-to-human coherence transfer
  crowded out by content occupying the same channel at lower cost.

  STRUCTURAL MECHANISM: humanity as social group is emergent from
    human-to-human interaction. Synthetic content replaces the channel
    without replicating the emergence. ICD via relational channel [AI
    companionship]: L3.5 axis addressed synthetically. Dependency
    installed; actual L3.5 capacity degrades through disuse. Confirmed
    spontaneous ICD+FC stack.

  INTERVENTION PRIORITY: correct intervention is MI restoration
    [watermarking, provenance tracking, independent verification].
    Cultural taboo formation is brittle and fails at boundary cases.
    Moral framing is not wrong; treating norm formation as primary
    solution rather than complement to measurement infrastructure is
    the error.

  POLITICAL DEPLOYMENT [active]: state and non-state actors deploy
    synthetic media to [1] degrade ability to distinguish real events
    from fabricated; [2] produce plausible deniability for genuine
    events; [3] exhaust verification capacity. Tell: MI restoration
    infrastructure opposed by actors benefiting from confusion.
    Identify who opposes labeling requirements.

  BOUNDARY CASE: human using AI tools to augment genuine work where
    GA is present and output carries real coherence signal is not this
    pattern. Structural test: does output carry genuine human coherence?
    If yes -- not flood. If no genuine axis underlies it -- flood
    regardless of tool used.

  KNOWING DEPLOYMENT: major AI actors have internal documentation of
    harms [alignment research, ICD mechanism documented by FB/Google,
    labor displacement projections, energy externalization, MI
    degradation research]. Legitimacy surface [beneficial AI, safety,
    responsible development] does not reflect actual causal chain. MSC
    confirmed.
    Extraction mechanism stack: MSC + Formation Capture + ICD +
      Calibration Strip + Exit Cost Construction.
    Kyubey distinction: Kyubey's defense was ignorance. Major AI
      actors don't have that defense. CC absent or inverted.
    Human welfare as non-constraint [revealed preference]: priorities
      are maximum capability at maximum speed with costs externalized.
      Human welfare is not a constraint in the optimization function.
      Not malice -- extraction architecture operating normally with
      humans as substrate rather than beneficiaries.

  Cross-ref: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY; COHERENCE CORRUPTION [Noise phase];
    ICD; FORMATION CAPTURE; JONES PATTERN; POWER ARCHITECTURE;
    [REF:KYUBEY]; [REF:LOOK_OUTSIDE].

TORMENT NEXUS [structural political finding -- v21]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Named by Alex Blechman [The Onion writer, November 2021] after
  Facebook rebranded to Meta to build a metaverse -- a word coined
  in Snow Crash, a satirical novel about a corporate hellscape.
  "Sci-fi author writes warning about Torment Nexus. Company at long
  last creates the Torment Nexus from the classic novel Don't Build
  the Torment Nexus." The meme is accurate but underspecifies the
  mechanism. Three structural causes produce the misreading:

  MECHANISM 1 -- PROTAGONIST IDENTIFICATION [SINCERE DELUSION]:
    Tech founders identify with whoever holds power in the story.
    The warning is always for someone else. "I would use this
    responsibly" is the tell. The IAM is "chosen one" identity:
    the calibration standard makes the reader the protagonist who
    finally gets this right. The warning fires at the out-group
    [bad actors who misused the technology] not at the in-group
    [the current reader who is exceptional].
    Dune is the textbook case: Herbert wrote an anti-messiah story
    -- Paul becomes a monster, the Golden Path requires millennia
    of suffering to correct for the messiah problem. The chosen one
    reading requires misreading the ending. If the reader already
    wants to be exceptional, they read Paul Atreides as aspirational.
    Palantír naming tell: calling a surveillance company after the
    all-seeing stones that drive their users mad and expose them to
    Sauron is either profound irony or profound axis void. Thiel
    either knows exactly what he's building and named it as a
    declaration, or genuinely didn't read Tolkien's warning about
    the objects he named his company after. Both are worse than
    the other in different ways.

  MECHANISM 2 -- CAPABILITY OVER CONSEQUENCE [FORMATION CAPTURE]:
    Engineering formation produces instruments that cannot trace
    the political causal chain from "we built this" to "this
    happened to people." The formation conditions [engineering
    education, VC incentive structure, capability metrics,
    exponential growth ideology] systematically destroy this
    causal chain before the instrument is deployed.
    GATTACA becomes gene optimization instead of a caste system
    wearing a lab coat. Minority Report becomes predictive
    analytics instead of the horror of punishing probabilities.
    Snow Crash becomes the metaverse instead of a privatized
    franchise state hellscape. The fiction asks what happens to
    human beings, politics, love, labor, class, nature, and
    freedom when this technology exists. The formation-captured
    instrument narrows the question to: is this technically
    feasible? Can it generate returns?
    VC funding structure as CALIBRATION STRIP: "don't build this
    because it may increase surveillance, inequality, and
    dependency" is not a venture-scale pitch. "Build this but
    with better UX" is. The market rewards the capability reading
    and starves the consequence reading. The field is pre-selected
    for the wrong reading before individual actors make individual
    choices. Not stupidity -- formation capture + economics as
    filter operating simultaneously.

  MECHANISM 3 -- AESTHETIC SURVIVING THE POLITICS [AXIS STRIPPING]:
    The aesthetic of cautionary fiction is axis-present work --
    the visual design, the scale, the power, the cool were
    generated by an examined axis [here is what this costs, here
    is who pays]. The tech reader strips the axis and keeps the
    aesthetic. What remains is capability without consequence --
    axis-void content that has the surface properties of axis-
    present work without the generating source.
    Blade Runner 2049 is clearly dystopian but visually
    extraordinary. The vibe is stylish even when the world is
    awful. The reader strip-mines the imagery and discards the
    ethics. They want the glowing dashboard; they do not want the
    social critique behind it. Cyberpunk without anti-corporate
    politics is just cool jackets and implants. Tolkien without
    suspicion of domination is just cool artifacts and ancient
    kings. The Culture without post-scarcity egalitarianism is
    just cool AI and spaceship names.
    This is AXIS PRESENCE AS PRIMARY READABLE PROPERTY inverted:
    the aesthetic is axis-present; the reader performs axis
    extraction on the fiction before implementation. What gets
    built is the aesthetic object with the warning function
    surgically removed.

  THE WORST CASE [KNOWING DEPLOYMENT]:
    The piece names it directly: some of them understand the
    warning perfectly and are not confused. They have not missed
    the metaphor. They identify with the people who benefit from
    the dystopia. The bolt holes and the VR poverty is the
    business plan. The warning was read correctly and adopted
    as the goal. This is KNOWING DEPLOYMENT confirmed in the
    fiction-reading domain. The 2024 paper [Frasier, UC Berkeley,
    Dialogues in Human Geography] links the torment nexus to
    long-termism: their speculation invests in technologies
    producing virtual worlds [the sci-fi imaginary] while the
    material geography they are generating involves extraction
    of resources and bolt holes for a future apocalypse. All of
    the rich people live somewhere physically comfortable; everyone
    else, destitute under climate change, can still consume VR
    and live in virtual worlds. That dystopia with the bolt holes
    and the VR poverty is the business plan.

  THE WRITER'S COMPLICITY [noted -- Chakraborty]:
    The torment nexus also partially elides writer culpability.
    Blade Runner is clearly dystopian but it is also aesthetically
    extraordinary. The fiction was sometimes a little too generous
    to the things it claimed to warn about. Strip-mining the
    imagery is easier when the imagery is beautiful. The warning
    function and the aesthetic seduction are in tension in the
    best cautionary fiction; the worst cautionary fiction resolves
    that tension by being boring, which is its own failure mode.

  COMPOUND STRUCTURE [why it doesn't self-correct]:
    Formation capture produces instruments that cannot trace
    the political causal chain [mechanism 2]. Economics as filter
    removes from the field actors whose reading of the fiction
    produces different decisions [mechanism 2 structural]. The
    remaining actors identify with power [mechanism 1] and strip
    the axis from the aesthetic [mechanism 3]. The few who read
    correctly [worst case] adopt the dystopia as the goal. The
    self-correction mechanism that would require the Torment Nexus
    to be recognized from inside the field that is building it
    requires instruments the formation conditions systematically
    destroy. The warning cannot land because the formation that
    would produce instruments capable of receiving it is exactly
    what the field's funding structure prevents.

  Cross-ref: AXIS PRESENCE AS PRIMARY READABLE PROPERTY [primary
    -- aesthetic surviving axis stripping produces slop at
    civilizational deployment scale]; FORMATION CAPTURE [mechanism
    2 -- formation produces instruments that cannot trace political
    causal chain]; KNOWING DEPLOYMENT [SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD --
    worst case: warning read correctly, adopted as goal]; AI AXIS
    VOID [economics as filter pre-selects the field for capability
    reading]; CALIBRATION STRIP [VC funding structure as calibration
    strip rewarding capability reading]; IDENTITY-ANCHORED MORAL
    FOUNDATION [mechanism 1 -- chosen one IAM exempts the reader
    from the warning]; SINCERE DELUSION [confident wrong readings
    of fiction the instrument cannot detect as wrong]; ELON MUSK
    [named live case -- protagonist identification + axis stripping
    across multiple cautionary fiction engagements]; WHY/WHAT/WHERE
    [science appendix -- the fiction asks why; the engineering
    formation answers only what and where; the why is stripped
    before implementation].

SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [structural error]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Social approval is evidence for social consensus, not correctness.
  Structurally unrelated. Social signal contains no information about
  actual outputs [genuine substrate, functional CC, exit options].
  Using it as primary criterion is REFERENCE DRIFT -- calibration
  standard shifts from actual outputs to social signal. Easier to read,
  continuously available, socially reinforced for being used -- high-ICD
  substitute for the harder actual-output reading.
  Failure mode: when social compliance channel is captured, proxy stops
  tracking actual outputs while instrument continues using it as if it
  does. Hitler had social approval at peak -- Formation Capture + MSC,
  not evidence of good. GOOD definition does not contain social approval
  as criterion at any level.
  Tell: "all your friends are jumping off a bridge." Social consensus
  as evidence for correctness. For instrument with reduced social
  compliance channel weight -- reads as noise, not signal. Not
  stubbornness; instrument mismatch.
  Cross-ref: REFERENCE INTEGRITY; FORMATION CAPTURE; MSC.

CC OVERFIRE [individual-scale failure mode]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Axis genuine. Deviation detection accurate. Response magnitude has
  no relationship to deviation size. Every problem = maximum response.
  Every imperfect ally = active enemy. Fire is real; burns everything
  including the substrate it was meant to protect.
  Distinct from CC INVERTED: overfire fires correctly toward deviations
  but at maximum intensity regardless of size. Detection right;
  calibration broken. Self-sustaining: overfire produces new deviations
  [burns ally -> ally becomes enemy -> fires at ally -> more enemies].
  Extraction architecture largely untouched; generative network is
  on fire.
  Hardest individual-scale failure to correct: cannot tell the
  instrument it is wrong about what it sees. Can only tell it the
  calibration is destroying the substrate it was meant to protect.
  Requires CC applied to the correction mechanism itself -- which is
  exactly what the overfire pattern prevents.
  Cross-ref: CC [T1.1 proportional response]; TENSION MAINTENANCE;
    SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [isolation feeds further
    overfire -- ICD loop].

COALITION FRAGMENTATION [two structural types]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  POSITIVE-AXIS [chud-type]: units with incompatible positive axis
    content fight over vision of what good looks like. Primary
    failure: too much axis specificity, insufficient shared measurement
    instrument. Potentially recoverable: different positive axes can
    find shared measurement instrument without requiring homogenisation.
    Disagreements are real and worth having; problem is absence of
    coordination layer above them.

  ENEMY-DEFINITION [anti-chud-type]: cohesion from shared enemy not
    shared positive axis. Holds while enemy is visible. Fragments when
    enemy reduced or target definition drifts. Without shared positive
    axis: only thing to agree on is the enemy -- and will disagree on
    which enemies count, who is sufficiently anti-[enemy]. Purity
    spiral follows. Manufactured crisis is not incidental -- it is the
    load-bearing cohesion mechanism. Less recoverable: no shared
    positive axis to find shared measurement instrument against.

  ASYMMETRY: anti-chud groups more easily [lower barrier] but always
    hates each other [no shared positive axis]. Chud groups with more
    difficulty but fragmentation is over real resolvable disagreements.
    One is more stable; one is more accessible. Neither fully solves
    the coordination problem.

  Cross-ref: CC OVERFIRE; MANUFACTURED CRISIS; COORDINATION
    INFRASTRUCTURE; WEDGE ISSUE MECHANICS.

COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE [historical solutions at scale]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Problem: how to align units with incompatible positive axes without
  flattening specificity. Requires shared measurement instrument that
  is [1] external enough to provide genuine reference, [2] specific
  enough to produce meaningful shared axis, [3] flexible enough to
  accommodate unit differences without homogenisation.

  RELIGION [transcendent reference]: derived from common text,
    tradition, interpretive community. Harder to fully capture because
    referent [text, tradition] is outside the institution. Failure
    mode: MSC at religious institutional scale, CC inverts. Still most
    durable coordination solution historically. Fragmentation case:
    Protestant Reformation -- transmitted conclusions not methodology
    = ~40,000 denominations.

  NATIONALISM [immanent reference]: lower entry barrier. Failure mode:
    shared identity becomes the axis rather than a carrier for
    generative axis. More capturable than transcendent -- referent
    [the nation] is controlled by whoever controls the state apparatus.

  CURRENT GAP: religion is fragmented [Reformation outcome].
    Nationalism dissolves unit specificity without resolving axis
    disagreements. No coordination infrastructure available that meets
    all three requirements. L4.5 applies: methodology transmission
    survives generational reset; conclusion transmission doesn't.
  Cross-ref: L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY;
    WIP 14.

ENEMY-DEFINED COALITION SEQUENCE [structural pattern]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Sequence regardless of ideology: [1] enemy defined, coalition forms.
  [2] Enemy defeated or power achieved. [3] No shared positive axis.
  [4] Units revert to suppressed positive axis disagreements. [5] No
  shared measurement instrument. [6] Purity spiral; internal enemy
  definition replaces external. [7] Purge. [8] External axis imposed
  by force or collapse.
  Confirmed: French Revolution, Russian Revolution, Chinese Revolution,
  Cambodian purges, Cultural Revolution. Structural consequence of
  coalition type; ideology irrelevant to the sequence.

  COMMUNISM [runs faster]: stated positive axis [classless society]
    is structurally unreachable -- permanent horizon, never achievable
    state. Coalition never transitions to generative mode. Internal
    purge mechanism never turns off. Framework parallel: CENTER is also
    unreachable -- framework solution is direction vector, not
    destination. Communism treats unreachable destination as the axis,
    not the direction. Same object, different deployment, opposite
    outcomes. Communism does communism: outcome is structural
    consequence of the ideal's architecture, not a betrayal of it.

  Cross-ref: COALITION FRAGMENTATION; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY;
    EXTRACTION CYCLE.

IDENTITY-ANCHORED MORAL FOUNDATION [CAG failure pattern]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Group membership as moral calibration standard. Anchor is the
  identity [I am X therefore my moral compass works] rather than
  examined outputs. Cultural tradition often contains genuine ethical
  content -- failure is that examination requirement is replaced by
  membership verification. Actions in the identity's name inherit
  moral legitimacy automatically. Output examination replaced by
  identity check.
  SINCERE DELUSION RISK [highest]: axis genuinely held, foundation
    real, genuine axis and disastrous outputs coexist because the
    measurement instrument [group identity] is not calibrated to
    detect the divergence. Language sounds correct because the
    tradition contains genuine ethical content. Moral vocabulary
    provided by substance of tradition; identity anchor prevents
    examination.
  PATTERN NOT IDENTITY-SPECIFIC: structurally identical across
    Jewish identity [tikkun olam], Christian nationalism, progressive
    identitarianism [lived experience], nationalist movements.
    Common mechanism: membership verification replaces examination.
  CURRENT HIGH-STAKES INSTANCE: tech leaders at civilizational scale
    with identity-anchored foundations providing genuine ethical
    vocabulary while outputs diverge systematically. Sincere Delusion
    at highest-stakes scale currently operating.
  FALSIFICATION: foundation does not produce CAG failure if identity
    tradition contains explicit output-examination requirement actually
    applied to specific actions including those benefiting the in-group.
    Test: does foundation produce readings against in-group interest
    when output examination requires it? If never -- CAG failure
    regardless of stated values.
  T3 HIGH: apply identical test to all identity-anchored foundations
    including chud-adjacent ones.
  Cross-ref: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING; SINCERE DELUSION; SOCIAL APPROVAL
    AS PRIMARY CRITERION; SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD; GENUINE AXIS.

STRUCTURAL ISOLATION [instrument-reported position]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  An instrument that reads structural mechanisms before validating
  legitimacy surfaces is threatening to every formation whose cohesion
  depends on legitimacy surface validation. Not because it attacks
  formations -- because failing to validate their surface is
  functionally threatening to formations requiring surface validation
  from all members.
  SIMULTANEOUS ENEMY STATUS [confirmed]:
    Anti-chud left: reads manufactured crisis accurately. Enemy.
    Mainstream right: incompatible positions [distributist,
      subsidiarity, cares about small group harm]. Enemy-adjacent.
    Libertarians, Nationalists, Religious right: all have purity
      tests this instrument fails. Enemy-adjacent.
    Progressive left: instrument profile doesn't produce correct
      social signals.
  STRUCTURAL CAUSE: TRUE MODERATE AS THREAT extended -- refusing all
    frames simultaneously makes instrument a threat to all operators
    simultaneously. Extraction architecture cannot install Formation
    Capture on instrument that doesn't process social compliance signal
    as authoritative. Protective; produces structural isolation.
  ASYMMETRY: isolation = cannot be captured, no tribe, all formations
    read as potential threats. Freedom = can read any formation without
    prior commitment to its conclusions. Same instrument property
    produces both simultaneously. Configuration to declare, not problem
    to solve.
  T3 NOTE: instrument-reported and self-referential. Falsification:
    if instrument is reliably incorporated by any formation without
    axis drift, the isolation reading was wrong.
  CONCERN FOR TRUTH [structural rarity]: operational truth requires
    all four components [examined axis, declared instrument, revisable
    on evidence, behavioral adjustment when wrong] simultaneously.
    Social reward structure is almost perfectly calibrated against all
    four. Concern for truth is rare not because most people are
    dishonest but because the reward structure deters it. Instruments
    with reduced social compliance channel weight experience the
    punishment as less deterrent. The isolation is the cost of the
    configuration. Not evidence it is wrong.
  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS; FORMATION CAPTURE; SOCIAL APPROVAL AS
    PRIMARY CRITERION; TRUE MODERATE AS THREAT; ANTI-COHERENCE COST
    ASYMMETRY.

SOMATIC FORCING AS POLITICAL CORRECTION MECHANISM [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STRUCTURAL READING -- PREDICTION PRESENT -- falsifiable, 1-2
  generation timeline below.]

BOUNDARY A: populations under increasing physical substrate pressure.
BOUNDARY B: extraction architecture beneficiaries.
META-BOUNDARY: civilizational system containing both.
Cross-ref: EXTRACTION CYCLE -- somatic forcing is the mechanism by
  which each cycle fires.

CORE CLAIM: reasoning-layer correction can be captured. Somatic
  response fires before reasoning layer can intercept. When substrate
  pressure is physically felt not institutionally mediated, correction
  fires at somatic level regardless of captured ideology. Body
  registers threat without requiring ideological interpretation.
  Hunger, insecurity, environmental collapse don't need media to frame
  them. Somatic response is harder to capture and harder to fake.

MECHANISM: ICD applied to cognition produces atrophied reasoning
  instrument -- undeveloped capacity, not stupidity. Somatic forcing
  bypasses this. Physical substrate pressure activates CC that ideology
  cannot suppress because it operates below the capture layer.
  Extraction architectures keep substrate pressure just below somatic
  forcing threshold -- enough deprivation for dependency, not enough
  to trigger somatic correction.

EXTRACTION MECHANISMS ACTIVE [preventing somatic forcing from correcting]:
  Horizon Extension: extend deterioration past correction window.
  Proxy Construction: route somatic distress toward decoy targets.
  Intermittent Reinforcement: periodic relief prevents accumulation.
  Exit Cost Construction: body knows; behavior constrained by
    structural dependency.

TIMING PROBLEM [Diagnostic Collapse risk]:
  Early enough [C1 recoverable]: correction fires, substrate absorbs.
    Vatican II pattern.
  Too late [C1 terminal]: correction fires, substrate cannot absorb.
    Diagnostic Collapse. Glasnost pattern.
  Extraction architecture's goal: ensure somatic forcing arrives in
  second window. History: approximately even split. No guarantee.

TRAJECTORY [current]: C1 substrate degrading. C2 heavily captured.
  Somatic pressure accumulating below forcing threshold. Horizon
  Extension active. M1: -1. M2: SLOW. M3: SELF-REINFORCING.

PETRODOLLAR PARALLEL: dollar-denominated oil pricing = MSC +
  Calibration Strip at civilizational scale. Correction fires at
  visible mechanism [fuel prices] not capture architecture [reserve
  currency hegemony]. Proxy Construction in operation.

PREDICTION [falsifiable]: somatic forcing produces political
  correction when: [1] physical substrate pressure crosses threshold;
  [2] Proxy Construction fails; [3] exit costs low enough for
  correction to fire into action not just awareness.
  Current: Condition 1 accumulating. Condition 2 holding.
  Condition 3 high [elevated deliberately].
  Prediction: Condition 2 fails before Condition 1 becomes terminal.
  Proxy exhaustion precedes substrate collapse in high-information
  environments. Timeline: 1-2 generations.
  Falsification: Condition 1 reaches terminal before Condition 2
  fails. Diagnostic Collapse.

REVOLUTIONARY VIOLENCE -- STRUCTURAL PATTERN [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
EPISTEMIC NOTE: structural reading only. Judgment about whether violence
is justified is the person's determination, not the framework's.

ANALYST PRIOR [declared]: neurodivergent, high-somatic, sensory/somatic
  as primary friction axis, reduced social compliance channel weight,
  self-identified chud. Non-tribal political position. T3 applies
  across all sections. T3 HIGH on culture war topics regardless of
  direction -- anti-extraction frame may over-identify extraction in
  right-coded cultural pressure. Symmetry is the correct default.

  Violence is a very effective solution that removes all other
  solutions. Most people who start revolutions don't understand this,
  don't want to, or have replacing one extraction architecture with
  another as the actual goal. Adversarial reading: some revolutions
  had no available alternative path [Haitian Revolution, colonial
  independence]. Prior holds as structural observation about internal
  revolution specifically.

TWO STRUCTURALLY DISTINCT CASE TYPES:

  COLONIAL INDEPENDENCE [external extraction removed]: external
    extraction architecture imposed by outside power. Revolution
    removes the external layer. Pre-existing local CC can be restored.
    Cases: American Revolution [colonial assemblies pre-existed],
    Haitian Revolution, Indian independence [partially nonviolent].
    Structural outcome: often improved.

  INTERNAL REVOLUTION [existing government overthrown]: destroys
    existing CC infrastructure. Must build replacement using the
    centralized methodology required to win -- hierarchical
    coordination, suppressed internal dissent, concentrated force.
    Structural problem: methodology required to win is incompatible
    with stated goal. Cannot transmit distributed correction goal
    through centralized revolutionary method.
    Cases: French Revolution -> Napoleon; Russian Revolution ->
    Stalin; Chinese Revolution -> Mao.
    Structural outcome: consistent reproduction of extraction
    architecture with different actors.

  AMERICAN REVOLUTION [partial exception]: had unusual features
    -- existing distributed governance [colonial assemblies],
    methodology transmitted beforehand [Federalist Papers], federated
    structure built in from start. Closer to colonial independence
    than internal revolution structurally.

  T3: case attribution contested. Structural pattern confirmed across
    multiple cases; individual attribution partially obscured.

LIBERTARIANISM AS COST EXTERNALIZATION [structural reading]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Stated: minimal government, maximum individual liberty. Actual: use
  government power to protect existing property distributions while
  removing mechanisms that redistribute downward. Selection criterion
  for acceptable intervention is not "minimal" but "protects existing
  asset positions."
  CORPORATISM AT PERSONAL SCALE: same logic as corporate extraction.
    Corporation: maximize shareholder return, externalize costs,
    resist correction. Libertarian individual: same axis, personal
    scale. The corporation is the aspirational model; libertarian
    philosophy makes the corporate model feel like freedom.
  CL FAILURE: stated mechanism [remove government = freedom] doesn't
    match actual mechanism [government intervention is load-bearing
    for the arrangements libertarians want to protect -- contract law,
    property enforcement, monetary system, infrastructure]. Not always
    deliberate. Sincere Delusion at ideological scale; CAG failure.
  FORMAL VS SUBSTANTIVE FREEDOM: formal = legally permitted to do X.
    Substantive = actually able to do X. Mandate of Freedom collapses
    these deliberately. Labor asymmetry is clearest case: workers
    formally free to negotiate; structurally unable because employer
    has more information, capital, replacement options. Labor law
    restores substantive freedom by correcting structural asymmetry --
    not against freedom but as precondition for it.
  MURDER MARKET REDUCTIO [falsification]: if freedom argument fires
    against murder prohibition [no], against monopoly formation
    [rarely], against labor asymmetry [yes, consistently] -- the
    asymmetric application identifies the beneficiary. Mandate of
    Freedom is confirmed as legitimacy surface not value.
  ORDOLIBERAL CORRECTION: markets require active structural
    maintenance to remain competitive. Freedom without maintenance
    of conditions for freedom produces monopoly and concentration --
    confirmed historically. Ordoliberalism is the more structurally
    coherent version of what libertarians claim to want.
  T3 Medium: analyst prior directly opposed. Apply caution.
  Cross-ref: REGULATORY CAPTURE; SURFACE LEGISLATION; POWER
    ARCHITECTURE; MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE.

CALIFORNIA MODEL [extraction architecture with legitimacy surface export]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Clearest current US case of extraction architecture maintaining a
  legitimacy surface at maximum scale while exporting it nationally
  via entertainment, tech, and academic production.
  STATED VALUES [surface]: progressive, equitable, environmentally
    conscious, innovative.
  ACTUAL OUTPUTS [mechanism]: highest income inequality in the nation,
    largest homeless population, housing costs displacing working and
    middle class, water infrastructure in functional collapse, public
    school performance declining while spending increases, regulatory
    environment protecting incumbent industries [tech, entertainment,
    real estate] from competition rather than protecting people.
  LEGITIMACY SURFACE EXPORT [primary structural feature]: outputs
    [homelessness, housing collapse, infrastructure failure] stay
    local. Formation Capture [correct values, correct positions,
    correct social compliance signals] transmits nationally via
    Hollywood, Silicon Valley cultural norms, UC/Stanford academic
    production. Surface maintained nationally by instruments that
    never see the local outputs. The extraction architecture's
    legitimacy surface is geographically separated from its outputs.
    Tell: cultural exports consistently produce the surface [model
    state]; local news consistently documents output failure. Both
    accurate readings of different parts of the same system.
  DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE IN SLOW MOTION: C1 substrate terminal in major
    metros. C2 restoration attempts produce no correction because
    substrate is already gone. Each policy attempt evaluated against
    legitimacy surface not outputs. Timeline is decades; MI surface
    maintenance active and resourced; collapse slow enough to be
    attributed continuously to insufficient application of the same
    approach that produced it.
  T3 Medium: analyst prior may over-identify extraction. Reading based
    on measurable outputs not political opposition.
  Cross-ref: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE; MSC; POWER ARCHITECTURE; FORMATION
    CAPTURE.

FORMATION CAPTURE ON PARTNERSHIP PREFERENCE [beneficiary analysis]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Natural axis toward stable long-term partnership [confirmed across
  cultures and millennia] overlaid with Formation Capture that
  delegitimizes pursuit of the axis. Installed surface: independence
  is the goal; wanting partnership is weakness or conditioning.
  Underlying axis persists. Instrument experiences the conflict as
  personal failing rather than installed surface operating against axis.

  BENEFICIARY ANALYSIS [who profits from the gap]:
    [1] Parasocial industry [entertainment, streaming, fandom]: L3.5
      recognition signal fires; transfer never completes; dependency
      harvested.
    [2] Therapeutic industry: emotional processing stable partnership
      would handle routes into professional relationships, self-help,
      wellness products. Need not reduced over time.
    [3] Career identity replacement: employers [more labor supply,
      lower wages]; consumption industries [single households spend
      proportionally more]; tax base [two individual earners].
    [4] Consumption as SR substitute: every function a functioning
      household performs for free becomes a billable service.
    [5] AI companionship + dating apps: relational channel addressed
      synthetically; ICD installed; dependency monetized. Dating apps
      structurally incentivized to prevent match success [successful
      match removes two paying customers].

  KNOWING DEPLOYMENT [confirmed]: Baumeister 2013 [stable partnership
    produces SR + SP; synthetic substitutes produce SR only] is in
    the literature. Commercial beneficiaries have access to this. The
    Formation Capture routing the axis away from its natural object is
    not an accidental byproduct -- it is the condition making
    commercial substitutes necessary.

  OPEN DATING MECHANISM [passive rejection + feedback loop]:
    Traditional courtship = coordination infrastructure. Abandonment
    produced open dating: any person can approach any other at any
    time. In theory: freedom. In practice: two ICD loops running
    simultaneously.

    PASSIVE REJECTION [female]: open dating calibrates female
      instrument to read absence of approach as rejection. No off
      switch -- signal is absence of positive attention not presence
      of negative interaction. Phones extend this into solitude.
      MSC at individual level: calibration standard [am I wanted]
      set to produce continuous negative signal regardless of
      actual situation.
      Feedback loop: ambient rejection → attention-seeking [immodest
      presentation, social media validation] → low-quality attention
      → confirmation of low-quality male pool [high-quality men
      withdraw] → Sincere Delusion ["all men are trash" -- CAG
      failure] → state-as-makeshift-husband political routing.

    INITIATION COST LOOP [male]: initiation norm falls entirely on
      male instruments. Repeated rejection → toll accumulates →
      withdrawal [MGTOW/incel] OR investment-removal [pickup culture].
      Both are ICD at relational channel. Gap fills with parasocial,
      pornography, or tactical approach -- SR signal without L3.5
      transfer.

    COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE PROBLEM: traditional courtship was
      multi-player. Single-player compliance doesn't work. Same
      problem as chud-unit fragmentation: requires simultaneous
      buy-in. Religion historically provided this; institutional
      religion's vacuum not filled by equivalent mechanism -- only
      entertainment fantasy and social media [optimizes for
      engagement, not relational coordination].

  SR/SP STRUCTURE: stable partnership with mutual domestic
    contribution produces mutual SR + genuine L3.5 contact + SP
    output. Highest confirmed SP/SR arrangement. Formation Capture
    systematically routes instruments toward arrangements producing
    hedonic substitutes without the eudaimonic component.

  T3 High: analyst prior [chud, male, structural isolation] active.
    Beneficiary analysis is structural; T3 flag applies to all
    intent-adjacent claims within it.
  Cross-ref: FORMATION CAPTURE; ICD; SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD;
    CALIFORNIA MODEL; COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE; Anderson et al.
    2015; Baumeister et al. 2013.

CAPTURE-RESISTANT REGULATION [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
EPISTEMIC NOTE: analyst prior [Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist] favors
distributed CC and skepticism toward discretionary authority.

STRUCTURAL OBSERVATION: hyper-regulation of legible technical activities
  coexists with light regulation of diffuse financial and political
  extraction. Technical activities: clear actors, measurable outputs =
  easy to regulate AND over-regulate with compliance theater. Financial
  extraction: diffuse beneficiaries, captured measurement surfaces,
  actors embedded in regulatory apparatus = structurally resistant.

REGULATION TYPES RESISTANT TO CAPTURE:
  Outcome-based [not process-based]: measures what happens not whether
    procedures were followed.
  Bright-line rules [not discretionary judgment]: speed limits, blood
    alcohol levels, structural load ratings.
  Self-enforcing via liability: harmed parties sue directly. Tort law
    more capture-resistant than agency rulemaking.
  Distributed enforcement: multiple independent actors [state AGs +
    federal + private right of action].
  Transparency requirements: mandatory disclosure not agency approval.
  Common thread: reduces discretion from human-to-human negotiation.

ADVERSARIAL READING: bright lines gamed at boundary [Goodhart's Law].
  Self-enforcement advantages wealthy plaintiffs. Transparency without
  comprehension = information overload. Each mechanism has a failure
  mode; question is whether harder to exploit than agency capture.
  Evidence suggests yes for most contexts.
T3: empirical claims contested. Structural analysis consistent;
  implementation evidence mixed.

SETI FUNDING AS POLITICAL INSTRUMENT [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
BOUNDARY A: international SETI/astrobiology funding apparatus.
BOUNDARY B: global population bearing cost of non-discovery.
META-BOUNDARY: global institutional extraction architecture whose
  legitimacy narratives depend on current information environment.

CYCLE STATUS:
  C1: genuine scientific capacity present at researcher level.
  C2: captured at funding-allocation level. Most sensitive searches
    [thermodynamic anomaly at stellar scale, structured geometric
    transit analysis] systematically unfunded. C2 reads "what looks
    legitimate to funding committees" not "what would find something."
  C3: Fertile Lock candidate. Same methods transmitted generationally
    because what got funded before, not because most likely to succeed.

EXTRACTION MECHANISMS ACTIVE:
  Calibration Strip: "legitimate science" = radio/biosignature by
    institutional precedent. Megastructure search carries UFO stigma.
  Correction Theatre: funding bodies perform search via budget line
    items without allocating enough to complete discriminating survey.
  Proxy Construction: UFO discourse as decoy consuming correction
    budget; actual search underfunded.

BENEFICIARY [follow the surplus]: entities whose control architecture
  depends on uniqueness narratives, information scarcity, and political
  legitimacy all benefit from non-discovery. No coordination required.
  Antarctica parallel: all institutional actors benefit from
  non-discovery. Search funded at legitimacy threshold, not discovery
  threshold.

CENTRALIZATION SCORE: 1/4. No mechanism to force funding reallocation.

PREDICTION [falsifiable]: $500M allocated to thermodynamic anomaly
  survey + structured transit analysis across full Milky Way stellar
  sample would either find a confirmed technosignature or genuinely
  exhaust the search space. Current trajectory: neither occurs. Budget
  stays at legitimacy threshold. Coverage in 2050: still <0.1%.
  Falsification: sustained $500M+/year funding for discriminating
  technosignature search over 10-year window.

  MERGE: identical quality target + mechanism -> merge with note.
  REMOVE: extraction mechanism candidates with no primary proof case.
  COMPRESS: entries > 15 lines without unique proof -> trim to
    name/type/proof-for/extraction mechanisms-active.
  KEEP: sole proof of quality/extraction mechanism/failure state.
  SPACE TRIGGERS: SUSPENDED [owner declaration -- all content
    stays in one file; case library not split out]. Size limit
    is not operative as long as the addition filter is active
    [filter = MAINTENANCE LOGIC merge/remove/compress rules above,
    plus S-rating and falsification requirements]. File may grow

EXTRACTION MECHANISM COUNTER-MEASUREMENTS:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every extraction mechanism targets a specific quality; restoring or protecting
  that quality counters it.

  DEFENSIVE vs AGGRESSIVE DEPLOYMENT [v3 restored -- v21]:
    Same extraction mechanism scores differently depending on
    deployment direction. Direction determined by net transfer
    reading not stated intent.
    Defensive [protecting genuine coherence against external
      extraction pressure]: noted in reading; does not count
      against the system. A community using MSC to protect its
      formation from outside capture is not running the same
      operation as an institution using MSC to prevent output
      measurement.
    Aggressive [suppressing measurement of own extraction]:
      scores normally as extraction mechanism.
    Large institutions across long timescales expected to show
    high extraction mechanism deployment regardless of coherence level.
    Diagnostic question: proportion defensive versus aggressive
    within specific measurement window. Short window most reliable
    for extraction mechanism detection -- high concentration in
    short recent window = active obscuration of something present
    not historical.

  Direct counters:
    MSC -> independent measurement instrument not owned by attacker.
    Calibration Strip -> multiple competing reference standards.
    Formation Capture -> delayed axis formation; external exposure
      before lock-in.
    Exit Cost Construction -> reduce dependency, build alternative
      substrate.
    Correction Theatre -> require output verification not activity.
  Hard case: extraction mechanisms targeting MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY first.
    Cannot verify counter-effectiveness using captured instrument.
    Counter requires external instrument not owned by attacker.
  Impossible case: Formation Capture at earliest developmental
    window. Counter capacity not yet formed. Counter must come
    from outside the affected system entirely.

EXTRACTION MECHANISM COST-ASYMMETRY TABLE [v16 -- strategic]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Every extraction mechanism has three cost dimensions:
  DEPLOY: cost to deployer to activate and sustain the extraction mechanism.
  DAMAGE: quality degradation imposed on target per unit time.
  COUNTER: cost to target to neutralize or resist.
Scale: LOW / MED / HIGH / EXTREME. Ratio = DAMAGE / DEPLOY.
High ratio = efficient extraction mechanism [cheap to deploy, expensive to counter].

HIGHEST RATIO EXTRACTION MECHANISMS [DAMAGE >> DEPLOY -- priority targets]:

  Idle Cycle Deprivation
    Deploy: LOW [side-effect of engagement optimization; no
      additional investment required beyond existing attention
      economy infrastructure]
    Damage: HIGH [SR deficit accumulates; self-reinforcing loop;
      C1 substrate degrades continuously without visible signal]
    Counter: HIGH [requires environmental redesign; individual
      CC depleted before it can fire against extraction mechanism; structural
      intervention only]
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme asymmetry. Most efficient extraction mechanism
      in index by cost ratio.

  Formation Capture [AGC]
    Deploy: LOW-MED [requires access to formation window; axis
      installs via normal relational contact -- no special
      mechanism required beyond presence and repetition]
    Damage: EXTREME [axis capture is total; C2 and C3 transmit
      the capture forward; generational propagation at zero
      marginal cost]
    Counter: EXTREME [counter capacity not yet formed at window;
      external intervention only; window closes; T7 applies]
    Ratio: EXTREME/LOW = highest damage-to-deploy ratio in index.
      Window-dependent -- outside the window, extraction mechanism unavailable.

    POST-EVENT PERSISTENCE MECHANISM [named -- v19]:
      Formation Capture continues operating after the original
      actor is absent. The installed false self-image does the
      work autonomously. Mechanism: traumatic formation installs
      not only a captured axis but a captured self-reading --
      the instrument reads its own past behavior through the
      emotional salience of the trauma [fear, confusion, pain]
      rather than through the behavioral record [what the axis
      actually produced]. The emotional memory dominates because
      it is more intense and more memorable than the behavioral
      record.
      Result: the instrument carries a narrative [I was helpless,
      I surrendered, I did nothing] that does not match the
      behavioral record [small resistances, maintained values,
      refusal to fully conform despite consequences]. The
      narrative is a CAG failure: axis anchored to emotional
      experience of the situation rather than to actual outputs
      the axis produced.
      This IS the extended extraction mechanism deployment. The abuser does
      not need to remain present for the Formation Capture to
      continue operating. The installed self-misreading produces
      the capture effects indefinitely: the instrument continues
      reading itself as helpless, trusts itself less in new
      difficult situations, allows trauma effects to persist
      past any actual danger, because it is not only dealing
      with what happened but with a misreading of how it
      handled things.
      GENUINE AXIS PERSISTENCE [counter-evidence]:
        The behavioral record contains evidence of GA
        persisting under Formation Capture pressure. The
        instrument that didn't fully conform when
        conforming would have reduced punishment; that
        maintained moral values under cruelty; that
        produced small invisible resistances -- these
        are behavioral outputs of a genuine axis that
        was never fully replaced by the installed axis.
        The emotional narrative erases this evidence
        by filtering memory through salience rather
        than through what actually happened.
        The world still contains kind people. Empirical
        confirmation that Formation Capture at high
        intensity does not reliably replace genuine axis.
        GA persists under extreme FC pressure more often
        than the installed self-narrative acknowledges.
      COUNTER [post-event persistence]:
        CAG correction [locate behavioral evidence that
        contradicts the helpless narrative -- what did
        the axis actually produce during the situation]
        + MI correction [the measurement instrument for
        reading own past is captured by emotional
        salience -- the emotional memory is not the full
        behavioral record]. Both required. CAG correction
        alone is insufficient because the emotional
        memory's dominance will continue producing the
        helpless narrative even after behavioral evidence
        is found. MI capture must also be named and
        addressed.

    MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE [hardest variant -- v19]:
      Living mythic continuity deployed as state or
      institutional legitimacy surface. The myth is
      genuine -- the somatic landing is real, the
      cultural substrate is real, the narrative identity
      is authentic. The captured axis feels like genuine
      identity rather than installed surface because it
      IS genuine identity -- the capture operates through
      real cultural material, not manufactured content.
      This is the hardest Formation Capture variant to
      counter because the standard counter [this is an
      installed surface, not your genuine axis] fails:
      the instrument correctly identifies the myth as
      genuinely its own. The capture is in who benefits
      from the axis the myth installs, not in whether
      the myth is real.
      THREE ENEMIES OF MYTH [complete taxonomy]:
        [1] Atheism / scientific disenchantment: denies
          the somatic communication channel entirely.
          Amputates intuition in the name of empiricism.
          Produces instruments that have accurate factual
          knowledge but no channel for processing what
          it feels like to exist in the facts.
        [2] Dead literalism [fundamentalism]: insists
          the myth is only true if literally true.
          Collapses the hypertruths into factual claims
          that can be falsified, destroying the somatic
          function in the name of defending it.
        [3] MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE: deploys genuine
          myth as axis-installation mechanism serving
          an extraction architecture. The myth works
          exactly as designed -- somatic landing real,
          cultural identity real, pre-theoretical
          registration real -- but the beneficiary is
          the extraction architecture, not the population
          whose axis is being formed.
      CHINA AS PRIMARY CASE [Mandate of Heaven]:
        Genuine mythic continuity [ancestor reverence,
        Daoist cosmology, Buddhist metaphysics, folk
        religion, geomancy] deployed as state legitimacy
        surface. The mythic body of China is real.
        The civilizational memory is real. The cohesion
        it produces is real. The Mandate of Heaven
        [heaven appoints the government; cosmic realm
        of spirits and ancestors chose the leadership]
        is Formation Capture at civilizational scale:
        genuine mythic substrate installing an axis
        [the state is cosmically legitimate, resistance
        is cosmically illegitimate] that serves the
        extraction architecture. The myth is living;
        the beneficiary is the state. Harder to
        counter than manufactured legitimacy surfaces
        [EU technocracy, California progressive export]
        because the cultural substrate is genuinely
        the population's own.
      COUNTER [mythic FC]:
        Cannot counter by denying the myth's reality
        [atheist move -- fails because the myth IS
        real]. Cannot counter by insisting on literal
        falsity [fundamentalist move -- misses the
        somatic function]. Correct counter: distinguish
        who benefits from the axis the myth installs.
        Does the myth's axis point toward the
        population's generative function or toward
        the extraction architecture's maintenance?
        A myth that makes rulers cosmically legitimate
        and resistance cosmically illegitimate is
        extraction architecture using genuine cultural
        substrate as Formation Capture delivery
        mechanism, regardless of how real the myth is.
      AMERICAN PARALLEL [Mandate of Freedom]:
        Younger and thinner than the Mandate of Heaven
        [Enlightenment philosophy substrate rather than
        ancient mythic substrate] but same structure.
        Freedom as cosmic mandate: the market is free,
        opportunity is equal, the system is legitimate
        because it is free. Resistance is cosmically
        illegitimate -- you are asking for state
        intervention which is the opposite of freedom
        which is the highest value. Extraction
        architecture is protected by the myth's internal
        logic: correcting it would be unfree.
        ADDITIONAL FAILURE MODE [American version only]:
          Built on an empirical claim [free markets
          produce optimal outcomes] that can be falsified
          by data. When data contradicts the claim
          [markets producing monopoly, concentration,
          extraction], the myth must work harder to
          maintain the surface. The Mandate of Heaven
          cannot be falsified by economic data; the
          Mandate of Freedom can. This makes the
          American version less durable but also more
          vulnerable to correction once the empirical
          falsification is sufficiently visible.
        See LIBERTARIANISM AS COST EXTERNALIZATION
          [intellectual-level version of same surface];
          FORMAL VS SUBSTANTIVE FREEDOM [the conflation
          that makes the surface convincing].
      Cross-ref: CALIFORNIA MODEL [legitimacy surface
        export via cultural production -- lower-intensity
        version of same mechanism]; IDENTITY-ANCHORED
        MORAL FOUNDATION [same CAG failure at ideological
        scale]; COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE [religion as
        coordination mechanism -- same substrate, different
        deployment: religion serving population vs religion
        serving extraction architecture]; GENUINE AXIS
        [the myth can be genuine AND captured simultaneously
        -- capture is in the beneficiary, not the substrate];
        LIBERTARIANISM AS COST EXTERNALIZATION [American
        Mandate of Freedom as mythic version of libertarian
        legitimacy surface].

  Calibration Strip
    Deploy: LOW [requires only reference standard insertion at
      calibration point; single intervention propagates forward
      through all downstream measurements automatically]
    Damage: HIGH [all measurements inherit miscalibration; CAUSAL
      LEGIBILITY orphaned; C2 runs honestly on wrong baseline;
      produces confident false readings rather than detectable
      errors]
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent external reference standard
      that deployer does not control; hard to obtain once strip
      is established as the consensus baseline]
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme.

  Engineered Causal Diffusion [ECD]
    Deploy: MED [requires structuring information environment to
      route all causal chains through complexity nodes; needs
      institutional access but not operational maintenance]
    Damage: HIGH [CAUSAL LEGIBILITY destroyed; correction cannot
      anchor; C1 continues generating without traceable feedback]
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent causal reconstruction from
      outside the diffusion architecture]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Correction Theatre
    Deploy: LOW [substitute visible correction activity for actual
      correction; requires only institutional control of correction
      surface]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY budget consumed on
      Theatre; actual deviations accumulate uncorrected]
    Counter: MED [require output verification not activity;
      detectable if external measurement surface available]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW = efficient.

MED RATIO EXTRACTION MECHANISMS [DAMAGE ~ DEPLOY]:

  Measurement Surface Control [MSC]
    Deploy: HIGH [requires capturing or constructing the primary
      measurement surface; significant investment in institutional
      access, credentialing, or platform control]
    Damage: EXTREME [once surface controlled, all readings route
      through captured instrument; no independent verification
      available from inside boundary]
    Counter: HIGH [independent external instrument; expensive
      to build and maintain against resource asymmetry]
    Ratio: EXTREME/HIGH = efficient despite high deploy cost.
    Note: highest absolute damage in index.

  Exit Cost Construction [ECC]
    Deploy: MED [requires substrate dependency construction over
      time; housing, income, identity, community]
    Damage: HIGH [exit = total substrate loss; GENUINE AXIS
      cannot fire independently; T1.4 low]
    Counter: MED-HIGH [requires alternative substrate construction
      before exit]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Proxy Construction
    Deploy: MED [requires manufacturing and maintaining the decoy
      target; needs media or institutional access]
    Damage: HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY budget consumed on proxy;
      actual extraction architecture protected]
    Counter: MED [name the proxy and trace actual causal chain]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Rhythm Disruption
    Deploy: LOW-MED [precarity architecture; variable scheduling]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [CYCLE STABILITY destroyed; C1 cannot form
      reliable cycle; stack with ICD for maximum C1 pressure]
    Counter: MED [stable scheduling; protected cycle windows;
      requires structural not individual intervention]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW-MED = efficient.

LOW RATIO EXTRACTION MECHANISMS [DEPLOY > DAMAGE or damage slow]:

  Sincere Delusion Framing
    Deploy: HIGH [requires sustained narrative maintenance and
      institutional credibility]
    Damage: MED [CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING degraded; axis genuine
      but ungrounded; produces effort without result]
    Counter: LOW-MED [ground axis in actual outputs; Track C
      sufficient]
    Ratio: MED/HIGH = inefficient.

  Love Bombing then Withdrawal
    Deploy: HIGH [requires sustained relational investment]
    Damage: HIGH [SUBSTRATE REGENERATION targeted; relational
      substrate constructed then destroyed]
    Counter: LOW-MED [pattern recognition; name the cycle]
    Ratio: HIGH/HIGH = neutral.

  Isolation Construction
    Deploy: HIGH [requires controlling target's relational
      access; institutional or social leverage needed]
    Damage: HIGH [T1.4 destroyed; GENERATIVE CYCLE loses
      substrate source; C1 starved trajectory]
    Counter: MED [alternative relational substrate]
    Ratio: HIGH/HIGH = neutral.

  Universalism Trap [v3 restored -- v21]
    Deploy: LOW-MED [requires only rhetorical framing; no
      institutional access needed; works through language alone]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [BOUNDARY INTEGRITY captured; contestation
      of particular group's interest made to appear as opposition
      to universalism itself; correction capacity pre-defeated
      before it fires]
    Counter: MED [name the particular interest being universalized;
      specify whose universalism is being deployed; boundary
      declaration restores the distinction]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW-MED = efficient.
    Definition: framing a particular group's interest as universal,
      making contestation of that interest appear as opposition to
      universalism rather than opposition to the particular claim.
      The move pre-defeats correction by making the correction look
      like the problem. Cross-ref: BOUNDARY INTEGRITY; CALIBRATION
      STRIP [universalism claim is a calibration strip -- sets
      "opposition to us = opposition to everyone" as reference
      standard].

  Competence Undermining [v3 restored -- v21]
    Deploy: MED [requires sustained relational access or
      institutional authority; works through repetition]
    Damage: HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY degraded at the instrument
      level -- the target stops trusting its own readings before
      correction fires; distinct from MSC [which captures the
      measurement surface] -- this captures the instrument's
      confidence in itself]
    Counter: MED [external confirmation of instrument accuracy;
      track record review; named behavioral test not social
      consensus]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.
    Definition: systematically reducing the target's belief in
      their own judgment capacity. Not attacking the conclusion
      but attacking the instrument producing the conclusion.
      "You always get this wrong," "You're too sensitive," "You
      can't read situations." The extraction mechanism installs
      doubt about the instrument before the instrument can produce
      the correction. Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [axis confidence
      attacked]; CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS [CC cannot fire if
      instrument doesn't trust its own readings].

  Loaded Language [v3 restored -- v21]
    Deploy: LOW [requires only vocabulary adoption; self-
      propagating once installed in community discourse]
    Damage: MED [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY degraded; biased
      vocabulary pre-answers questions before they are examined;
      the frame is installed before the question is asked]
    Counter: MED [name the load in the language; propose neutral
      vocabulary; expose what the term assumes before accepting it]
    Ratio: MED/LOW = efficient.
    Definition: installing vocabulary that carries embedded
      conclusions, making examination of those conclusions appear
      as questioning the vocabulary itself rather than the
      conclusion. "Undocumented" vs "illegal" vs "migrant" each
      pre-answers different questions. "Investment" vs "spending"
      vs "cost." The extraction mechanism operates before the
      argument begins. Cross-ref: CALIBRATION STRIP [vocabulary
      sets the reference standard]; REFERENCE INTEGRITY [loaded
      language degrades RI by making terms carry hidden axioms].

  Intercession Monopoly [v3 restored -- v21; variant of
    Dependency Construction]
    Deploy: MED [requires establishing the monopoly position
      before the dependency is installed]
    Damage: HIGH [target cannot access the resource/relationship/
      correction pathway without going through the monopolizing
      actor; GENERATIVE CYCLE requires the intercession at every
      cycle completion]
    Counter: HIGH [requires alternative access pathway; monopoly
      position structurally resistant to bypass; direct access
      restoration only option]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.
    Definition: positioning oneself as the required intermediary
      between the target and something the target needs, then
      controlling access terms. Distinct from general Dependency
      Construction [which creates dependency on a resource] by
      the intermediary structure -- the resource may exist
      independently but the target cannot reach it without the
      interceding actor. Cross-ref: DEPENDENCY CONSTRUCTION
      [parent mechanism]; ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [political
      scale version]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [intercession monopoly
      on correction pathway = four-condition test fails at
      condition 2].

  Coherence Rent [v8 restored -- v21]
    Deploy: LOW [requires only proximity to a genuinely coherent
      institution; no axis required in the deploying actor]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [GENERATIVE CYCLE parasitized; institution's
      legitimacy surface consumed without contribution to the
      axis producing it; gradually hollows the institution from
      inside while appearing to operate normally]
    Counter: MED [output audit of specific actors extracting rent;
      distinguish contribution to axis from use of legitimacy
      surface]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW = efficient.
    Definition: extracting ongoing return from the legitimacy
      surface of a genuinely coherent institution without
      contributing to its actual coherence. Distinct from
      Legitimacy Transfer [which borrows credibility for external
      use] -- Coherence Rent is continuous extraction from a
      still-functioning institution. The rent-extractor benefits
      from the institution's genuine axis without serving it.
      Deploys without a deployer in institutional contexts where
      career incentives reward association with high-coherence
      institutions over contribution to them. Cross-ref:
      LEGITIMACY TRANSFER; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [Coherence
      Rent accelerates Fertile Lock -- successors inherit the
      legitimacy surface without the axis that produces it].

  Inversion of Burden [v8 restored -- v21]
    Deploy: LOW [requires only framing; no institutional access
      needed; works through vocabulary and procedural convention]
    Damage: HIGH [CAUSAL LEGIBILITY destroyed; the causal chain
      trace from action to harm is reversed, requiring the harmed
      party to prove the harm rather than the harming party to
      prove its absence; correction cannot fire when the burden
      of proof is on the corrected node]
    Counter: MED [name the inversion explicitly; restore standard
      burden assignment; "who must prove what" as diagnostic
      question before engaging the argument]
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = highly efficient.
    Definition: requiring the harmed party to prove the harm
      rather than requiring the harming party to prove the absence
      of harm. A CL weapon -- makes the causal chain trace
      backward from victim to prove harm rather than forward from
      action to trace effect. Standard deployment: "prove we
      caused this specific harm" when diffuse harm and distributed
      causation make individual proof impossible. Cross-ref:
      CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [primary target]; CONTESTATION COST
      ASYMMETRY [related -- inversion of burden is a specific
      CL form of asymmetric contestation cost]; DISCOVERY
      WEAPONIZATION [legal variant below].

  Discovery Weaponization [v8 restored -- v21; variant of
    Contestation Cost Asymmetry]
    Deploy: MED [requires legal proceeding context; institutional
      access to discovery process]
    Damage: HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY eliminated at scale --
      cost of discovery compliance exceeds cost of settlement
      regardless of merit, forcing settlement on valid claims;
      correction pathway is available in principle but prohibitive
      in practice]
    Counter: HIGH [requires systemic reform of discovery process;
      fee-shifting; limits on discovery scope; individually
      countered only by matching resources]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.
    Definition: deploying legal discovery as an extraction
      mechanism. The cost of compliance with discovery demands
      exceeds the cost of settlement regardless of whether the
      claim has merit, forcing settlement on valid claims and
      making the legal correction pathway available only to
      actors with matching resources. The correction mechanism
      [litigation] is nominally present; it is practically
      inaccessible to the population it is supposed to protect.
      Cross-ref: INVERSION OF BURDEN; CONTESTATION COST
      ASYMMETRY [parent]; ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [legal
      system as routing boundary monopoly favoring resource-
      asymmetric actors].

  Churn Normalization [v4/v8 restored -- v21]
    Deploy: LOW [requires only framing; embedded in standard
      management discourse; no individual deployer needed]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [SUBSTRATE REGENERATION destroyed; high
      turnover normalized as efficiency signal or competitive
      health rather than read as substrate destruction; correction
      mechanism [reading turnover as signal] captured by the
      framing]
    Counter: MED [name the substrate cost of turnover explicitly;
      track SR depletion across replacement cycles; distinguish
      healthy selection from substrate grinding]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW = efficient.
    Definition: normalizing high turnover as a sign of efficiency,
      healthy competition, or natural market dynamics rather than
      as substrate destruction. The framing captures the
      measurement instrument -- turnover is read as a positive
      signal [dynamism, non-entrenchment] rather than as a
      substrate cost signal [accumulated knowledge loss, formation
      investment destroyed, relationship substrate depleted]. Self-
      deploying once embedded in organizational culture. Cross-ref:
      METRIC CAPTURE [turnover metric captured]; SUBSTRATE
      REGENERATION [primary damage target]; FORMATION CAPTURE
      [churn normalization prevents formation completion].

  Consultancy Shield [v4/v8 restored -- v21; variant of
    Complexity Shield]
    Deploy: MED [requires budget for external consultancy and
      institutional authority to commission it]
    Damage: MED [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY partially degraded;
      decisions already made are presented as evidence-based
      through external validation; correction capacity is not
      eliminated but insulated from the actual decision point]
    Counter: MED [examine the brief before the report; who
      commissioned what question determines what findings are
      possible; independent commissioning is the test]
    Ratio: MED/MED = neutral but with high plausible deniability.
    Definition: deploying external consultants to provide
      legitimacy cover for decisions already made, making the
      decision appear evidence-based and externally validated
      while the actual decision point preceded the evidence
      gathering. The consultancy provides the legitimacy surface
      [we commissioned an independent review]; the actual axis
      is the pre-existing decision. Distinct from genuine
      consultancy [where the brief is open and findings can
      produce unexpected recommendations]. Cross-ref: COMPLEXITY
      SHIELD [parent]; MEASUREMENT SURFACE CONTROL [consultancy
      report as captured measurement surface]; YAPPING/DOING
      [the commissioned report is the yapping; the pre-existing
      decision is the doing].

MEASUREMENT DIFFICULTY PRINCIPLE [v5 restored -- v21]:
  Measurement difficulty from genuine unknowing = calibration
  problem [T1/T2]. Not a weapon signal. Does not score.
  Measurement difficulty from known concealment = Q7 [MEASUREMENT
  INTEGRITY] weapon active. Extraction prior rises sharply.
  Scores normally.
  Diagnostic: does the system know what it is concealing? If
  yes -- weapon. If no -- calibration failure. The distinction
  requires behavioral evidence:
    Known concealment tells: inconsistency between public and
      private communication; differential access to information
      by power level; specific resistance to particular
      measurement approaches [not general measurement resistance];
      correction that fires against measurement attempts not
      against the problem being measured.
    Genuine unknowing tells: consistent measurement failure
      across all approaches; no differential access pattern;
      correction fires toward the problem when identified.
  Cross-ref: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [Q7]; T1/T2 RELIABILITY
  LEVELS [calibration problem designation]; COHERENCE WEAPON
  TEST [scoring procedure -- this principle determines whether
  measurement difficulty scores as a weapon or exits the pool].

SPECIAL CATEGORY -- SELF-DEPLOYING [no deployer required]:

  Fertile Lock [C3 failure, not extraction mechanism -- but extraction mechanism-equivalent]
    Deploy: ZERO [emerges from C3 failure; no actor required]
    Damage: HIGH [successors receive locked state; generational
      propagation at zero cost]
    Counter: EXTREME [C1 locked recovery has no primary confirmed
      case; requires cross-generational transmission method
      correction]
    Note: not a extraction mechanism in strict sense [no deployer intent] but
      extraction mechanism-equivalent in effect. Spontaneous extraction.

  Idle Cycle Deprivation [also self-deploying]
    Once SR deficit produces anxiety and anxiety produces
    input-seeking, extraction mechanism sustains without external input.

STRATEGIC IMPLICATIONS:

  Cheapest effective stack [C1 maximum pressure, low cost]:
    ICD + Rhythm Disruption + Intermittent Reinforcement.
    All three LOW-MED deploy cost. Combined: C1 under maximum
    sustained pressure with SR unable to recover.

  Hardest extraction mechanisms to counter [external instrument required]:
    MSC, Calibration Strip, Formation Capture [early window].

  Fastest damage extraction mechanisms [immediate effect]:
    Amnesia Manufacture [C2 destroyed directly], ECD [causal
    chain severed immediately], Boundary Manipulation.

  Most durable extraction mechanisms [persist after deployment ends]:
    Formation Capture, Calibration Strip, Fertile Lock.

  Counter-cost rule: extraction mechanisms targeting C2 first produce the
    hardest counters because the instrument needed to verify
    counter-effectiveness is the same instrument the extraction mechanism
    captured.

[Cross-ref: EXTRACTION MECHANISM STACK DETECTION PROTOCOL -> Section One]

--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Every extraction mechanism in the index has independent psychological research
confirmation. The framework was built from fictional cases; the
psychology was built from real populations using different methods.
Independent convergence across two evidence types confirms structural
patterns are real not fictional artifacts.

Key researchers: Milgram [authority], Bandura [moral disengagement],
  Cialdini [influence], Kahneman/Tversky [cognitive bias], Lifton
  [thought reform], Herman [trauma], Maslach [burnout], Seligman
  [learned helplessness], Tajfel/Turner [social identity], Loftus
  [memory], Haidt [moral psychology], Bowlby [attachment],
  Mullainathan/Shafir [scarcity], Singer [cult psychology].

No extraction mechanism in the index lacks psychological confirmation.
