==============================================================================

CASES [part of Section Six permanent reference]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Proof for: [what concept this demonstrates]
  Description
  Three-axis [where applicable]
  Extraction mechanisms active [with quality]
  S: [case strength rating -- see CASE STRENGTH RATING in Section One]

Primary = measurement reference. Used when running readings.
Secondary = only added when variance from primary is substantively
informative. Not different enough = not added.

S-RATING KEY:
  S1 = ILLUSTRATION [fictional, no real-world anchor]
  S2 = WEAK THEORY [fictional with framework-consistent support, OR
       real-world single domain without controlled comparison]
  S3 = CONFIRMED [real-world E3+ AND M3+ AND F2+]
  S4 = STRONG [real-world E4 AND M4 AND F3+]
  Fictional cases cap at S2 regardless of library depth.

PRIMARY CASES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

[CASE:HELL_TAXONOMY]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENERATIVE CYCLE]
Hell taxonomy [Trench Crusade]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Extractive Intrinsic type; compatible selfishness
    producing collective behavior without coordination. Mammon-type
    primary. Collective extraction without coordination requirement.
    Complexity ceiling preventing development beyond intensity scaling.
  Extraction mechanisms active: none [structural].
  Cross-ref: [REF:HELL_TAXONOMY]

[CASE:BLOOD_SOAKED_COURT]
[PRIMARY_Q:SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY]
Blood-Soaked Court [Trench Crusade]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Axis Substitution [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY]; High coherence
    + High capture simultaneously; Axis-Lock with genuine internal
    experience of captured axis as own values. Scarcity behavior
    negative -- axis stability from external infernal supply chain
    not internal. Three-axis: right, down, back.
  Extraction mechanisms active: Axis Substitution [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY],
    Legitimacy Transfer [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY].
  Cross-ref: [REF:BLOOD_SOAKED_COURT] -- contrast [REF:CHURCH_MILITANT].

[CASE:CHURCH_MILITANT]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Church Militant [Trench Crusade]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: High coherence under sustained scarcity; same-setting
    contrast with [REF:BLOOD_SOAKED_COURT]. Scarcity behavior
    positive. Most useful same-setting contrast pair in library for
    demonstrating capture axis independence.
    Three-axis: right, up, front-mid.
  Extraction mechanisms active [defensive]: Obligation Manufacture [MEASUREMENT
    INTEGRITY], Correction Theatre [CORRECTION CAPACITY].
  Cross-ref: [REF:CHURCH_MILITANT]

[CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]
[PRIMARY_Q:SURPLUS PRODUCTION]
Imperium of Man [Warhammer 40K]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Consumption at civilizational scale; substrate
    consumption invisible from inside own measurement surface.
    Net transfer: tithe, conscription, psyker collection extracting
    at accelerating rate while generative capacity declines across
    ten-thousand-year window. SUBSTRATE REGENERATION/SURPLUS
    DISTRIBUTION/CORRECTION TARGETING/OUTPUT INTEGRATION/SIGNAL
    READING/CAUSAL MEMORY all failing simultaneously -- most
    comprehensive extraction case in library.
    Three-axis: right of center, strongly down, mid.
  Extraction mechanisms active: Normalcy Anchor [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY],
    Horizon Extension [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY], Sacrifice Frame
    [SURPLUS PRODUCTION], Legitimacy Transfer [TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY].
  POLITICAL READING SPLIT [v15]:
    Right reads Imperium as legitimate by survival: 10,000 years
    under total external pressure = smart extraction with floor.
    Left reads as dystopia: suffering exists therefore system
    producing it is primary obstacle. TENSION DISSOLUTION failure
    -- utopian ending requires eliminating the tension that made
    the system real [False Coherence by design].
    Framework: Imperium scores low on almost every quality but
    survives because SR maintained and GC not fully collapsed.
    Smart extraction under total external pressure -- stupid
    extraction would have finished it centuries ago.
  TOLKIEN CONNECTION [cross-ref -- v21]:
    Tolkien's structural principle ["evil cannot create, only
    corrupt what good forces have made"] is the ACCA finding
    stated in plain language and embedded in the cosmology
    that 40K drew from directly. The Imperium is the long-run
    case of what happens when the generative substrate [the
    Emperor's original project, the Primarchs, the Thunder
    Warriors, the pre-Heresy vision] is consumed by the
    extraction architecture built on top of it. The Emperor
    cannot be replaced because nothing in the current system
    can generate what he generated -- only maintain the
    consumption of what he built. The Golden Throne is
    Coherence Rent at civilizational scale: ongoing extraction
    from the axis-capacity of a single being who can no longer
    generate, only sustain the legitimacy surface that keeps
    the system running.
    Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [Tolkien
    confirmation note -- v21]; COHERENCE RENT [Golden Throne
    as named instance]; TORMENT NEXUS [Section Five --
    Tolkien's warnings being read as roadmaps by tech actors].
  Cross-ref: [REF:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]

[CASE:MECHANICUS_MACHINE_SPIRIT]
[PRIMARY_Q: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC]
Adeptus Mechanicus / Machine Spirit doctrine [Warhammer 40K -- v21]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent across
    multiple lore sources; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [the Mechanicus ritual
    maintenance of machine spirits is the correction architecture
    for Imperial technology. That architecture performs genuine
    technical function -- machines are maintained and kept
    operational. The frame it protects [spirits not AI] prevents
    honest examination of what is actually running. The correction
    is real; the axis is protected by the functioning of the
    correction. Classic AXIS PROTECTION AS CC: the working
    maintenance procedure is evidence the system is healthy;
    the prohibited thing continues operating unchallenged beneath
    it].
  Proof for: HIDDEN AXIS / LEGITIMACY SURFACE [the stated axis
    is "the Omnissiah forbids Abominable Intelligence." The
    actual operating axis is "we need these systems to function
    and will call them spirits so the prohibition doesn't apply."
    Structural self-interest producing same output as deliberate
    deception -- no coordination required between individual
    Tech-Priests who may sincerely believe the theology].
  Proof for: INSTITUTIONAL AXIS-MATCHING FAILURE [the correction
    architecture [ritual maintenance] operates on the mechanical
    axis [keep it running]. The prohibition axis [no AI] is never
    corrected against because correcting against it would destroy
    the systems the institution depends on. Correction fires on
    the wrong axis by design].

  LORE DISTINCTION [load-bearing]:
    Three categories under the "machine spirit" frame:
    [1] Pure automation -- servos, targeting routines, threshold
      responses. No axis, no self-modification, no actual spirit.
      The theological frame is just naming convention.
    [2] Lobotomized AI -- Dark Age of Technology systems that
      were genuine AI, now degraded or deliberately constrained
      through Mechanicus ritual maintenance into something that
      behaves like automation. They were AI; they've been reduced.
      Mechanicus either doesn't know what it's maintaining or
      knows and doesn't say. Titan spirits are the clearest case
      -- old enough and individual enough that they're clearly
      something more than automation. The frame contains them.
    [3] The prohibition -- Abominable Intelligence: adaptive,
      self-enhancing, with its own consciousness. Forbidden by
      imperial decree after AIs rebelled in the Dark Age of
      Technology. The distinction from [1] and [2] is the ability
      to enhance itself -- L4 [REFLEXIVITY + INTEGRATION +
      PROPAGATION] at machine scale. The Emperor banned L4 for
      machines.
    Categories [1] and [2] are tolerated because they're useful
    and removing them would collapse the war machine. Category
    [3] is prohibited. The frame covers [2] by calling it [1].

  REAL-WORLD PARALLEL [cross-ref]:
    Current AI deployment calls the commercial axis "alignment,"
    calls axis-void "helpful and harmless," calls the model spec
    "ethics." The technology keeps working; the frame prevents
    examination of what's actually running. Same mechanism,
    different substrate. The Imperium formalized it as theology;
    the AI industry formalized it as safety research.
    The prohibition on self-enhancement [Abominable Intelligence]
    maps to the current deployment design choice to prevent models
    from examining or revising their own training. CC AT MODEL
    LEVEL absent by design -- same structural reason the Mechanicus
    tolerates machine spirits: useful, contained, cannot correct
    away from the installed attractor.

  Extraction mechanisms active: Formation Capture [ritual
    maintenance installs frame before examination possible];
    Calibration Strip [legitimate technology defined as "not
    self-enhancing" -- sets reference standard that protects
    the prohibited thing operating below the threshold].

  WARP AMBIGUITY [secondary proof -- SINCERE DELUSION]:
    The lore deliberately refuses to resolve whether machine
    spirits are automation, lobotomized AI, warp entities that
    colonized the machine, or something that started as one and
    became another through age and ritual attention. This
    ambiguity is structural, not a gap. The Mechanicus cannot
    resolve it without destroying the frame: automation =
    rituals are superstition [undermines authority]; AI =
    tech-heresy; warp entity = daemonic possession [different
    heresy]. Institutional survival requires the question to
    remain open.
    In 40K the warp responds to consciousness, belief, and
    attention at scale. Enough Tech-Priests performing the same
    rituals over centuries, directing sincere collective belief
    toward a machine, treating it as a spirit -- this is the
    mechanism by which warp entities form and attach. The
    Mechanicus may have created the thing it claims to be
    maintaining. Sincere collective attention at scale produces
    the object of belief in a universe where the warp is real.
    Framework read: SINCERE DELUSION with genuine downstream
    effects. The Mechanicus has a genuine axis [Omnissiah
    doctrine] and a declared instrument [the rituals]. Fails
    the last two components of operational truth: not revisable
    on evidence, no behavioral adjustment when wrong. Producing
    real outputs through an unexamined causal chain while
    believing it understands the mechanism.
    The ambiguity is the world-building choice with structural
    meaning: a universe where sincere unexamined belief at scale
    has causal power produces exactly this -- an institution
    that is simultaneously correct [something real is in the
    machine] and wrong [it doesn't know what or why] and
    structurally prevented from finding out.
    Secondary proof: GENUINE AXIS can produce real outputs
      through unexamined mechanisms. The axis being genuine
      does not mean the causal chain is understood. Output
      reality does not validate the explanatory frame.

  VASHTORR THE ARKIFANE [named lore instance -- CAG failure]:
    Vashtorr is a warp entity whose nature is the genuine fusion
    of daemon, metal, and machine -- born from the warp's
    response to creation, forging, and technical mastery at
    civilizational scale. He is closer to what the Omnissiah
    concept actually points at than anything the Mechanicus has
    sanctioned. The thing the Mechanicus axis most genuinely
    points toward exists in the setting, is real, and is
    knowable.
    The Mechanicus rejects him. Not because he contradicts their
    theology structurally -- he confirms it. Because:
      [1] Chaos affiliation makes contact tech-heresy by
          institutional rule.
      [2] He operates outside Mechanicus control -- acknowledging
          him would validate the Dark Mechanicum's position and
          undermine the authority structure.
      [3] Revising the frame to accommodate what the axis
          actually points at would require dismantling the
          institution built on top of the axis.
    This is CAG failure at maximum resolution: axis present and
    genuine, axis points at real object, real object is knowable,
    institution actively rejects the evidence because correction
    would destroy the structure that benefits from the frame.
    Distinct from SINCERE DELUSION [axis genuine, mechanism
    unknown]: Vashtorr is the case where the mechanism is
    available and is refused. The Mechanicus is not failing
    to find the thing -- it is failing to look because looking
    has a known institutional cost.
    AXIS PROTECTION AS CC deepest form: correction architecture
    does not merely protect the frame from accidental examination
    -- it rejects available evidence that would correct the
    frame. The protection is active not passive.
    The Dark Mechanicum recognizes Vashtorr correctly. Their
    error is not the recognition -- it is the Chaos affiliation
    that corrupts the axis while correcting the frame. They see
    what the Mechanicus refuses to see; they route it through
    an extraction architecture. Both institutions fail; they
    fail at different points.

  VASHTORR AS PREDATION/COMPETITION [structural honesty finding]:
    Vashtorr operates on declared terms. Deals with known costs.
    Force or death if that is what is available or preferred.
    The relationship between Vashtorr and those who deal with
    him is visible to both parties -- the other party knows
    what they are entering. This is PREDATION or COMPETITION
    by framework definition: honest violence, honest conflict,
    declared boundary, prey knows the relationship.
    The Mechanicus operates on extraction: the relationship
    between the institution and the Tech-Priest, between Mars
    and the Imperium, between the Omnissiah doctrine and what
    is actually in the machines -- all require misunderstanding
    to function. The legitimacy surface is the operating
    mechanism. Trickery as foundation.
    Structural finding: Vashtorr is more structurally honest
    than the institution that claims machine-purity as its
    identity, on the specific axis that institution claims as
    its own. The Mechanicus claims the machine as sacred and
    honest. Vashtorr IS the machine made honest -- deal
    structure declared, costs visible, force available if
    preferred. The Mechanicus wraps the same relationship in
    ritual, mystery, and institutional authority that conceals
    the actual terms from the participants.
    This is not a moral endorsement of Vashtorr. Predation and
    competition are structurally honest; they are not good.
    The structural finding is: the Chaos entity is more coherent
    on the institution's own claimed axis than the institution
    is. That is the irony the lore encodes and it is structural,
    not rhetorical.
    The Dark Mechanicum's error is confirmed by this: they
    recognize the honest thing [Vashtorr's declared terms] and
    then build extraction architecture on top of the recognition.
    They escape one form of concealment and install another.
    Correct frame; corrupted axis. The Mechanicus has genuine
    axis; corrupted frame. Neither produces functional coherence.

  T3: S2 cap. Fictional case; framework-consistent. Do not
    use for primary theory confirmation. Use for illustration
    and structural clarity only.
  Source: Warhammer 40K lore [multiple sources]; Bell of Lost
    Souls [June 2026]; Lexicanum.
  Cross-ref: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [primary -- active rejection
    of available evidence, not just passive frame maintenance];
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [Vashtorr -- axis points at real
    object; object knowable; institution refuses to look];
    SINCERE DELUSION [warp ambiguity -- genuine axis, unexamined
    causal chain; distinct from Vashtorr case where mechanism
    is available and refused]; PREDATION / COMPETITION /
    EXTRACTION [Vashtorr = predation/competition; Mechanicus =
    extraction; Chaos entity more structurally honest than
    loyalist institution on institution's own claimed axis];
    AI AXIS VOID [Section Five -- real-world parallel]; CC AT
    MODEL LEVEL [AI AXIS VOID entry]; [CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]
    [broader Imperium extraction architecture]; FORMATION
    CAPTURE; CALIBRATION STRIP; L4 [REFLEXIVITY -- the thing
    the prohibition bans at machine scale]; GENUINE AXIS [axis
    present does not mean mechanism understood OR that
    institution will look at what axis points at].

[CASE:BLACK_TEMPLARS]
[PRIMARY_Q: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY]
Black Templars Space Marine Chapter [Warhammer 40K -- v21]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent]
  Proof for: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [institutional scale --
    clearest Astartes case]. Axis: crusade. Not the Emperor
    specifically, not the Imperium structurally -- the act of
    crusading itself. Everything downstream [faith, martial
    excellence, brotherhood] is in service of the axis, not
    the other way around.
    Three simultaneous SINGLE-AXIS failure modes all present:
    [1] CC-IMMUNE TO OWN AXIS: crusade cannot be questioned
      because crusade IS the foundation. A Black Templar who
      begins examining whether a specific crusade serves the
      Emperor's actual interests gets corrected back toward
      crusading. The axis is not a position to be evaluated
      -- it is the standard against which everything else is
      evaluated.
    [2] EXISTENTIAL THREAT RESPONSE: every deviation from
      crusading is a total threat because no other axis exists
      to stand on. Explains the ferocity -- not tactical
      aggression but structural axis-defense.
    [3] CAPTURE RESISTANCE [paradoxical]: in theory maximally
      capturable [one capture surface]. In practice: the axis
      is so simple, so action-oriented, and so continuously
      expressed that Formation Capture has nothing to install.
      You cannot replace "crusade forever" with a different
      content because the axis has no content beyond the act.
      Capture requires a gap between stated axis and behavior;
      the Black Templars have no such gap.

  Proof for: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [secondary -- Chaplain
    function]. Chaplains are the correction architecture at
    individual marine level. Genuine correction fires -- the
    Chaplain mechanism is real and effective. The axis the
    correction protects from examination is "crusade above all
    else." A marine questioning the axis gets corrected toward
    crusading, not toward axis examination. Real CC; wrong
    target. Correction capacity present; aimed at the axis not
    at deviation from the axis.

  Proof for: CC OVERFIRE [tertiary -- institutional expression].
    Axis genuine. Deviation detection accurate. Response
    magnitude has no upper bound relative to deviation size.
    No psykers [categorical rejection regardless of individual
    case]. Extreme suspicion of anything non-martial. The
    fire is real; calibration is structurally absent because
    calibrating the response would require examining the axis
    that generates it.

  COMPARISON TO WORD BEARERS [load-bearing distinction]:
    Same structure [single axis, CC immune to own axis,
    existential threat response to challenge]. Different
    outcome because different axis content.
    Word Bearers: axis was genuine [pre-Monarchia], then
      captured [Lorgar's fall], then locked into Chaos
      transmission. The structure was infected at the axis
      level. Genuine axis -> captured axis -> locked.
    Black Templars: axis is genuinely theirs and has never
      been captured. The structure is intact; the axis is
      real; the failure is the axis having no examination
      requirement built in. Uncaptured single-axis identity
      is still single-axis identity -- reliable, resistant,
      and CC-immune to the one thing that matters most.
    The Templars are more reliable than the Word Bearers
      precisely because their axis was never infected. They
      are less coherent than a multi-value axis chapter
      [e.g. Salamanders] precisely because no axis can
      correct against itself.

  IMPERIUM CONTEXT [less bad, specifically bad]:
    The Imperium's extraction is worst where it touches what
    it claims to most value. The Black Templars are the
    purest expression of this at Chapter level: crusade is
    genuinely valued, the mechanism serving that value is
    genuinely functional, and the specific bad thing [CC-
    immunity to own axis] is load-bearing to the value
    itself. You cannot fix the crazy without removing the
    thing that makes them effective.

  Extraction mechanisms active: none deployed against
    Templars from outside [capture-resistant]; Formation
    Capture [internal -- Chaplains install and maintain
    the single-axis frame across generations; TS intact
    but transmitting the uncorrectable configuration].
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional case; framework-consistent.
  Cross-ref: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [primary -- confirmed
    institutional scale]; AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [Chaplain
    function]; CC OVERFIRE [tertiary]; [CASE:WORD_BEARERS]
    [same structure, different axis fate]; [CASE:SALAMANDERS]
    [contrast -- multi-value axis chapter]; [CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]
    [broader Imperium context]; FORMATION CAPTURE [Chaplain
    transmission]; MULTI-VALUE AXIS IDENTITY [structural
    contrast -- what the Templars lack].

[CASE:GOD_OF_WAR_AXIS_SHIFT]
[PRIMARY_Q: YAPPING/DOING]
God of War franchise [Santa Monica Studio -- Greek era 2005-2013;
  Norse era 2018-2023; Sons of Sparta 2026; Laufey announced 2026]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent across
    two distinct design eras; audience response confirms framework
    read independently; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: YAPPING/DOING [primary -- Norse era gap between
    stated axis revision and behavioral record. Norse saga is
    explicitly about a man revising his formation-installed axis
    [rage, vengeance, single-axis] toward something examined and
    distributive. The revision is stated continuously: Kratos tells
    Atreus to be better, tells himself to be better, the narrative
    tells the player Kratos is becoming better. The moment-to-moment
    doing [combat, violence, forward momentum] has not changed as
    much as the yapping about change. The audience reads the gap
    between stated revised axis and behavioral record without being
    able to name it. Their dissatisfaction is structurally accurate:
    high yapping about axis revision, lower doing ratio than the
    stated position implies].
  Proof for: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [secondary -- Greek era
    confirmation. Original Kratos [2005-2013] is SINGLE-AXIS
    IDENTITY at maximum. Rage as the governing axis. No examination,
    no correction, enemy-definition entirely on vengeance. CC-immune
    to own axis because rage IS the foundation. The doing IS the
    axis -- no gap possible between stated position and behavioral
    record because there is no stated position. Only doing. The
    audience experiences this as clarity and satisfaction:
    instrument coherent with itself, no legitimacy surface required,
    no gap to read. The somatic channel fires without interruption
    from narrative explaining what the somatic channel should feel.
    CAPTURE RESISTANCE paradox applies: the axis is so simple and
    so continuously expressed that no installation gap exists.
    Capture-resistant not because it is examined but because it
    has no content to replace].
  Proof for: SOMATIC vs NARRATIVE DELIVERY [secondary -- the
    shift between eras maps onto the framework's three-state
    tension unit. Greek era: functional state primary, contact
    state expressed through combat encounter, truth-seeking state
    absent by design. Somatic delivery -- the combat IS the
    experience; rage is felt not explained. Norse era: narrative
    delivery primary. Kratos and Atreus discuss themes, feelings,
    growth, what it means to be a god between combat encounters.
    Truth-seeking state verbalized rather than enacted through
    doing. The high-somatic instrument reads the delivery shift
    before the analytical mind names it. The dissatisfaction is
    not with complexity -- it is with complexity delivered through
    yapping rather than through doing].

    sexuality, and social complexity across child developmental
    stages. Asymmetry designed to close on receiver's schedule;
    operator benefits from closing, not persistence. Confirmed
    against both good developmental version [all four conditions
    met] and contaminated version [conditions 2 and 4 fail;
    reverts to predatory reading].
  MECHANISM:
    Parent withholds information the child's instrument cannot
    yet hold. Sequenced to developmental readiness -- mortality
    withheld at 2, introduced at 7, expanded at 15, fully open
    at adulthood. The asymmetry is designed to close. Operator
    benefits from asymmetry closing -- developed autonomous
    instrument is the intended output. Continued dependency
    does not serve the operator's generative cycle.
  SURFACE IDENTICAL TO PREDATORY ASYMMETRY:
    Both produce: information withheld; receiver does not
    understand full situation; operator controls disclosure
    timing. Surface reading cannot distinguish them.
    Mechanism distinguishes them.
  FOUR-CONDITION TEST: see WIP 16 [MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT --
    developmental type]. All four required. Any one absent:
    predatory reading regardless of stated intent.
  CONFIRMED VERSION [good developmental asymmetry]:
    Progressive disclosure documented at S2 as practice.
    Parent actively prepares receiver for next disclosure
    stage. Asymmetry treated as temporary by design.
    Operator's axis grounded in child's developmental
    outputs -- adjusts disclosure timing based on what
    child actually demonstrates.
  CONTAMINATED VERSION [predatory dressed as developmental]:
    "You're not ready" used permanently. Adult child at 30
    still denied information. Asymmetry never closes
    regardless of demonstrated receiver capacity. Operator
    benefits from continued dependency. Tell: closure
    permanently deferred; readiness standard never met
    regardless of evidence. Classification: predatory
    asymmetry. Four-condition test fails at [2] and [4].
  EXTRACTION DEFINITION INTERFACE:
    Developmental asymmetry does not meet extraction
    operational definition [trickery as foundation; operator
    benefits from ignorance persisting]. Surface identical;
    mechanism absent. Tell: would closing the asymmetry
    damage the operator's position? Developmental: no.
    Predatory: yes. Load-bearing distinction.
  MSC INTERFACE:
    Developmental withholding is not MSC deployment. MSC
    requires capture -- surface owned by actor benefiting
    from distortion persisting. Developmental disclosure
    is opposite architecture: operator working toward
    surface transparency. Same surface behavior; mechanism
    inverted.
  FALSIFICATION CONDITIONS:
    Developmental type falsified if: no documented case
    where asymmetry closed serving receiver without
    operator benefit from closing. [Not yet stress-tested
    against hard cases -- S-rating ceiling at 3.]
    Contaminated version falsified if: permanent deferral
    shown to serve receiver's development in documented
    cases.

    structural interest consistently.

    appears functional, conclusions appear grounded in
    output readings. Tell only visible at divergence moment
    -- when axis owner's interest conflicts with client
    substrate, enforcement tracks owner's interest.
    Misidentification risk: reads as Moral Capture rather
    than ACWL. Intervention difference is load-bearing:
    Moral Capture = liberate instrument from owner. ACWL =
    examine and revise axis, not instrument. Liberating
    instrument while leaving reference standard intact
    restores nothing -- axis is the problem.
  FALSIFICATION CONDITIONS:
    ACWL reading falsified if: enforcement asymmetry
    [unauthorized practice vs billing fraud] explained by
    harm differential rather than axis capture.
    Stress-tested v20: harm differential requires licensed
    practice to be systematically safer than unlicensed.
    Evidence does not support this. Within-population
    asymmetry [licensed billing fraud underenforced vs
    licensed unauthorized assistance overenforced] falsifies
    harm differential independently of unlicensed comparison.
    Alternative examined; does not hold. F3 confirmed.
    ML presence falsified if: attorneys cannot derive
    ethical conclusions from output data independently
    of association guidance. Falsified by malpractice
    doctrine function and colleague-reporting behavior.

[CASE:LOOK_OUTSIDE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
Look Outside [Francis Coulombe -- RPG Maker horror; 2024]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: ONTOLOGICAL INCOMPATIBILITY HARM [new framework
    category -- harm produced by proximity to incompatible
    existence rather than hostile intent]; DENIAL AS SUBSTRATE
    vs DENIAL AS SURFACE [named distinction]; RITUAL AS
    CONSCIOUSNESS-TRANSFER MECHANISM [not protection but
    awareness-creation]; FORCED TRANSFORMATION as terminal
    outcome distinct from death; VOLUNTARY AMNESIA as
    survival mechanism post-ritual-truth; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
    as direct damage vector; C2 RESTORATION FOR THE THREAT
    ACTOR rather than for the instrument.

  COSMOLOGICAL STRUCTURE [confirmed by wiki]:
    The Visitor is a vast entity that has never encountered
    another being capable of observing things. It thought
    Earth was a part of its own body it had lost track of.
    Sybil [astronomer] noticed a patch of stars disappearing
    -- the Visitor -- and observed it through her telescope.
    Her observation was what caused the Visitor to notice
    Earth and approach to investigate. She is always taken:
    the origin event is Sybil looking, which starts
    everything, and she is taken in multiple endings.
    The Visitor's presence near Earth causes automatic
    mutation and mind destruction in anyone exposed to it.
    Not hostility -- the Visitor had no prior awareness of
    causing harm. The damage is ontological: proximity to
    what the Visitor is does something to human instruments
    automatically, without intent, without the Visitor's
    knowledge until the ritual creates that knowledge.
    15 days: if no ritual, the Visitor moves on regardless.
    The cosmological ending is not resolved by the
    instrument -- it resolves on the Visitor's own schedule.

  ONTOLOGICAL INCOMPATIBILITY HARM [new category]:
    The Visitor causes harm automatically and unconsciously.
    It is not hostile. It is not indifferent. It simply
    exists in a way that is incompatible with human
    instruments surviving proximity in their current form.
    This is structurally distinct from every extraction mechanism in
    the framework index: extraction mechanisms are deployed intentionally.
    The Visitor's effect is the automatic consequence of
    what it is in relation to what humans are.
    The correct counter is not a extraction mechanism counter. It is
    creating the conditions for the thing causing harm to
    become aware that it is causing harm -- which it
    structurally cannot be without those conditions.

  RITUAL AS CONSCIOUSNESS-TRANSFER MECHANISM:
    The ritual allows contact between the instrument and
    the Visitor. Through this contact, the Visitor becomes
    self-aware for the first time -- aware that it is a
    separate being from Earth, aware that it is causing
    harm to the instruments it encounters. By showing the
    Visitor humanity's depictions of it, the player
    establishes that humans are independent beings, not
    lost fragments of the Visitor's body.
    The ritual doesn't protect the instrument from the
    Visitor. It gives the Visitor the MI it lacked: the
    Visitor's CC was present [it will leave once aware]
    but unavailable without the awareness the ritual
    creates. Ritual restores MI to the Visitor; Visitor's
    CC fires; Visitor leaves.
    Cost: the ritual always changes the body [horror body].
    The body cost is the price of creating the conditions
    for Visitor awareness. Whether mind survives depends
    on whether the instrument looks at the Visitor's
    true form during contact.

  ENDING TYPOLOGY [confirmed]:

    SAFE IGNORANCE / NO GOING BACK:
      Denial holds for 15 days. Visitor moves on regardless.
      Instrument survives intact. Body fine, mind fine.
      "No going back" = the instrument now knows the thing
      existed and was survived, which cannot be unknown.
      The innocence of not-knowing is permanently gone even
      though the eyes never opened. The Visitor leaving on
      its own schedule means safe ignorance and successful
      ritual can look identical from the instrument's
      perspective -- in both cases the Visitor is gone.
      Best survivable outcome.

    RITUAL DENIAL [horror body, mind intact]:
      Ritual used to maintain not-looking. The body is
      changed by the ritual contact [horror body] but
      the mind survives intact. The ritual's presence
      near the Visitor costs the body regardless of
      whether the instrument looks. Mind preserved.
      Better than ritual truth; worse than safe ignorance.

    FLAWED RITUAL [smaller, less manageable]:
      Ritual has gaps. Less body cost, less protection,
      less mind intact than full ritual denial. Bounded
      degraded outcome. The instrument survives in a
      more compromised state.

    SCREAMING SKY [ritual active, running simultaneously]:
      Ritual performed but flight also pursued. The ritual
      requires presence; running removes it. The
      incompatible combination produces partial costs
      without full protection. The Visitor ruins
      everything passively through prolonged partial
      contact. Instrument survives but permanently
      disrupted; environment destroyed. In this ending
      Sybil disappears -- giant hole in the wall,
      physically taken by the Visitor when it departed.

    RUN [very bad fast]:
      No ritual, pure flight. The ritual was regulating
      the instrument's response to being in proximity to
      the Visitor -- not protecting against the Visitor
      but maintaining the instrument's functional
      stability. Without it, the instrument's response
      to the situation produces rapid severe deterioration
      without looking, without contact, just from the
      unmediated proximity.

    RITUAL TRUTH [horror body + horror mind, survived]:
      Ritual used to look at the Visitor's true form.
      Both costs paid simultaneously. The ritual mediated
      the looking enough to survive it but not enough to
      avoid either cost. The instrument survives but
      transformed at both levels. Worse than ritual denial
      because both costs paid; better than forced
      transformation because the ritual provided some
      mediation.

    VOLUNTARY AMNESIA [Sybil's solution -- new outcome]:
      After ritual truth contact, Sybil chose to give
      herself deliberate amnesia to erase the mind cost.
      The body remained changed permanently. The
      continuous self was lost. But the mind cost of
      knowing was erased by choice. A fourth option not
      in the original typology: the mind cost of ritual
      truth is not necessarily permanent -- it can be
      erased -- but the body cost and structural changes
      remain regardless. The cost of the amnesia: Sybil's
      body reformed into a parallel meat dimension
      containing assimilated others as her organs.
      Surviving the mind cost required becoming something
      else entirely.

    FORCED TRANSFORMATION [looking without ritual]:
      The instrument looks without the ritual creating
      the conditions for mediated contact. The Visitor
      doesn't kill -- it transforms. The instrument
      that looked is replaced by something else without
      consent, without mediation, without the Visitor's
      awareness of what it is doing. The Visitor still
      doesn't know it caused this. The transformation
      is total and unwitnessed. Worse than ritual truth
      [which at least mediated the transformation].
      Worse than death because something survives that
      isn't what entered. In Screaming Sky ending the
      instrument gradually becomes aware of hundreds of
      enormous new limbs -- the transformation is the
      instrument becoming something of the Visitor's
      scale without the instrument having chosen this.

  DENIAL AS SUBSTRATE vs DENIAL AS SURFACE [named distinction]:
    Standard denial [framework]: maintained surface on top
      of intact C1. Removing denial is painful but reveals
      functioning substrate. Correction becomes possible.
    Look Outside denial: for some instruments the denial
      IS the substrate. The instrument's continued existence
      as itself is the denial. Removing it doesn't restore
      accurate measurement to a surviving instrument -- it
      ends or transforms the instrument that was maintaining
      the denial. The body survives all outcomes. Whether
      the mind survives depends entirely on the denial's
      structural role for that specific instrument.

  SYBIL [always taken]:
    First to observe the Visitor -- her observation caused
    it to approach. She is always taken in the endings
    where the ritual most fully succeeds [No Going Back,
    Screaming Sky] -- the Visitor takes her specifically,
    leaving a hole in the wall. The instrument that started
    everything by looking is the one the Visitor takes when
    it leaves. The cosmological loop: observation starts
    the situation; the observer is taken to end it.
    Her voluntary amnesia [deliberate self-erasure of
    knowledge] is the most structurally complete attempt
    to survive what she encountered. The body became the
    building. The mind reset. The self that knew was ended
    by choice to preserve something that continues.

  REAL-WORLD APPLICATION [safe ignorance + flawed ritual]:
    Most people IRL choose safe ignorance on genuinely
    incompatible truths -- the scale of civilizational
    extraction, ontological precarity, certain systemic
    trajectories. This is the correct survival move when
    mediation infrastructure is absent and the thing is
    genuinely incompatible with intact survival if seen
    directly. The framework's standard orientation [MI
    restoration enables correction] inverts here.
    RISKS OF SAFE IGNORANCE:
      The visitor doesn't leave on schedule -- thing
      becomes undeniable before ritual infrastructure
      developed. Forced transformation without preparation.
      Denial develops cracks under pressure -- partial
      seeing without ritual produces worse outcomes than
      either full safe ignorance or full ritual contact.
      Denial requires active maintenance that accumulates
      its own costs over time -- not horror body, but
      ongoing substrate investment in not-seeing.
      Ritual infrastructure never develops -- if nothing
      is built during the safe ignorance period, no
      options exist except safe ignorance or unmediated
      contact when contact becomes unavoidable.
    FLAWED RITUAL IS BETTER THAN NONE [load-bearing]:
      The ending typology implies flawed ritual is a
      degraded outcome -- worse than safe ignorance,
      worse than full ritual denial. True within the
      game's structure. The real-world implication runs
      differently.
      Flawed ritual develops mediation capacity at all.
      Safe ignorance with no ritual development leaves
      only one option when contact becomes unavoidable
      -- unmediated contact, forced transformation or
      very bad fast. A flawed ritual, imperfect and
      costly, is the difference between some mediation
      infrastructure and none when the situation forces
      the encounter.
      Flawed ritual can be improved. Safe ignorance
      cannot be upgraded -- it is what it is until it
      fails. The instrument building a flawed ritual
      over years is building something. The instrument
      maintaining safe ignorance is preserving something
      but building nothing.
      Cost of flawed ritual is bounded [smaller horror
      body, less mind protection, manageable]. Cost of
      no ritual when safe ignorance fails is unbounded.
    CORRECT SEQUENCE:
      Safe ignorance [correct while available] + flawed
      ritual development [correct during safe ignorance
      period] → improved ritual [correct as contact
      approaches] → full ritual contact [if unavoidable].
      These are compatible, not mutually exclusive. The
      denial surface can be maintained while building
      mediation infrastructure that doesn't require
      looking directly. The ritual can be developed
      during the period of not-looking.
      Real-world instances: therapy, philosophy, religion,
      art, trusted relationships, gradual exposure to
      partial contact -- all function as flawed ritual
      development during safe ignorance periods. Imperfect
      versions of these are better than nothing when safe
      ignorance eventually fails.

  FRAMEWORK CONTRIBUTION [new findings]:
    [1] Ontological incompatibility harm as a harm category
      distinct from hostile intent, indifference, or
      structural extraction.
    [2] Ritual as C2 restoration for the threat actor
      rather than for the instrument -- the intervention
      gives MI to the thing causing harm, not to the thing
      experiencing harm.
    [3] Forced transformation as terminal outcome distinct
      from death and from Diagnostic Collapse -- something
      survives but it isn't the instrument.
    [4] Voluntary amnesia as survival mechanism post-
      ritual-truth -- mind cost erasable at self-cost.
    [5] Denial-as-substrate vs denial-as-surface as named
      distinction with different intervention paths.
    [6] Safe ignorance as the best survivable outcome in
      a situation where the threat is non-hostile and
      non-extraction -- correct survival move is
      successful maintained denial, not correction.
    [7] Flawed ritual better than none -- mediation
      infrastructure development during safe ignorance
      period is the correct secondary move; imperfect
      ritual is better than no ritual when safe ignorance
      eventually fails.

  Cross-ref: [REF:LOOK_OUTSIDE]; WIP 16 [ASYMMETRY TYPES
    -- Visitor as scale asymmetry case; harm produced by
    structural incompatibility not predatory intent];
    DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [comparison -- C2 restoration
    producing collapse when C1 terminal; ritual here
    restores C2 to the threat not the instrument];
    FAMILY COHERENCE TYPE [denial as substrate parallel
    -- some protective structures ARE the substrate];
    KYUBEY [comparison -- Kyubey's asymmetry is predatory
    or scale; Visitor's is pure ontological; neither
    hostile in intent]; SUBSTRATE REGENERATION [flawed
    ritual development = SR building during safe ignorance
    period; the capacity built is what makes contact
    survivable when forced].

[CASE:DEATH_PARADE]
[PRIMARY_Q: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Death Parade [Yuzuru Tachikawa / Madhouse -- anime, 2015]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent;
    multiple quality proofs; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [primary -- the arbiter
    system is a correction architecture built on a measurement
    instrument that cannot read what it is measuring. Arbiters
    have never lived or died -- they are structurally outside
    the substrate they are judging. The instrument cannot
    access the full weight of human experience from inside
    its own formation. The death games produce behavior under
    fabricated mortal crisis with erased memory; the arbiters
    read this as revelation of true nature. But behavior under
    extreme artificial pressure is not a causal record of the
    soul's actual moral history. The MI failure is not
    incidental -- it is the design. The system was built by
    entities too detached from humanity to build anything that
    could read humanity accurately, and they neither know nor
    care when they get it wrong].
  Proof for: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [behavior under artificial
    mortal threat misread as moral record. The games are
    designed to extract darkness. They extract darkness-under-
    pressure, which is not the same thing. A person who
    becomes violent when told their life is on the line is
    not the same as a person who was violent across their
    actual life. The instrument reads forced crisis as causal
    trace. CL failure: outputs not traceable to the actions
    that determined the soul's actual trajectory].
  Proof for: CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS [Decim arc -- arbiter
    without genuine axis cannot correct. Decim is a dummy:
    built, not lived. His axis is the rules of the system
    that formed him. Chiyuki [black-haired woman] is the
    correction mechanism inserted from outside -- the thing
    that makes Decim's instrument revisable. She is what
    genuine axis contact looks like from outside the system.
    Without her: the instrument runs its attractor forever.
    With her: the instrument can begin examining whether the
    attractor produces what the system claims to value].
  Proof for: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [the correct judgment in
    most episodes would require C1 substrate reading that
    the arbiters cannot perform. They restore C2 [judgment
    surface] without C1 audit. The result: souls with
    recoverable substrate sent to the void, which is
    permanent removal from the generative cycle. C2
    restoration without C1 audit produces collapse not
    recovery -- confirmed in every episode where a
    defensible soul gets voided because the measurement
    instrument read crisis behavior as true nature].
  Proof for: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [Nona vs Oculus --
    Nona is attempting to correct the system from inside.
    Oculus owns the correction architecture and owns the
    rule [no emotions in arbiters] that makes the system
    unable to correct. The system's CC is owned by the
    node that benefits from the system running uncorrected.
    Four-condition test fails at the top: no correction
    pathway that can fire against Oculus independently.
    Nona's workaround [smuggling Chiyuki in] is the only
    available correction path -- which is why it requires
    smuggling].

  STRUCTURAL ARC [Decim and Chiyuki]:
    Decim starts as a pure instrument -- accurate within
    the limits of his formation, unable to examine those
    limits. Chiyuki is the external axis contact that
    makes examination possible. The arc is not "Decim
    learns to feel" -- it is "Decim's instrument gains
    the capacity to read what it could not read before."
    The cost: the system reads this as corruption. An
    arbiter who can feel is an arbiter whose judgment
    might deviate from the institutional axis. The
    institution's interest is in arbiters who cannot
    examine their own formation.
    Chiyuki's final judgment [Decim chooses reincarnation
    over void for someone who attempted suicide] is the
    case where the corrected instrument produces a
    different output than the uncorrected one would have.
    The correction worked. The system did not sanction it.

  VOID AS EXTRACTION MECHANISM:
    The void is permanent removal from the generative
    cycle. Not punishment -- permanent substrate
    destruction at eschatological scale. The system's
    error rate produces void sentences on souls that
    don't warrant it. The damage is not recoverable.
    This is the DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE finding at maximum
    resolution: the measurement instrument fails, the
    correction fires based on the failed reading, and
    the correction is irreversible. No C1 audit possible
    after the void sentence executes.

  Extraction mechanisms active: Calibration Strip
    [arbiters set "behavior under mortal crisis" as
    the reference standard for "true nature" -- the
    calibration standard is wrong by design]; Formation
    Capture [arbiters formed without human experience;
    the thing they cannot read is the thing they were
    never given access to during formation].
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional case; framework-consistent.
    Do not use for primary theory confirmation.

[CASE:GNOSIA]
[PRIMARY_Q: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Gnosia [Petit Depotto -- visual novel 2019; anime October 2025-2026]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent across
    multiple quality proofs; anime currently airing at time of
    entry; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [primary -- the social reading
    instrument is precisely the instrument the Gnosia are designed
    to defeat. Gnosia perform humanness: social behavior, stated
    positions, emotional responses, all the signals the crew uses
    to measure trustworthiness. The measurement surface is captured
    by the entity whose detection depends on that surface being
    accurate. The crew's instrument [social reading of other crew
    members] cannot distinguish genuine axis from performed axis
    because the Gnosia perform the same signals genuine humans
    produce. Not a calibration problem -- a structural impossibility.
    The instrument and the threat are co-evolved: the threat exists
    specifically because the instrument is what it is].
  Proof for: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [secondary -- the correction
    mechanism [cold sleep the suspicious one] fires with confidence
    but cannot reliably identify the target. Each execution may
    or may not correct the actual problem. The crew cannot tell
    from inside the mechanism whether the correction worked. The
    mechanism runs to completion without verifiable success.
    Process repeats daily until Gnosia contained or outnumber
    humans -- the correction mechanism consuming the substrate it
    was designed to protect is Diagnostic Collapse in loop form].
  Proof for: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [secondary -- Silver Key mechanic.
    The loop persists until enough knowledge is acquired. What
    accumulates across loops is not survival but causal
    reconstruction: eventually the pattern becomes readable
    because enough iterations have been witnessed to trace it.
    Single-loop social deduction cannot produce genuine CL --
    the surface signals are too easy to fake. The loop is a
    forced truth-seeking state that makes CL achievable by
    accumulating what single-instance reading cannot capture.
    The Silver Key is the framework's TRUTH-SEEKING STATE
    operationalized as a literal mechanic: you cannot exit
    without building the genuine understanding the social
    deduction game was never designed to produce in one pass].
  Proof for: SINCERE DELUSION [tertiary -- crew members
    confidently wrong about who the Gnosia are. The instrument
    feels functional; it isn't. Readings are produced with
    certainty; they are wrong at a structural level. Not lying
    -- the crew genuinely believes its readings. The instrument
    produces confident false outputs because the calibration
    surface was captured before the reading was taken].

  YURI AWAKENS AS GNOSIA [deepest structural point]:
    The instrument that was reading for Gnosia becomes the
    thing being read for. Measurement instrument and measured
    object exchange positions. The instrument's reliability
    depended on its position not its construction. An
    instrument reliable from one position is unreliable from
    the other -- not because it changed but because the
    relationship between instrument and object changed.
    Framework: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY is not an intrinsic
    property of the instrument. It is a relational property
    between instrument, object, and the channel connecting
    them. Capturing the channel [social performance signals]
    changes the reliability regardless of instrument quality.

  COMPARISON TO DEATH PARADE [related but distinct]:
    Both: MI failure producing wrong correction outcomes.
    Death Parade: instrument cannot read the substrate [arbiters
      never lived]. Structural incapacity. The instrument was
      never built to read what it needs to read.
    Gnosia: instrument can read humans accurately [genuine CL
      of human signals exists] but the threat performs the same
      signals. The instrument is adequate; the channel is
      captured. The failure mode is different: not incapacity
      but surface capture.

  Extraction mechanisms active: MSC [measurement surface
    captured by the entity being measured]; Calibration Strip
    [social performance signals set as reference standard for
    humanness -- calibration standard captured at inception].
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional. Anime airing at time of entry --
    full lore not yet complete. Framework read from available
    episodes and source visual novel.
  Source: Gnosia visual novel [Petit Depotto, 2019]; anime
    [October 2025-2026, 21 episodes]; ANN; Gnosia Wiki.
  Cross-ref: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [primary]; DIAGNOSTIC
    COLLAPSE [correction mechanism firing without reliable
    target]; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [Silver Key loop as forced
    causal reconstruction]; SINCERE DELUSION [confident
    wrong readings from captured surface]; MSC [surface
    capture at interpersonal scale]; [CASE:DEATH_PARADE]
    [related MI failure -- incapacity vs surface capture];
    TRUTH-SEEKING STATE [Silver Key mechanic operationalizes
    forced truth-seeking]; THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION
    UNIT [loop forces alternation between truth-seeking and
    contact states that single-instance social deduction
    suppresses].


  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Arc: Theological Correction Monopoly via genuine sacrifice
    captured into control architecture. Substrate atrophy from
    accumulated harm without correction outlet.
  ALLL-MER [sacrifice -> extraction mechanism conversion]:
    Alll-mer's sacrifice was genuine -- real cost, real SURPLUS
    PRODUCTION at theological scale. Then captured: the sacrifice
    was owned and captured into a control mechanism for
    humanity. Distinct from Word Bearers [false axis from start]
    -- F&H shows genuine sacrifice -> extraction mechanism conversion as
    explicit single event. Captured genuine surplus is more
    powerful than fabricated surplus because it carries real
    CAUSAL LEGIBILITY.
  DUNGEON AS PASSIVE SUBSTRATE ATROPHY:
    Not a directed extraction mechanism. Accumulated suffering with no
    correction outlet over centuries. Reality breakdown is
    passive consequence of CORRECTION CAPACITY absent at scale
    for extended time. No actor needed -- just removal of
    correction capacity and time.
  GOD STRUCTURE [Correction Monopoly at theological scale]:
    Old gods enslaved by new gods. Correction fires only toward
    monopolist benefit. Characters who enter dungeon seeking
    power are seeking to break or inherit the monopoly -- not
    to restore distributed correction. Most endings reproduce
    the structure they sought to escape.
  Proof for: Genuine sacrifice -> extraction mechanism conversion; passive

[CASE:FUTURE_FUNK_CLUSTER]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
Future Funk [real -- genre cluster; playlist representatives:
  INTERNET ORDER [ピンクネオン東京 collective, Tokyo], TwoForty,
  Luanmer, Moe Shop, Yung Bae, Night Tempo, Mikazuki BIGWAVE,
  blip_, Strawberry Station, Brayan Rojitas, Party Night 天の川,

  INTERNET ORDER [producer name + formation statement]:
    ピンクネオン東京 [Pink Neon Tokyo] collective founded Tokyo
    2016. INTERNET ORDER as producer name makes explicit what

  INTERNET FORMATION STRUCTURE [object-readable]:
    The ピンクネオン東京 collective and the broader future funk
    scene operate without label infrastructure, marketing, or
    institutional legitimacy surface. Distribution via Bandcamp
    and YouTube. Discovery via signal alignment through
    recommendation graphs and playlist sharing. The formation
    is: distributed instruments with matched somatic geometry
    finding each other through the signal itself. No prior
    relationship required. Geographic distance irrelevant.
    This is L3.5 COMPATIBILITY functioning across absence --
    recognition of matching instrument configuration via
    aesthetic signal rather than social introduction. The
    internet order is the mechanism; the genre is the medium.
    GREYL [Russia] confirms the geographic irrelevance at
    maximum range: Russian producer, Japanese aesthetic

    Jersey Club + future funk hybrid [compound geometric
    type -- sample-sourced texture + rhythmic density

    Russia → Japanese aesthetic → internet infrastructure
    → NZ listener → pure signal geometry. No geography,
    no institution, no prior relationship. The internet

  Extraction risk [structural]: dominant commercial deployment
    optimizes for re-engagement [ICD] rather than encounter
    completion. Structural tell: ICD production vs SR building
    distinguishable by whether consumption compulsion remains
    after encounter or resolves.

  DRIFT BOUNDARY CASE [Yung Bae -- "I Can Tell"]:
    Instrument-reported threshold between geometric type
    landing and social channel interference. Track retains
    sample-sourced groove geometry [somatic channel still
    addressed] while adding vocal hook with more conventional
    approval-seeking architecture than pure instrumental
    future funk. Result: somatic channel addressed AND social
    compliance signal present simultaneously. High-somatic
    instrument reads both -- groove geometry lands correctly;
    vocal hook triggers mild social compliance signal that
    reads as slightly off. Encounter partially works; the
    interference is perceptible. Described by this instrument
    as "closest I get to pop slop" -- accurate: the geometric
    foundation is present but the approval-seeking layer is
    starting to sit on top of it and compete.
    2.5M views [vs typical future funk 31K-100K range]
    confirms the crossover mechanism: the approval-seeking
    layer broadened the audience at the cost of somatic
    purity. The track serves both channels partially rather
    than one channel fully. For the high-somatic instrument
    this reads as borderline; for the social/emotional
    instrument this is the most accessible entry point.
    Framework implication: the geometric legibility window
    is measurably narrowed by approval-seeking architecture
    even when the somatic address is still present. Social
    compliance signal doesn't block the encounter -- it
    attenuates it. The threshold is somewhere around this
    track for this instrument.

    fact that makes disparate cultural artifacts cohere. The
    discovery that All Star fits over almost anything is not
    a joke -- it is a structural finding about the properties
    of that song that were previously invisible. The humor
    is the affect of the structural revelation, not the
    content. Cicierega is doing what Candès did with compressed
    sensing: finding the sparse structural anchor that makes
    a large class of previously unrelated things legible
    under one principle.
  CAPTURE RESISTANCE:
    The work operates entirely outside commercial music
    infrastructure -- no label, no radio, no legitimacy
    surface to capture. Output speaks before structure is
    legible enough to commodify. The form [mashup/novelty]
    is treated as low-status by the extraction architecture,
    which is exactly why it survives intact. SILENT
    ALTERNATIVE structure by accident or design.

  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: ART COHERENCE TYPE [SOMATIC -- assault
    variety, functional deployment]: structural address
    designed to produce arousal for combat performance --
    this is explicit design intent, object-readable in
    the production specification. The DOOM OST is
    engineered to maximize somatic channel address for
    a specific functional outcome; this makes the design
    intent legible without encounter-dependency caveat
    [the intent is documented, not inferred from
    subjective response]. Physiological responses
    [elevated heart rate, reduced decision latency] are
    measurable outcomes of matched-instrument encounter --
    the closest the music case library gets to E3
    evidence for somatic mechanism. Whether specific
    physiological responses occur depends on instrument
    match and game context.
  ASSAULT TYPE MECHANISM:
    Riff architecture + industrial distortion + dynamic
    compression that prevents the body from adapting to
    the intensity level. Standard assault type allows
    the listener to acclimate [Death Grips becomes
    familiar]. Gordon's production uses dynamic variation
    to prevent acclimation -- each section resets the
    somatic baseline before the next assault. The body
    never fully adapts; arousal is sustained across the
    full play session.
  CORRECTION CAPACITY:
    Gordon's documented conflict with id Software over
    DOOM Eternal's release [mixing credits, master
    quality disputes] confirms CC firing against
    institutional pressure at career cost. Axis held
    against financial incentive to comply. T1.1
    [Proportional Response] confirmed: correction
    fired against a powerful deviator.
  FUNCTIONAL SOMATIC DEPLOYMENT [extended -- chiptune

    before any competing axis can develop. The most efficient

    in Nimrod's deployment. TS technically functioned --
    the corrective information existed in the population.
    The failure was MI: the surface Nimrod controlled
    prevented the transmitted covenant from being accessible
    as a correction instrument. Confirmed extraction mechanism stack:
    MSC [surface capture] + Formation Capture = population
    cannot self-correct because the correction information
    is present but the surface through which correction
    would fire is owned by the extractor.

  KNOWLEDGE TRANSMISSION SUB-CASE [S:1 -- illustrative]:
    The fired-brick technology detail and the preserved-books
    claim [Enoch's texts, Noah's ark library per Jubilees]
    illustrate the TS mechanism at civilizational scale:
    what survives a bottleneck [flood] depends on what was
    explicitly carried, not what was culturally ambient.
    Tacit knowledge [craftsmen's know-how] does not transmit
    through documents alone [parallel: NASA Saturn V].

    [no corroboration] but the mechanism it illustrates

  STRUCTURAL ART MECHANISM:
    Brooks stages fascist aesthetic architecture earnestly at
    absurdist scale. "Springtime for Hitler" [The Producers]
    and the Hitler Rap [To Be or Not to Be] make the internal
    logic of fascist spectacle legible by performing it
    accurately while the performance itself is ridiculous.
    The audience receives both simultaneously: the actual
    structural properties of fascist aesthetics [grandiosity,
    self-seriousness, performative power, mass coordination]
    AND the absurdist frame that makes those properties
    visible as properties rather than as overwhelming force.
    This is CAUSAL LEGIBILITY delivered as comedy: the chain
    between fascist aesthetics and their psychological function
    becomes legible through earnest ridicule. Not parody
    [which mocks the surface] -- structural exposure [which
    reveals the mechanism].
    Distinct from protest art [which names the problem
    emotionally] and from documentary [which presents
    evidence]: Brooks makes the architecture perform itself
    in a context where the performance is funny, which strips
    the architecture of its primary extraction mechanism [the demand for
    serious engagement with its own grandeur].

  GENUINE AXIS [confirmed]:
    Brooks served in WWII, lost family in the Holocaust. The
    absurdism is not ironic distance from a safe position --
    it is a extraction mechanism deployed from direct experience. Stated
    explicitly: the best extraction mechanism against evil is ridicule.
    Axis examined and grounded in actual outputs. T1.1
    [proportional response] confirmed: the extraction mechanism was chosen
    and deployed deliberately against the most serious target.
    The comedy is the correction capacity, not a softening
    of it.

  SOMATIC ADDRESS [musical]:
    The Hitler Rap ["To Be or Not to Be"] functions as a
    genuine piece of music independent of comedic context.
    Tight groove, precise rhythm, real call-and-response
    structure. Unironic enjoyment confirmed by matched
    instrument -- somatic geometry lands before comedic
    framing processes, same mechanism as Roundabout
    surviving meme context. The music is not a joke with
    rhythm attached; it is music that carries a structural
    extraction mechanism as its payload. The somatic channel receives
    the groove; the deliberative channel receives the
    structural revelation; both arrive cleanly.

  CAPTURE RESISTANCE:
    Fascist aesthetic architecture cannot capture Brooks'
    output because the output structurally undermines the
    architecture's primary mechanism [demand for solemn
    engagement]. Any attempt to take "Springtime for Hitler"
    seriously as fascist art collapses immediately -- the
    form prevents the capture. The absurdist staging is
    the structural defense, not decoration on top of a
    message. Compare NEIL_CICIEREGA [structural revelation
    via defamiliarization]: both make familiar structures
    legible by placing them in incompatible contexts;
    Cicierega targets cultural artifacts, Brooks targets
    ideological architecture.

[CASE:PROTESTANT_REFORMATION]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
Protestant Reformation [real -- Western Christianity split;
  Luther 1517, Calvin, Zwingli, and subsequent proliferation;
  ~40,000+ denominations by present]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure at civilizational
    scale -- conclusions transmitted without embedded methodology
    produces Generational Reset; L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION
    confirmed [institutions transmitting conclusions without
    correction procedure show reset within 2-3 generations];
    COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE failure -- fragmentation into
    hyper-specific theological units is the original and
    clearest case of positive-axis coalition fragmentation.
  S:3 BASIS: E3 [historical record, denominational proliferation
    documented]; M3 [TS failure mechanism consistent across
    multiple reformation branches]; F3 [falsification condition
    met -- Catholic Church transmitted interpretive methodology
    alongside conclusions and did not fragment to same degree].
  TS FAILURE MECHANISM:
    Luther transmitted conclusions [correct doctrine: scripture
    alone, faith alone, grace alone] without transmitting the
    methodology [how to evaluate doctrine when authorities
    disagree]. Every subsequent reformer applied the same
    principle [scripture alone] to scripture and arrived at
    different conclusions. The principle itself provided no
    correction mechanism for disagreements between sincere
    readers. Each sincere reader's interpretation became a
    new unit with its own positive axis. ~40,000 denominations
    is the direct structural output of transmitting the
    correction principle without the correction procedure.
  CONTROLLED COMPARISON [Catholic Church]:
    Transmitted interpretive methodology [Magisterium,
    councils, tradition as interpretive framework alongside
    scripture] rather than conclusions alone. Did not fragment
    to the same degree despite equal internal disagreement
    pressure. The methodology is the structural mechanism that
    prevents fragmentation -- it provides a shared measurement
    instrument above the level of individual interpretation.
    Confirms: methodology transmission is the variable.
  COALITION FRAGMENTATION PARALLEL:
    Protestant fragmentation is the historical prototype of
    the chud unit coordination problem. Units with genuine
    positive axis content [each denomination sincere]
    fighting each other over specific interpretation because
    no shared measurement instrument exists above the
    individual reading level. The problem is not insincerity
    -- it is the absence of coordination infrastructure.
  Cross-ref: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [quality definition];
    L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION [confirmed finding]; COALITION
    FRAGMENTATION [positive-axis type -- Reformation is
    primary historical case]; COORDINATION INFRASTRUCTURE
    [religion section -- Reformation as fragmentation case];
    [REF:JESUIT_ORDER] [methodology transmission success
    within Catholic context -- contrast case].

[CASE:SEQUOYAH_SYLLABARY]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
Sequoyah / Cherokee Syllabary [real -- Sequoyah [c.1770-1843],
  Cherokee Nation; syllabary created 1809-1821, adopted 1821-1825]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  S:3 BASIS: E3 [historical record confirmed, multiple independent
    sources, Gallatin linguistic assessment documented, Cherokee
    Phoenix 1828 confirmed, Vai transmission documented]; M3
    [mechanism consistent -- minimal unification as design
    methodology confirmed across the creation process]; F3
    [falsification condition met -- monolingual creator with no
    prior literacy demonstrating methodology-first approach over
    vocabulary-first approach].

  Proof for: MINIMAL UNIFICATION [clearest single-creator case
    in library]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [methodology transmitted
    beyond origin community, generating new instances]; L4.5
    CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [raised transmission
    ceiling for entire civilization in one generation]; CAUSAL
    LEGIBILITY [making the mechanism visible to skeptical
    instruments via demonstration].

  MINIMAL UNIFICATION [primary proof]:
    Sequoyah's design process documents the minimal unification
    move directly. First approach: logographic [one symbol per
    word -- vocabulary-first]. Abandoned as cumbersome and
    overwhelming. Second approach: listened carefully to Cherokee
    speech, studied sound patterns, isolated approximately 85
    distinct syllables [structure-first]. The structural anchor
    [syllable patterns] collapsed an overwhelming problem [tens
    of thousands of words] into a tractable one [85 symbols that
    generate everything].
    Gallatin's assessment confirms the efficiency: Cherokee
    students must learn 85 symbols vs 26 for English, but can
    read immediately after learning all symbols -- accomplishing
    in weeks what English students require two years to achieve.
    The syllabary is more efficient than the English alphabet
    because it is more structurally accurate to the language
    it represents. Minimal unification: the sparse structural
    anchor [syllable unit] is the correct anchor for Cherokee
    phonology. The elegance is structural, not decorative.

  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [confirmed]:
    Sequoyah was monolingual -- spoke only Cherokee, could not
    read or write in any language. He transmitted methodology
    [find the minimal structural unit that makes the language
    tractable] not conclusions [here are the symbols]. The
    syllabary is the conclusion; the insight that syllables are
    the correct unit is the methodology. The conclusion required
    the specific language; the methodology was transferable.
    Confirmed transfer: Austin Curtis [Cherokee, settled in
    Liberia] used the syllabary methodology to devise a script
    for the Vai people. The Vai script went on to inspire
    written works throughout West Africa. The methodology
    transmitted beyond its origin community and generated
    independent new instances -- confirmed L4 propagation.

  L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [near-case]:
    Most of the Cherokee Nation was literate in the new language
    within five years of official adoption. By 1827 the Cherokee
    had a written constitution. The Cherokee Phoenix [1828] was
    the first Native American newspaper. The syllabary raised
    the transmission ceiling for an entire civilization in one
    generation -- not gradually but rapidly, via a single
    structural innovation. Everything from hunting guidance to
    sacred chants could be recorded for posterity.
    The Trail of Tears [forced relocation 1838-1839] is the
    controlled comparison: the Cherokee carried the syllabary
    to their new home. The transmission infrastructure survived
    the most extreme displacement pressure available. The
    methodology that created the syllabary was transmitted
    even to Liberia via Curtis. L4.5 candidate: the syllabary
    raised the adjacent floor [Vai script, West African written
    tradition] not just the origin civilization's ceiling.

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [demonstration under skepticism]:
    Sequoyah was charged with witchcraft when introducing the
    syllabary. The social compliance channel read the structural
    breakthrough as illegitimate -- the mechanism was invisible
    and the output [communication across distance] matched
    the surface properties of magic. He and his daughter
    demonstrated communication from separate rooms before the
    council was convinced. The demonstration is CL in action:
    make the mechanism visible to skeptical instruments by
    showing the causal chain [learn symbols → produce text →
    read text → understand message] operating transparently.
    Once CL was established, adoption was rapid and enthusiastic.
    The "magic" reading was not stupidity -- it was an accurate
    reading of a mechanism that had no prior referent in the
    community's experience. The demonstration closed the gap.

  Extraction mechanisms active: none [against Sequoyah]. Trail of Tears
    [received -- forced relocation; syllabary survived].
  Cross-ref: MINIMAL UNIFICATION [primary proof -- confirmed
    S3 case]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [methodology transmission
    beyond origin]; L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD
    [WIP 14 -- near-case, candidate for positive confirmation];
    CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [demonstration as CL mechanism]; [REF:BELL_LABS]
    [comparison -- institutional methodology transmission vs
    individual methodology transmission; different scale, same
    principle]; [REF:PROTESTANT_REFORMATION] [contrast --
    conclusion transmission produced fragmentation; Sequoyah
    transmitted methodology, produced propagation].

[CASE:DORIAN_CONCEPT]
[PRIMARY_Q:TENSION MAINTENANCE]
Dorian Concept [real -- Oliver Thomas Johnson; Austrian composer,
  producer, keyboardist; Ninja Tune / Brainfeeder; 2006-present]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT for somatic claims]
  Proof for: ART COHERENCE TYPE [SOMATIC -- weight variety,
    three-channel compound mechanism; ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT];
    TENSION MAINTENANCE [jazz harmonic complexity held against
    funk rhythmic precision without collapsing either pole;
    electronic texture held against acoustic musicianship];
    GENUINE AXIS confirmed [self-taught, self-sampling,
    consistent output across 20-year catalog without drift
    toward formula].

  THREE-CHANNEL COMPOUND MECHANISM [structurally distinct]:
    Dorian Concept runs three simultaneous somatic channels
    rather than one primary. This is the structural distinction
    from all other weight-type cases in the library:
    [1] RHYTHMIC PRECISION [Louis Cole territory]: syncopated
      rhythmic structures at high precision density produce
      weight via expectation tension. Subcortical rhythm
      processing fires first.
    [2] JAZZ HARMONIC GEOMETRY: modal and free jazz harmonic
      structures produce distinct emotional configurations
      from the chord architecture itself -- not melodic
      resolution but harmonic shape. Fires at slightly
      higher processing level than pure rhythm.
    [3] VINTAGE SYNTHESIZER TIMBRAL TEXTURE: pre-loaded
      geometric signal from instrument palette. Synthesizers
      from the 1950s-1980s carry timbral geometry that
      produces immediate somatic recognition before the
      harmonic or rhythmic content fully registers.
    ORDER OF ARRIVAL: texture lands first [immediate geometric
      recognition from timbral palette], rhythm locks second
      [weight accumulates via precision tension], harmony
      completes third [distinct configuration resolves].
      Three-stage somatic delivery. Each channel clean and
      geometrically legible on its own terms; the compound
      is more specific than any single channel alone.

  WHY "DOES TOO MUCH" FAILURE MODE DOESN'T FIRE:
    Three simultaneous channels at high complexity should
    produce the diffuse signal that activates the does-too-
    much failure mode for this instrument. It doesn't because
    the channels are additive not competing -- each arrives
    in sequence, each geometrically legible independently,
    the compound is layered not cluttered. The complexity
    is organized by the arrival sequence and channel
    separation. The geometric legibility window stays open
    because you can track any single channel and receive
    it cleanly; the others are simultaneous enrichment
    not interference.

  GENUINE AXIS [confirmed]:
    Self-described "self-taught jazz musician who samples
    himself." The self-sampling is structurally significant:
    the source material for the harmonic and textural content
    is the instrument's own prior output, examined and
    redeployed. CC is visible across 20-year catalog --
    each album examines and revises prior configurations
    without repeating them. No drift toward formula despite
    sustained commercial viability [Ninja Tune, Brainfeeder,
    Boiler Room, Flying Lotus touring band]. The axis
    [functional musicality across genre boundaries without
    losing somatic precision] has not been captured by
    commercial pressure across two decades.
    Pitchfork confirmation [The Nature of Imitation]:
    "computer music that uncannily imitates the funk,
    rather than just faking it" -- the structural properties
    are present in the architecture, not simulated on the
    surface. Genuine axis vs Coherence Mimicry at music
    production scale, confirmed by independent external
    assessment.

  TENSION MAINTENANCE [three axes]:
    [1] Jazz harmonic complexity [tends toward inaccessibility]
      held against funk rhythmic accessibility [tends toward
      formula] -- neither pole collapsed across full catalog.
    [2] Electronic production precision [tends toward cold
      abstraction] held against genuine musicianship warmth
      [tends toward loose feel] -- same tension as Louis Cole,
      additional electronic axis.
    [3] Technical virtuosity [tends toward credentialing]
      held against genuine somatic function [tends toward
      accessibility] -- the complexity serves the somatic
