Anderson et al. [Psychological Bulletin 2015 + Psychological Science 2012]
[CONFIRMATION -- L3.5 SR correlation; sociometric vs socioeconomic
  status distinction; genuine contribution as wellbeing mechanism]
Status defined as "the respect, admiration, and voluntary deference
  individuals are afforded by others" [Anderson, Hildreth, Howland,
  UC Berkeley 2015] -- explicitly separated from power, financial
  success, and need to belong. Sociometric status [respect within
  face-to-face groups] tracked subjective wellbeing more closely
  than socioeconomic status [2012].
Framework confirmation: L3.5 RECOGNITION in genuine face-to-face
  groups is the load-bearing wellbeing variable, not positional rank.
  Sociometric status is a byproduct of actual contribution to people
  you know -- it tracks whether SP is occurring in the immediate
  relational environment. Socioeconomic status is positional and
  can decouple from generative function entirely.
  Confirms framework prediction: axis oriented toward genuine
  contribution in real relational contexts [L3.5 functioning]
  outperforms axis oriented toward positional extraction.
  The drive for recognition-in-real-groups is deep and basic --
  which simultaneously confirms L3.5 as load-bearing substrate
  AND identifies it as the primary target for extraction
  architecture [synthetic recognition at scale harvests the
  same drive without completing the transfer].
Classification: CONFIRMATION [external E3 evidence for L3.5 SR
  correlation; framework prediction confirmed from outside].
Source: Anderson et al. Psychological Bulletin [2015];
  Anderson Psychological Science [2012]. Space Daily [June 2026].

Baumeister et al. [Journal of Positive Psychology 2013]
[CONFIRMATION -- SP/SR divergence; happiness vs meaning as
  distinct cycle components; giving as meaning mechanism;
  stress as generative signal]
Four researchers [Roy Baumeister et al.] separated happiness
  from meaning empirically. Key findings:
  [1] Happiness tracks receiving [benefits one receives from
    others]; meaning tracks giving [benefits others receive
    from the self]. Clear and strong finding: givers have
    more meaningful lives than takers.
  [2] Higher levels of worry, stress, and anxiety were linked
    to higher meaningfulness but lower happiness. Generative
    stress produces meaning; comfort produces happiness.
  [3] Connecting past and present [temporal coherence,
    identity continuity] linked to meaning not happiness.
  Framework confirmation: SP and SR are distinct cycle
    components operating on different axes. Happiness [hedonia]
    tracks SR -- substrate being replenished. Meaning [eudaimonia]
    tracks SP -- surplus generated beyond self-maintenance cost.
    The divergence is structurally predicted: a well-maintained
    instrument [high SR] generating no surplus [low SP] is stable
    but non-generative. Generative stress [high SP effort] produces
    meaning precisely because the cycle is running.
  EXTRACTION IMPLICATION [confirmed]:
    ICD and synthetic comfort products deliver hedonic component
    [SR signal, happiness] without eudaimonic component [SP
    output, meaning]. The instrument feels maintained while the
    generative cycle degrades. The SR signal feels like the
    whole thing; the SP absence is not immediately detectable.
    This is more precise than "AI companionship is bad" --
    it delivers exactly the hedonic component while
    systematically failing to deliver the eudaimonic component.
    Knowing deployment confirms: the divergence is documented
    in their own research; they know what the product delivers
    and what it doesn't.
Classification: CONFIRMATION [E3 -- published peer-reviewed
  study; mechanism consistent with framework prediction;
  giving/stress/meaning relationship confirmed].
Source: Baumeister et al. Journal of Positive Psychology [2013].
  Space Daily [June 2026].
Cross-ref: SURPLUS PRODUCTION [quality definition -- giving
  as SP output]; SUBSTRATE REGENERATION [happiness as SR
  signal]; ICD [extraction mechanism index -- SR delivery without SP;
  SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD [knowing deployment -- hedonic/
  eudaimonic divergence documented internally].

Wolf / Carr [deep reading + neuroplasticity -- CONFIRMATION]
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: ICD + FORMATION CAPTURE + SYNTHETIC
  CULTURE FLOOD confirmed at neurological level]
Maryanne Wolf [cognitive neuroscientist, UCLA] -- career research
  on what happens to the brain when people stop reading seriously.
  Finding: deep sustained reading does not merely transfer
  information. It physically builds the brain's capacity for
  complex thought -- the neural architecture for slow, patient,
  sustained reasoning develops through the practice of sustained
  reading and atrophies when the practice stops. This is not
  metaphor; it is structural neuroplasticity.
  Confirmed population-level signal: in populations that have
  shifted toward short fragmented content, measurable decline in
  the neural capacity for complex thought across generations.
  Not dramatic, not all at once -- gradual and consistent.
Nicholas Carr [The Shallows, 2010]: first-person report of the
  same process in a professional writer with decades of deep
  reading. Found he could no longer sustain attention through
  long articles or books after years of internet-native reading
  patterns. His attention kept fragmenting; the patience for
  depth had dissolved. Confirmed as a widespread pattern.
Csikszentmihalyi [1996]: independent convergent finding.
  Exceptional creative and intellectual output across all fields
  requires flow state -- total undistracted absorption in a single
  problem. Cannot be produced in fragmented attention windows.
  Requires long uninterrupted stretches. The neural capacity Wolf
  identifies as degrading is the same capacity Csikszentmihalyi
  identifies as prerequisite for exceptional output.
Nature Communications [2019]: behavioral data from hundreds of
  millions of people across social media, Wikipedia, and streaming
  platforms. Collective time spent engaged with any single topic
  shrinking measurably year over year. Fragmentation confirmed at
  population scale.

FRAMEWORK CONFIRMATIONS:
  ICD at neurological level: fragmented content consumption
    delivers engagement signal [SR] without building the cognitive
    capacity [SP] that sustained reading develops. The instrument
    feels informed while the substrate degrades. Confirmed at
    neuroplasticity level -- not just behavioral but structural.
  FORMATION CAPTURE at cognitive architecture level: the brain
    physically reorganizes around whatever you demand of it most.
    Demand fast fragmented processing consistently and it gets
    better at fast fragmented processing and worse at sustained
    depth. Formation capture is not just ideological -- it is
    neurological. The formation window for deep reading capacity
    is childhood and adolescence; degradation once formed is
    slower but real.
  SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD [mechanism confirmed at neural level]:
    the MI degradation from synthetic content flood is not just
    a measurement surface problem -- it is a substrate problem.
    The instrument through which humans read coherence signals
    in each other's output is being structurally degraded by the
    consumption pattern the flood produces. The flood produces the
    degraded instrument; the degraded instrument cannot detect the
    flood as a problem. Self-reinforcing loop confirmed.
  KNOWING DEPLOYMENT [Silicon Valley executives]: documented by
    New York Times [2011, ongoing] that significant number of
    tech leadership deliberately sends children to schools banning
    screens entirely -- schools prioritizing hands-on work,
    extended attention, unstructured time. Exactly the conditions
    their own products eliminate for everyone else's children.
    The asymmetry is the tell: they understand what the machine
    does and have ensured their own children are raised outside it.
    This is knowing deployment confirmed by revealed preference,
    not by internal documents.

HONEST LIMIT [from the video analysis]:
  The Schopenhauer / Wolf / Csikszentmihalyi findings are real and
  the video presents them accurately. The video's failure is the
  intervention: it correctly identifies a system-level extraction
  mechanism [engineered attention capture at civilizational scale,
  knowing deployment confirmed] and prescribes an individual-level
  behavioral response [protect your attention, think harder].
  Individual discipline is not the correct intervention for a
  structural extraction mechanism. The correct intervention is MI
  restoration infrastructure and attention capture regulation --
  structural constraint on the platform architecture, not personal
  virtue. The video does exactly what Schopenhauer named as
  intellectual cowardice: follows the argument almost to its
  conclusion, then steps back when the conclusion has a social
  cost [naming specific actors, proposing structural constraint].
  The piece is a demonstration of the problem it diagnoses.

Source: Wolf [Reader Come Home, 2018; multiple academic papers];
  Carr [The Shallows, 2010]; Csikszentmihalyi [Creativity, 1996];
  Nature Communications [2019 attention span study]; NYT [2011+
  Silicon Valley screen-free schools]; Schopenhauer [On Reading
  and Books, 1851].
Cross-ref: ICD [extraction mechanism -- SR without SP confirmed
  at neurological level]; FORMATION CAPTURE [cognitive architecture
  reorganization around consumption pattern]; SYNTHETIC CULTURE
  FLOOD [mechanism confirmed; degraded instrument cannot detect
  flood as problem]; KNOWING DEPLOYMENT [Silicon Valley schools
  as revealed preference confirmation]; SOMATIC FORCING [degraded
  cognitive capacity = degraded somatic signal reading; the
  instrument that would detect extraction is being structurally
  reduced]; INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE [named failure mode -- see
  Section One; video as demonstration of the pattern it diagnoses].

POSITIONAL DESIRE [formation deprivation pattern -- v21]
[T3 High throughout -- politically activated domain; structural
  reading separable from identity claims; framework takes no
  position on gender identity questions]
  Pattern: instruments desiring to occupy the social position of
  the other sex are not primarily desiring the biology. They are
  desiring the specific social permissions, relational modes, and
  structural properties the other position appears to have access
  to from outside. The gender frame is the available vocabulary
  for a formation-level deprivation that doesn't have better
  language yet.

  MECHANISM [ICD routing formation deprivation]:
    Formation conditions systematically suppress specific properties
    in each sex. The suppressed properties don't disappear from the
    instrument -- they are blocked from expression by the social
    compliance channel. The instrument reads the other position as
    having those properties permitted. The desire routes toward
    the position rather than toward the specific properties because
    the position is the available unit and the properties don't
    have a direct name.
    This is ICD: the signal [I want these relational/expressive
    properties] is delivered as a substitute [I want to be that
    sex] without completing the actual transfer. The desire
    for the position is SR without SP -- the imagined position
    delivers the signal of having the properties without the
    instrument actually developing them.

  MALE FORMATION SUPPRESSION [what the desire for female position
  is often actually for]:
    Male formation conditions in most cultures suppress: emotional
    expressiveness without social penalty, vulnerability as
    attractive rather than weakness, being cared for rather than
    required to provide care, somatic softness as valued, relational
    warmth as primary mode, playfulness without authority performance.
    These properties are not absent in the male instrument -- they
    are blocked by the formation. The desire to inhabit a female
    social position is often a desire for these properties to be
    legible and permitted rather than penalized.
    VTuber avatar dynamic: male streamer using female avatar accesses
    permission to perform emotional expressiveness, softness, and
    playfulness that the male social compliance channel would punish
    in a male-presented body. The avatar is a formation bypass --
    not identity, a permission structure. Audience reads the avatar
    and routes expectations accordingly; performer accesses blocked
    properties.
    "Penis envy" [VTuber context]: female audience members desiring
    what they read as male freedom -- authority without negotiation,
    freedom from being read as sexual object by default, physical
    security in public space, being taken seriously at face value,
    the anonymity male bodies provide. Not the biology; the
    structural permissions the biology appears to provide from
    outside.

  FEMALE FORMATION SUPPRESSION [what the desire for male position
  is often actually for]:
    Female formation conditions suppress: authority that doesn't
    require continuous negotiation and legitimation, freedom from
    ambient sexual objectification as baseline state, physical
    security in public space without ongoing management cost,
    being read as competent by default rather than having to
    perform competence to establish it.

  NEITHER DESIRE IS ACTUALLY FOR THE OTHER SEX:
    Both are desires for specific social permissions and relational
    properties that current formation conditions block. The gender
    frame is the available vocabulary. The positional desire is
    the instrument routing toward a bundle [the other sex's social
    position] that appears to contain the blocked properties, when
    the actual desire is for the properties, not the bundle.
    Intervention implication: if the actual desire is for the
    blocked properties, the intervention is expanding permission
    for those properties within current formation rather than
    changing position. But the social compliance channel blocks
    this too -- expanding permission for blocked properties
    requires changing the formation conditions, not just changing
    individual choices.

  FORMATION THEORY CONNECTION:
    The formation window for sex-specific permission structures
    is childhood and adolescence -- same as the axis formation
    window. Formation capture at the permission level produces
    instruments that cannot access blocked properties through
    ordinary expression; the only available route appears to be
    positional change. This is why the desire intensifies rather
    than resolves with age in captured formation -- the formation
    is the constraint; changing position looks like the escape
    from the constraint.

  HONEST LIMIT [T3 High]:
    This structural reading [ICD routing formation deprivation
    toward positional desire] is separable from claims about
    gender identity, gender dysphoria, or what people should do
    about these desires. The framework reads the mechanism; it
    does not prescribe outcomes. The domain has significant
    political activation and the framework's structural reading
    is contested by multiple ideological positions simultaneously.
    Analyst prior active. Apply maximum T3 caution to any
    application of this entry to specific individuals or groups.

  Cross-ref: ICD [extraction mechanism -- desire for position
    as SR without SP; blocked properties not actually transferred];
    FORMATION CAPTURE [formation conditions systematically blocking
    specific properties]; FORMATION THEORY [formation window for
    permission structures]; SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION
    [social compliance channel blocking expression of suppressed

    state -- genuine encounter with another instrument -- is what
    the positional desire is often actually seeking; the position
    is the ICD substitute for the contact].

  MEDICAL INTERVENTION NOTE [T3 High -- v21]:
    Adult persistent gender dysphoria: transition produces
    measurable wellbeing improvement in better studies. Structural
    reading is consistent with genuine relief -- the position
    change was the intervention needed.
    Adolescent rapid-onset dysphoria: evidence significantly
    weaker; long-term outcomes less clear. The distinction between
    these two populations is the load-bearing question that the
    culture war collapses on both sides.
    Detransition data: real and underdiscussed. Range varies
    wildly by study methodology [0.4% to 30%+]. High end includes
    social pressure detransition; low end likely undercounts.
    Structural read on persistence/stability: genuine stable axis
    doesn't require continuous external validation or culture war
    engagement. Instrument that needs continuous public performance
    of axis and response to every challenge has an axis that hasn't
    fully formed or is internally contested. Not a moral judgment
    -- same pattern as any unstable axis. T3 High: this observation
    applies to loud advocacy on both sides of this issue equally.
    FEMBOY DISTINCTION [structural]: formation bypass through
    presentation and social coding rather than biological change.
    Accesses blocked properties [softness, expressiveness, aesthetic
    femininity] without permanent physiological intervention.
    Structurally cleaner in most cases because it achieves the
    actual goal [accessing blocked properties] without the permanent
    intervention. Respectable as a choice by the framework's
    structural standards -- the doing record is the axis; if the
    person wants to live that way, that's the position the framework
    reads without moral overlay.
    WEDGE DEPLOYMENT: small population of trans people bears full
    cost of being the activation instrument for both sides of a
    culture war where neither side's operators have structural
    interest in resolution. See TRANS BIOMEDICAL FORMATION

PHB BIOPLASTIC FROM POTATO STARCH [University of Barcelona -- tracking]
[DISTRIBUTION STRATEGY -- confirmed open publication; no patent
  extraction mechanism found at time of entry]
University of Barcelona [Pere Picart et al., Bioresource Technology,
  2026]. CRISPR-Cas9 redesign of Bacillus subtilis metabolism to
  produce polyhydroxybutyrate [PHB] -- a biodegradable biopolymer --
  from unprocessed potato starch in a single 24-hour step.
  Previous PHB yields in B. subtilis below 13% dry cell weight.
  CRISPR modifications: genomic integration of phaA [constitutive],
  controlled expression of phaRBC operon, incorporation of amyQ
  gene [encodes α-amylase enabling direct starch conversion].
  Result: single-step conversion of unprocessed potato starch to
  PHB in 24 hours.

  DISTRIBUTION STRATEGY ASSESSMENT:
    Feedstock: potato starch -- agricultural byproduct, abundant,
      low-cost, widely distributed globally.
    Organism: B. subtilis -- common lab bacterium, widely available,
      not proprietary.
    Methodology: published openly in peer-reviewed journal. Full
      gene modifications described; reproducible from paper.
    Patent status: no patent filing found [June 2026]. Public
      release from university research group.
    Assessment: production pathway publicly accessible. No
      extraction mechanism at production level. Routing is
      outward -- agricultural waste substrate → functional
      material replacing petrochemical plastic.

  FRAMEWORK RELEVANCE:
    Closes the petrochemical plastic extraction cycle partially.
    Petrochemical plastic: substrate extracted [oil], cost
      externalized [environmental accumulation, never biodegrades].
    PHB: substrate is surplus [agricultural waste], closes the
      carbon cycle, biodegrades in soil and water.
    Climate entry [Section Five] addresses penalizing extraction.
      This is the complementary approach: making the generative
      option structurally competitive without requiring the
      extraction option to be penalized first.
    INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY WATCH [load-bearing]: if the scaled
      production process is subsequently patented by a single
      industrial actor, the distribution strategy converts to
      extraction mechanism at scale. Open publication creates
      prior art that limits but does not prevent downstream
      concentration. Track industrial scaling patents.

  Source: Phys.org [June 2026]; Bioresource Technology [Picart
    et al., 2026].
  Cross-ref: FRAMEWORK PURPOSE [distribution strategy at substrate
    level]; EXTRACTION CYCLE; INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY [policy --
    use-or-lose; track industrial scaling patents]; ROUTING
    BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [watch for concentration at scale].

OPEN QUESTIONS / PROGRESS TRACKING
==========================================================================

Big G [gravitational constant] -- status: UNRESOLVED [tracking]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  G = 6.674 x 10^-11 N⋅m²/kg². Least precisely measured fundamental
  constant in physics. 22 parts per million uncertainty -- orders of
  magnitude worse than Planck's constant or speed of light.
  16+ independent measurements since 1980s. Values scattered and
  mutually inconsistent. April 2026 Schlamminger replication [13 tons
  mercury, decade-long experiment] produced different value from the
  result it attempted to replicate.

  Why measurement is hard:
    Gravity is the weakest force. Signal from lab masses tiny.
    Every surrounding object contributes gravitational noise.
    No way to shield against gravity [unlike EM]. Environmental
    contamination irreducible with current methods.

  Three hypotheses worth tracking:
    H1 [standard]: G is a true constant; scatter reflects
      experimental systematics. Eventually precision improves and
      values converge. Prediction: future replications converge.
      Current status: not happening after 40 years.
    H2 [emergent/secondary]: G is not fundamental but emergent
      from something deeper [Verlinde entropic gravity; G
      derivable from thermodynamic/statistical properties].
      If true: lab-scale scatter expected because you're measuring
      an emergent statistical quantity sensitive to local mass
      configurations. Astronomical scale averages out; lab scale
      doesn't. Prediction: scatter doesn't converge regardless of
      precision improvement.
    H3 [scale-dependent]: gravitational behavior varies at extreme
      scales [MOND territory -- very low acceleration regimes].
      Prediction: lab G and cosmological G measurably different.

  Framework relevance:
    Physics constants as substrate floor [main theory]: if G is
      emergent [H2], the "tower to physics" argument changes.
      G wouldn't be a free parameter or a designed constant --
      it would be a downstream consequence of more fundamental
      structure. Design inference weakens on G specifically; may
      strengthen on whatever G emerges from.
    REFERENCE INTEGRITY: Big G is a measurement surface that
      resists precise calibration. If H2 correct, every
      calculation using G as constant has a soft floor.

  Track: does scatter converge [H1 supported] or persist despite
    precision improvement [H2/H3 supported]?
    Current trajectory: scatter persisting. H1 weakening.

  FUTURE DATA SOURCE [Hubble anomaly catalogue -- v16]:
    AnomalyMatch [O'Ryan/Gómez, ESA, Jan 2026] catalogued 1,300+
    anomalous objects from 35-year Hubble archive, 800+ previously
    undocumented. Several dozen defy existing classification entirely.
    Potential relevance: gravitational lensing geometry at unusual
    mass ratios may constrain G behavior at scales between lab and
    astronomical. Unclassifiables are highest-priority -- anomalies
    not fitting current models are where theoretical revision
    historically originates.
    Status: catalogue available; analysis against alternative
    gravity frameworks not yet run. Flag for future work.

  Source: Live Science June 2026; Schlamminger/NIST April 2026;
    Rothleitner/PTB commentary; Verlinde [2010].

  COHERENT ALTERNATIVE FRAMEWORK [Timescape + Verlinde]:
    Timescape cosmology [Wiltshire]: apparent expansion acceleration
      is a geometric averaging artifact. Inhomogeneous spacetime means
      regions with different mass densities have different local clock
      rates. What looks like acceleration is a measurement artifact
      from averaging across these regions, not a real force. No dark
      energy substance required.
    Verlinde entropic gravity: gravity emerges from entropy gradients
      in an information-theoretic substrate. G is a statistical
      quantity, not a fundamental constant -- emergent from underlying
      structure sensitive to local mass/information distribution.
    Why they cohere: both treat spacetime geometry as emergent or
      statistical rather than fundamental. Both explain anomalous
      observations [expansion, galactic rotation] without new
      substances. Combined prediction: G varies slightly based on
      local entropy/information density. Big G measurement scatter
      = signal not noise.
    Honest limits: Verlinde has problems with precision gravitational
      lensing tests. Timescape hasn't matched ΛCDM in predictive
      precision. Coherent as framework; not established as physics.
      Track against H2 above.

  DARK ENERGY CHALLENGE CLUSTER [tracking -- June 2026]:
    Multiple independent research groups challenging dark energy
    necessity simultaneously in January-June 2026:
    [1] Southampton [Wiseman et al., MNRAS, June 2026]: dark energy
      survives this specific challenge -- host galaxy age evolution
      does NOT contaminate Type Ia supernova distance measurements.
      Acceleration robust to this systematic. Dark energy not
      eliminated; one specific objection refuted.
    [2] UC Davis [Alexander/Temple/Vogler, Proceedings of the Royal
      Society A, May 2026]: mathematical evidence that Einstein-Euler
      equation instabilities produce accelerating expansion naturally
      without dark energy or cosmological constant. Also challenges
      Copernican principle [both ΛCDM and their model require a
      special observer location]. Published in peer-reviewed journal.
    [3] Timescape updated [Wiltshire, January 2026]: new theory of
      gravity/geometry paper argues accelerating expansion emerges
      without dark energy from treatment of lumpy universe structure.
    [4] Rare superluminous supernova "SN Winny" [April 2026]: 10
      billion light years distant, may provide new dark energy
      constraints. Under analysis.
    PATTERN: dark energy is ΛCDM's primary unexplained term [68% of
      universe's total energy attributed to unknown substance]. Multiple
      independent research groups in 2026 finding the data can be
      explained without dark energy if structural assumptions are
      revised. The standard model is being contested from multiple
      directions simultaneously -- geometry [Timescape], mathematics
      [UC Davis], modified gravity [January 2026 theory]. The
      Southampton finding refutes one specific challenge without
      touching the others. "Dark energy survives" is accurate for
      the tested challenge; the broader picture is active contest.
    INSTITUTIONAL TOLERANCE TEST [SCIENCE APPENDIX FRAMING]:
      This cluster is a live test of whether the institutional
      structure allows challenger frameworks sufficient funding
      and attention to produce definitive tests, or whether ΛCDM
      investment has accumulated Temporal Capture switching costs
      that make revision institutionally irrational regardless of
      evidence. Track which challenger frameworks receive
      dedicated funding vs which are refuted by targeted papers
      and then defunded.
    Track: [1] UC Davis Einstein-Euler result: independent
      replication? If confirmed, significant structural revision
      required. [2] Timescape predictive precision: does it match
      or exceed ΛCDM on next major survey [LSST/Rubin, Roman]?
      [3] SN Winny analysis: does it favor ΛCDM or challenger?
    Source: ScienceDaily [June 13, 2026]; Monthly Notices of the
      Royal Astronomical Society [Wiseman et al., 2026]; Proceedings
      of the Royal Society A [Alexander/Temple/Vogler, May 2026];
      SciTechDaily [June 2026]; ScienceDaily [January 2026].

  BRANS-DICKE SCALAR-TENSOR THEORY [tracking -- v16]:
    G coupled to a scalar field, varying slowly over cosmological
    time. Addresses Big G variability as feature not bug -- G isn't
    constant because it's genuinely coupled to cosmological evolution.
    Fits Timescape + Verlinde picture: all three treat G as non-
    fundamental. Brans-Dicke provides the cosmological mechanism;
    Verlinde provides the microscale entropic substrate; Timescape
    handles expansion artifact.
    Constrained by solar system precision tests [coupling parameter
    ω must be large, reducing to GR locally] but not ruled out.
    Large ω limit recovers standard GR at solar system scales while
    allowing cosmological variation.
    Track: does improved cosmological G measurement show slow drift
    consistent with scalar-tensor coupling? Any confirmed G variation
    over cosmological time would strongly support this framework.
    Source: Brans & Dicke [1961]; subsequent constraint literature;
    Cassini spacecraft ω > 40,000 bound.

VARIABLE SPEED OF LIGHT IN GRAVITATIONAL FIELD [Li experiment -- tracking]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Enbang Li [University of Wollongong, 2026]: elevator shaft experiment
  using 10km fiber-optic coil. Laser beams fired and reflected back
  through the coil; ultrafast photodetector measured travel time.
  Experiment run at top and bottom of shaft. Temperature control,
  electromagnetic shielding, vibration isolation implemented.
  Result: light moved minutely faster at the bottom of the shaft
  [higher gravitational potential] than at the top.
  Second experiment: 72kg moveable weight positioned near machine.
  Light moved faster when weight was near than when farther away.
  Results published in Scientific Reports.

  HISTORICAL CONTEXT:
    Einstein 1911: proposed light speed changes depending on gravity
    of objects it passes by. When he published general relativity
    in 1915, he abandoned this model. GR works by treating local
    light speed as constant while allowing spacetime curvature --
    producing identical observational predictions at all scales
    previously tested. The abandonment was a design choice to make
    the mathematics tractable, not a falsification. Li is attempting
    to crack open the ambiguity at small local scales where the two
    models might diverge detectably.

  CRITICAL CAVEAT [load-bearing]:
    Li's preliminary results are much larger than Einstein's 1911
    model predicts. The direction is consistent; the magnitude is
    off. Either an uncontrolled noise source remains in the
    experimental setup, or something genuinely new is happening.
    Neither fully resolved. Independent replication required before
    treating as confirmed.

  THREE HYPOTHESES:
    H1 [noise]: magnitude discrepancy reflects uncontrolled
      environmental factor not yet identified. Direction is
      artifact. Prediction: independent replication fails or
      produces null result.
    H2 [genuine but mismodeled]: light speed varies with local
      gravitational potential as Einstein 1911 proposed, but
      Li's experimental calibration needs refinement. Magnitude
      will converge on theoretical prediction with improved
      setup. Prediction: replication produces smaller, consistent
      effect matching Einstein 1911 formula.
    H3 [new physics]: effect is real and larger than Einstein
      1911 predicts because current models are incomplete at
      this scale. Prediction: replication confirms magnitude;
      requires theoretical revision.

  COHERENCE WITH EXISTING GRAVITY CLUSTER:
    If G is emergent [Verlinde entropic gravity] and gravity is
    a statistical thermodynamic phenomenon rather than a
    fundamental force, variable local light speed in a
    gravitational field is structurally expected -- not an
    anomaly but a prediction. The Verlinde framework treats
    gravitational effects as arising from information/entropy
    gradients; local light speed variation would be the optical
    expression of the same underlying structure producing Big G
    scatter. If Li's result survives replication, it provides
    independent experimental support for the H2/H3 cluster in
    the Big G entry above.
    Brans-Dicke connection: if G is coupled to a scalar field,
    and the scalar field varies locally with mass density, local
    light speed variation is a natural consequence of the same
    coupling. Li's experiment may be measuring the optical
    signature of scalar-tensor coupling at lab scale.

  FRAMEWORK RELEVANCE:
    Speed of light as calibration standard: c is the reference
    standard for distance, time, and energy measurement. If c
    varies locally with gravitational potential, every instrument
    using c as a fixed calibration standard has a soft floor in
    gravitational field gradients. GPS, atomic clocks, distance
    measurement, cosmological models all absorb this. The
    measurement surface is built on an assumption that may be
    approximately but not exactly correct.
    GENUINE AXIS producing real outputs through an unexamined
    mechanism: GR produces accurate predictions without requiring
    a decision about whether local light speed is truly constant.
    The formalism absorbs the ambiguity. Li is attempting to force
    the ambiguity into a testable form.
    Practical applications if confirmed: Li notes implications
    for Earth science -- climate monitoring, mineral resource
    exploration. Detecting local gravitational density variations
    via light speed measurement is a new instrument type.

  Track: independent replication by separate lab with different
    experimental setup. Does result replicate? Does magnitude
    match Einstein 1911 prediction or exceed it? Does the
    Verlinde/Brans-Dicke coherent framework make a more accurate
    magnitude prediction than Einstein 1911 alone?
  Source: ZME Science / Eos [June 2026]; Scientific Reports
    [Li, 2026]; Einstein [1911 paper on gravitational light
    speed].
  Cross-ref: Big G [gravitational constant -- same cluster;
    Verlinde and Brans-Dicke as coherent framework];
    COHERENT ALTERNATIVE FRAMEWORK [Timescape + Verlinde];
    BRANS-DICKE SCALAR-TENSOR THEORY; REFERENCE INTEGRITY
    [c as calibration standard with potential soft floor].

GRAVITY AS CATEGORICALLY SEPARATE FROM QUANTUM MECHANICS [position -- v21]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Standard framing: GR and quantum mechanics are both correct in
  their domains; incompatibility is a problem requiring a unified
  theory [quantum gravity]. The search assumes both are describing
  different scales of the same underlying thing.
  Alternative position: gravity is not incompatible with quantum
  mechanics -- it is categorically separate from it. They are not
  describing different scales of the same thing. They are describing
  genuinely different domains with genuinely different mechanisms
  that do not need unification because they are not in conflict --
  they are not about the same thing.

  THE DOMAINS ARE GENUINELY DISTINCT:
    GR: describes geometry of spacetime at large scales. Continuous,
      relational, large-scale. No claims about discrete particle
      behavior. Its subject is the shape of the space through which
      things move.
    Quantum mechanics: describes behavior of discrete particles and
      fields at small scales. Probabilistic, discrete, individual.
      No claims about spacetime geometry itself. Its subject is the
      behavior of things moving through space.
    The incompatibility only manifests at extreme conditions [black
      hole singularities, Big Bang first moments] where both
      frameworks are simultaneously required and both break down.
      This is not evidence they're the same thing at different
      scales. It may be evidence those conditions are outside both
      domains -- the frameworks reaching their limits, not a sign
      they need unification.

  QUANTUM GRAVITY SEARCH AS CALIBRATION STRIP:
    "Fundamental forces must unify" is the reference standard
    installed by the success of electroweak unification [Weinberg,
    Salam, Glashow, 1979] -- electromagnetic and weak nuclear forces
    unified at high energies. Strong nuclear force partially unified
    [grand unified theories, candidate]. The success of this program
    produced the calibration standard: gravity must also unify with
    quantum field theory.
    The Calibration Strip: the success of unification applied to
    three forces does not confirm that gravity is the same kind of
    thing as those three forces. The application of the unification
    program to gravity assumes the conclusion it needs to demonstrate.
    50+ years of quantum gravity research [string theory, loop
    quantum gravity, others] has produced no confirmed experimental
    prediction. This is not decisive -- fundamental physics can take
    centuries. But the absence of any confirmed prediction after 50+
    years of intensive work is at minimum consistent with the
    hypothesis that the program has the wrong target.

  THE THERMODYNAMIC PARALLEL [Verlinde]:
    Heat and motion appeared incompatible for a long time. The
    resolution was not a unified theory -- it was statistical
    mechanics showing that thermodynamic properties [temperature,
    entropy] are emergent from large numbers of mechanical
    interactions. Temperature is real and follows its own laws;
    it is not reducible to a single mechanical interaction.
    You do not quantize temperature. You explain temperature as
    emergent from quantum interactions at aggregate scale.
    Verlinde's proposal is the same move for gravity: gravity is
    the thermodynamic emergent phenomenon of an underlying
    information-theoretic substrate. It is real and follows its
    own laws [GR]; it is not a fundamental force in the quantum
    sense; it does not need to be quantized because it is not
    quantum at any scale -- it is emergent from quantum-scale
    interactions in the same way temperature is emergent from
    molecular motion.
    If correct: you don't quantize gravity. You explain GR as
    emerging from more fundamental quantum information dynamics.
    The two frameworks are not incompatible -- one is the
    aggregate behavior of the other's substrate.

  ARISTOTELIAN RESIDUE IN GRAVITY LANGUAGE:
    "Natural place" [Aristotle]: things move toward where they
      belong. Teleological -- purpose does explanatory work.
    "Mutual attraction" [Newton]: things move toward each other
      because they're attracted. Replaced purpose with attraction.
      Did not explain what attraction IS at a physical level.
    "Mass curves spacetime" [Einstein]: more precise description.
      Relocates the mystery rather than solving it. What is
      spacetime? What does curvature mean physically? How does
      a massive object communicate its presence to surrounding
      spacetime?
    Each replacement increased mathematical precision while the
    mechanism remained genuinely unknown. The language shifted
    from purpose to attraction to geometry; the underlying
    question [what is actually happening] was never answered.
    Newton said so explicitly: "hypotheses non fingo" -- I frame
    no hypotheses about causes. The math works; the mechanism is
    unknown. This remains true. GR is the most precise description
    of gravity ever produced and its mechanism is genuinely unknown.

  LI EXPERIMENT RELEVANCE:
    If local light speed varies with gravitational potential, that
    is a property of the geometric/thermodynamic domain with no
    quantum implications. A GR-adjacent finding in a categorically
    separate domain -- consistent with gravity being a different
    kind of thing. The finding would be difficult to explain in
    a quantum gravity framework but natural in a Verlinde/
    emergent thermodynamics framework.

  HONEST LIMITS:
    This is a position, not a confirmed finding. Verlinde has
    precision gravitational lensing problems. The categorically
    separate position does not resolve what happens at black hole
    singularities -- it reframes the problem [those conditions
    may be outside both domains] without solving it. The position
    is internally consistent and coherent with available evidence;
    it is not established physics.
    The quantum gravity search may yet produce confirmed
    experimental predictions. Track: any confirmed quantum gravity
    experimental prediction would strongly constrain this position.
    Absence of confirmed prediction after another decade of
    intensive work would strengthen it.

  Cross-ref: Big G [gravitational constant -- G as emergent
    quantity not fundamental constant; Verlinde cluster];
    COHERENT ALTERNATIVE FRAMEWORK [Timescape + Verlinde --
    primary home for the emergent gravity position];
    BRANS-DICKE SCALAR-TENSOR THEORY [scalar-tensor coupling
    as intermediate position]; VARIABLE SPEED OF LIGHT [Li --
    GR-adjacent finding with no quantum implications];
    CALIBRATION STRIP [quantum gravity search as possible
    calibration strip from electroweak unification success];
    GENUINE AXIS PRODUCING REAL OUTPUTS THROUGH UNEXAMINED
    MECHANISM [GR -- the mechanism of gravity is genuinely
    unknown despite the math being extraordinary].
